## מ (id: BDB04972)

מ,ם, *Mēm*, thirteenth letter; used as numeral 40 in postB. Heb.

> \[Str4100,4480] •מַ v. מָה. •מִ v. מִן. p. 552, 577

> \[Str3965,4124] מֹאָב v. מוֺאָב. \[מַאֲבוּס] v. אבס, 555

* **Associated Articles:** מָה (ID: `BDB05065@BDBHebrewLexicon`); אָבַס (ID: `BDB00076@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## מאד (id: BDB04975)

מאד (√ of following As. *ma’âdu, be many, increase; ma’adu, many; ma’adiš, in abundance,* DlHWB, 388 f.; cf. Sab. מאד *to add*, DHMZMG 1883, 342 f. HomSüb\-Ar. Chr. 127; Ar. مَأَدَ *begin to grow,* of plant).

> \[Str3966] מְאֹד **n.m.** muchness, force, abundance, exceedingly (cf. As. *mu’du, abundance*, HomZMG 1878, 711 (‘treasures *ana mu’di, in abundance’*) DlHWB 399\):—**1\.** *force, might,* †[Dt 6:5](https://ref.ly/Deut6:5) וּבְכָל־מְאֹדְֶָ and with all thy *might;* hence [2 K 23:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:25). **2\.** in diff. idioms (298 t.) to express the idea of *exceedingly, greatly, very* (whether of magnitude or degree): **a.** (so mostly) as adv. acc., qualifying both adjj. and obs., [Gn 1:31](https://ref.ly/Gen1:31) טוֺב מְאֹד good *exceedingly*, [4:5](https://ref.ly/Gen4:5) וַיִּחַד לְקַיִן מְאֹד and Cain was wroth *exceedingly*, [7:18](https://ref.ly/Gen7:18) מְאֹד וַיִּרְבּוּ הַמַּיִם וַיִּגְבְּרוּ, [12:14](https://ref.ly/Gen12:14), [13:2](https://ref.ly/Gen13:2) בַּמִּקְנֶה מְאֹד כָּבֵד וְאַבְרָם, v:[13](https://ref.ly/Gen13:13) מאד ´חַטָּאִים לי, [18:20](https://ref.ly/Gen18:20), [19:3](https://ref.ly/Gen19:3), [19:9](https://ref.ly/Gen19:9), [21:11](https://ref.ly/Gen21:11), [24:16](https://ref.ly/Gen24:16), [24:35](https://ref.ly/Gen24:35), \+ oft., [Psalm 46:2](https://ref.ly/Ps46:2) נִמְצָא מְאֹד עֶזְרָה בְצָרוֺת as a help in troubles, to be found (\= present) *exceedingly;* with an inf. [Is 47:9](https://ref.ly/Isa47:9) בְּעָצְמַת חֲבָרַיִח מְאֹד despite of thy spells being *very* numerous, [Jos 9:13](https://ref.ly/Josh9:13) מֵרֹב הַדֶּרֶח מְאֹד, [1 K 7:47](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:47); הַרְבֵּה מְאֹד [Gn 15:1](https://ref.ly/Gen15:1), [41:49](https://ref.ly/Gen41:49), [Dt 3:5](https://ref.ly/Deut3:5) \+, for which in the later language is found לָרֹב מְאֹד †[Zc 14:14](https://ref.ly/Zech14:14), [2 Ch 4:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:18), [9:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:9) (in [1 K 10:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:10) הַרְבֵּה מְאֹד), [24:24](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:24), [30:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:13), and even לְהַרְבֵּה מְאֹד †[2 Ch 11:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr11:12), [16:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:8). מְאֹד is not usually separated from the word it qualifies by more than one or two words (as [Gn 20:8](https://ref.ly/Gen20:8) האנשׁים מאד וייראו, [Nu 11:10](https://ref.ly/Num11:10) מאד ´ויחר אף י); see, however, [Dt 30:14](https://ref.ly/Deut30:14), Ju [12:2](https://ref.ly/Judg12:2), [2 S 12:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:5), [1 K 11:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:19), [Psalm 31:12](https://ref.ly/Ps31:12). It precedes its verb, † [Psalm 47:10](https://ref.ly/Ps47:10) מְאֹד נַעֲלָה (hence [97:9](https://ref.ly/Ps97:9)), [92:6](https://ref.ly/Ps92:6). **b.** עַד־מְאֹד *up to abundance, to a great degree, exceedingly*, †[Gn 27:33](https://ref.ly/Gen27:33) חֲדָדָה גְּדֹלָה ´עַד־מ, v:[34](https://ref.ly/Gen27:34), [1 S 11:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:15) ´עד מ …וישמח, [25:36](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:36) וְהוּא ´שִׁכֹּר עַד־מ, [2 S 2:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:17), [1 K 1:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:4), [Is 64:8](https://ref.ly/Isa64:8) (cf. [La 5:22](https://ref.ly/Lam5:22)), v:[11](https://ref.ly/Isa64:11) [Psalm 38:7](https://ref.ly/Ps38:7), [38:9](https://ref.ly/Ps38:9), [119:8](https://ref.ly/Ps119:8), [119:43](https://ref.ly/Ps119:43), [119:51](https://ref.ly/Ps119:51), [119:107](https://ref.ly/Ps119:107), [Dn 8:8](https://ref.ly/Dan8:8), [11:25](https://ref.ly/Dan11:25). **c.** עַד־לִמְאֹד (v. עַד לְ)† [2 Ch 16:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:14). **d.** duplicated (Ges§ 113 k), † [Gn 7:19](https://ref.ly/Gen7:19) ´מ ´וְהַמַּיִם גָּֽבְרוּ מ, [30:43](https://ref.ly/Gen30:43), [Nu 14:7](https://ref.ly/Num14:7), [1 K 7:47](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:47), [2 K 10:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:4), Ez [37:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek37:10). **e.** בִּמְאֹד מְאֹד lit. *with muchness, muchness*, †[Gn 17:2](https://ref.ly/Gen17:2), [17:6](https://ref.ly/Gen17:6), [17:20](https://ref.ly/Gen17:20), [Ex 1:7](https://ref.ly/Exod1:7) (all P), Ez [9:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek9:9), [16:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:13).

* **Associated Passages:** Genesis 20:8; 2 Chronicles 4:18; Ezekiel 37:10; Lamentations 5:22; Deuteronomy 3:5; Genesis 7:18; Genesis 19:3; Zechariah 14:14; Psalms 38:9; Psalms 46:2; 1 Samuel 25:36; Daniel 8:8; Genesis 41:49; 1 Kings 10:10; 1 Kings 1:4; Genesis 27:34; Psalms 38:7; Psalms 47:10; Ezekiel 9:9; Psalms 97:9; 2 Kings 10:4; 2 Samuel 2:17; Genesis 17:20; Numbers 14:7; Psalms 92:6; Psalms 31:12; 2 Chronicles 16:14; 1 Kings 7:47; Deuteronomy 30:14; Genesis 1:31; Genesis 19:9; Daniel 11:25; Numbers 11:10; 1 Samuel 11:15; Genesis 4:5; Exodus 1:7; Judges 12:2; Genesis 13:2; Isaiah 64:8; Genesis 24:35; 1 Kings 11:19; 2 Chronicles 9:9; Ezekiel 16:13; 2 Kings 23:25; Genesis 17:2; Genesis 30:43; 2 Chronicles 16:8; Genesis 7:19; Genesis 24:16; Genesis 12:14; Genesis 27:33; Genesis 17:6; Genesis 15:1; 2 Chronicles 11:12; Genesis 21:11; 2 Chronicles 30:13; Psalms 119:43; 2 Chronicles 24:24; Psalms 119:51; 2 Samuel 12:5; Genesis 18:20; Joshua 9:13; Psalms 119:8; Isaiah 47:9; Genesis 13:13; Psalms 119:107; Isaiah 64:11; Deuteronomy 6:5


## I מֵאָה (id: BDB04977)

\[Str3967] I. מֵאָה:583 **n.f.** hundred (NH *id.;* MI20 מאתן (du.); SI5 מאת, מאתים (du.); Ph. מאת מאתם (du.); As. *mê* (prob., Dl:§ 75\), TelAm. *mê\-at* WklTelAm 20\*; Ar. مِييءَةٌ Sab. מאת מאתם OsZMG x. 1856, 49; CIS iv. i. 6, 4; 46, 6; מאה EutSin 457; Eth. ምእት፡; Aram. מְאָה, ܡܳܐܐ; Palm. תלת מאה 300 VogPalm 6, 9\); —´מ [Gn 6:3](https://ref.ly/Gen6:3) \+ 144 t,; cstr. מְאַת [5:3](https://ref.ly/Gen5:3) \+ 30 t. (on מְאַת [Ec 8:12](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:12) v. **2 d** infr.); pl. abs. מֵאוֺת [Gn 5:9](https://ref.ly/Gen5:9) \+ 324 t. (מֵאֹת only [5:4](https://ref.ly/Gen5:4), [5:30](https://ref.ly/Gen5:30)); המאיות Kt [2 K 11:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:4) \+ 3 t. 2 K 11; du. מָאתַיִם (־תָיִם) [Gn 11:19](https://ref.ly/Gen11:19) \+ 76 t. (but [1 S 18:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:27) rd. מֵאָה (מְאַת) LXX We Dr Kit Bu HPS);—*hundred:*—**1\.** as simple number : **a.** abs. sg., (I) foll. by sg. of noun enumerated: מֵאָה קְשִיטָה [Gn 33:19](https://ref.ly/Gen33:19), [Jos 24:32](https://ref.ly/Josh24:32) (both E); אִישׁ ´מ Ju [7:19](https://ref.ly/Judg7:19), [1 K 18:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:13), [2 K 4:43](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:43); אַמָּה ´מ [1 K 7:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:2), Ez [40:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:19) \+; כִּכָּר ´מ [2 K 23:33](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:33), [2 Ch 25:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:6), [27:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr27:5), [36:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr36:3), [Ezr 8:26](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:26); אֶלֶף ´מ \= 100,000 [1 K 20:29](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:29) \+ 6 t. K Ch; cf. also [Gn 17:17](https://ref.ly/Gen17:17) (P), [2 S 8:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:4), [Is 65:20](https://ref.ly/Isa65:20), [65:20](https://ref.ly/Isa65:20), and sq. n. coll. רֶכֶב [2 S 8:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:4) \= [1 Ch 18:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr18:4), צאֹן [1 K 5:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:3); (2\) less oft. sq. pl. מֵאָה שְׁעָרִים [Gn 26:12](https://ref.ly/Gen26:12) (J), פְּעָמִים ´מ [2 S 24:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:3) \= [1 Ch 21:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:3), נְבִיאִים ´מ [1 K 18:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:4), etc.; (3\) seldom, and late, preceded by n.pl.: הָרִמֹּנִים מֵאָה [Je 52:23](https://ref.ly/Jer52:23), אַמּוֺת הַמֵּאָה Ez [42:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek42:2), cf. [Ezr 2:69](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:69), [2 Ch 3:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr3:16), [4:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:8), [29:32](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:32); (4\) exceptional is מֵאָה לְכִּכָּרִים כְּלֵי כֶסֶף [Ezr 8:26](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:26); also, with ellipsis, מֵאָה כֶסֶף [Dt 22:19](https://ref.ly/Deut22:19) (i.e. shekels); מֵאָה קַיִץ [2 S 16:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:1) (i.e. cakes); (5\) מֵאָה without noun enumerated, esp. \= *a hundred* (men, persons) Ju [20:10](https://ref.ly/Judg20:10), [20:10](https://ref.ly/Judg20:10), [Am 5:3](https://ref.ly/Amos5:3), [5:3](https://ref.ly/Amos5:3), [Lv 26:8](https://ref.ly/Lev26:8), [26:8](https://ref.ly/Lev26:8) (H), 1 Ch 12:l4; ´מ [Pr 17:10](https://ref.ly/Prov17:10) \= 100 (blows), [Ec 6:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl6:3) \= 100 (children), [8:12](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:12) \= 100 (times).—On [Ne 3:1](https://ref.ly/Neh3:1), [12:39](https://ref.ly/Neh12:39) v. infr. **b.** abs. du., (I) foll, by sg., מָאתַיִם אִישׁ [1 S 18:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:27), [30:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:10), [2 S 15:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:11), לֶחֶם ´מ [1 S 25:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:18), [2 S 16:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:1), צִנָּה ´מ [1 K 10:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:16) \= [2 Ch 9:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:15), אֶלֶף ´מ [1 S 15:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:4) \+, שָׁנָה ´מ [Gn 11:23](https://ref.ly/Gen11:23) (P); (2\) less oft. sq. pl. שְׁקָלִים ´מ [Jos 7:21](https://ref.ly/Josh7:21) (JE), [2 S 14:26](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:26), דְּבֵלִים ´מ [1 S 25:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:18), אֲנָשִׁים ´מ [1 S 30:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:21), cf. [Ezr 8:4](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:4); (3\) also preceded by n.pl.: ´עִוִּים מ [Gn 32:15](https://ref.ly/Gen32:15), ´רְחֵלִים מ v:[15](https://ref.ly/Gen32:15) (E), ´הָרִמּוֺנִים מ [1 K 7:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:20), cf. [1 Ch 15:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:8), [2 Ch 29:32](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:32), [Ezr 2:65](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:65); (4\) c. ellipsis כֶּסֶף ´מ Ju [17:4](https://ref.ly/Judg17:4); (5\) as pred., ´רָאשֵׁיהֶם מ[1 Ch 12:32](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:32) (v:[33](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:33) van d. H). **c.** abs. pl. ת(וֺ)מֵא:—(1\) alone, \= *hundreds*, לְמֵאוֺת [1 S 29:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam29:2) *by hundreds*, so [2 S 18:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:4) (both \+ thousands); esp. ´מּ(הַ)שָרֵי *captains of (the) hundreds* [Ex 18:21](https://ref.ly/Exod18:21), [18:25](https://ref.ly/Exod18:25) (E), [2 K 11:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:4), [11:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:9), [11:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:10), [11:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:15) \+; (2\) multiplied, sq. noun enumerated, in sg.: אִישׁ ´שְׁלשׁ מ Ju [7:6](https://ref.ly/Judg7:6) 300 *men*, cf. v:[7](https://ref.ly/Judg7:7), v:[8](https://ref.ly/Judg7:8), v:[16](https://ref.ly/Judg7:16), [8:4](https://ref.ly/Judg8:4), [Ex 12:27](https://ref.ly/Exod12:27) (E), etc.; שָׁנָה ´אַרְבַּע מ [Gn 15:13](https://ref.ly/Gen15:13) (JE) 400 *years;* אֶלֶף ´חֲמֵשׁ מ [2 S 24:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:9) 500,000, cf. [2 Ch 13:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:17); רֶכֶב ´שֵׁשׁ מ [Ex 14:7](https://ref.ly/Exod14:7) (J) 600 *chariots;* אִישׁ שְׁבַע ´מ Ju [20:15](https://ref.ly/Judg20:15), [20:16](https://ref.ly/Judg20:16) 700 *men;* חָלָל ´שְׁמֹנֶה מ [2 S 23:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:8) 800 *slain;* רֶכֶב ´תְּשַׁע מ Ju [4:3](https://ref.ly/Judg4:3), [4:13](https://ref.ly/Judg4:13) 900 *chariots;* (3\) less oft. sq. n.pl.: שׁוּעָלִים ´שְׁלשׁ מ Ju [15:4](https://ref.ly/Judg15:4) 300 *foxes;* אֲתוֺנוֺת ´חֲמֵשׁ מ [Jb 1:3](https://ref.ly/Job1:3) 500 *she\-asses,* etc.; (4\) rare, and chiefly late, after n.pl. ´פִּלַגְשִׁים שְׁלשׁ מ [1 K 11:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:3) 300 *concubines*, cf. [7:42](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:42) \= [2 Ch 4:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:13), [1 Ch 4:42](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:42), [2 Ch 14:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr14:8) etc.; after n.coll. ´בָּקָר חֲמֵשׁ מ [2 Ch 35:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:9); (5\) rare is בַּשֶּׁקֶל ´חֲמֵשׁ מ [Ex 30:24](https://ref.ly/Exod30:24) (P); (6\) ellipsis of n., כֶּסֶף ´שְׁלשׁ מ [Gn 45:22](https://ref.ly/Gen45:22) (E), etc. **d.** cstr. sg., only sq. sg., and late: מְאַת־שָׁנָה [Gn 11:10](https://ref.ly/Gen11:10), [21:5](https://ref.ly/Gen21:5), [25:7](https://ref.ly/Gen25:7) (all P) \+ 25 t. P, [1 Ch 25:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:9), [Est 1:4](https://ref.ly/Esth1:4) (DrIntr.. 124\) (also c. other numerals, v. **2** infr.); מְאַ֭ת [Ec 8:12](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:12) \= 100 *times* (מֵאָה פְּעָמִים), sc. פַּעַם, or perhaps only appar. cstr. (Köii. 228 cf. Ew§ 269 b. 279 e).—[Ne 5:11](https://ref.ly/Neh5:11) v. **3** infr. **2\.** as part of larger number: **a.** usually preceding tens and units, (1\) with noun expressed once, or not at all, earlier usage, usu. c. וְ before ten if no unit follows, otherwise without וְ before ten (see Köii. 218 ff.): מֵאָה וְעֶשֶר שָרִים Ju [2:8](https://ref.ly/Judg2:8); שִׁשִּׁים וָשֵׁשׁ כִּכָּר שֵׁשׁ מֵאוֺת [1 K 10:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:14) 666 *talents*, etc. (exceptions are [Ne 7:24](https://ref.ly/Neh7:24) and v:[31](https://ref.ly/Neh7:31)); (2\) chiefly late, with noun and וְ repeated: שָׁנָה וְעֶשְרִים שָׁנָה מֵאָה שָׁנִים וְשֶׁבַע [Gn 23:1](https://ref.ly/Gen23:1) (P) (cf. ואלף אמה ומאת אמה SI5.6 DaExpos. Times. May, 1898\). **b.** less oft., foll. tens and units, chiefly late, c. וְ repeated:—עֶשְרִים וּמֵאָה [Nu 7:86](https://ref.ly/Num7:86) (P); שֶׁבַע וּשְׁלשִׁים וּמְאַת שָׁנָה [Ex 6:16](https://ref.ly/Exod6:16) (P), etc.; c. noun after ten and after hundred, חָמֵשׁ וְשִׁשִּׁים שָׁנָה וּשְׁלשׁ מֵאוֺת שָׁנָה [Gn 5:23](https://ref.ly/Gen5:23) (P), etc.— On these usages v., further, Köii. 217 ff. DaSynt. §§ 36 R. 3, 37 (d) Ges: §§ 97, 134 and esp. HernerSynt. d. Zahlwörter im AT (1893\). †**3\.**מְאַת הַכֶּסֶף [Ne 5:11](https://ref.ly/Neh5:11) \= *a hundredth* part *of the money;* but read quite poss. מַשַּׁאת *the debt* (GeiJüd. Zeitschr. viii. 1870, 227 Gr.)

(fr. √ \[מוֺד \=] מוֺט, but rd. then rather וַיְמֹטֵט, although מוט not elsewhere in this conj.; We, plausibly, וַיְמֹגֵג √ מוג, cf. [Na 1:5](https://ref.ly/Nah1:5)). † **Hithpo.** וַיִּתְמֹדֵד sq. עַל־הַיֶ֫לָד; [1 K 17:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:21) *measured* (\= extended, stretched) *himself* upon the boy.

## מָה (id: BDB05065)

\[Str4100,4078] מָה, rarely מָ־ה (e.g. [Gn 31:43](https://ref.ly/Gen31:43), [Jos 22:16](https://ref.ly/Josh22:16), Ju [8:1](https://ref.ly/Judg8:1)), מַ־ה, •מַה, מֶה, •מַ († [Ex 4:2](https://ref.ly/Exod4:2), [Is 3:15](https://ref.ly/Isa3:15), [Mal 1:13](https://ref.ly/Mal1:13), [1 Ch 15:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:13), [2 Ch 30:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:3)), מָ (only in מָהֵם † Ez [8:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:6) Kt., Qr. מָה הֵם)—on the distinction in the use of these forms, see Ges§ 37—**pron. interrog.** and **indef.** what ? how ? aught; used of things, as מִי of persons (Aram. ܡܳܐ, מָא, Ar. مَا; prob. apoc. from a longer form with *n* or *nt*, As. *minû* (DlHWB 417 f.), Eth. ምንት፡ *ment;* cf. WCG 123 ff. K?:ii. 368\):—**1\.** interrog. *what ?* **a.** in a direct question, before either verbs or nouns [Gn 4:10](https://ref.ly/Gen4:10) מֶה עשית *what* hast thou done ? [15:2](https://ref.ly/Gen15:2) מה־תתן־לי *what* wilt thou give me ? [Ex 3:13](https://ref.ly/Exod3:13) מַה־שְּׁמוֺ, [12:26](https://ref.ly/Exod12:26), [13:14](https://ref.ly/Exod13:14) and so very often: מַה־הִיא [Zc 5:6](https://ref.ly/Zech5:6); מה אלה [Zc 1:9](https://ref.ly/Zech1:9); מָה אַתָּה רֹאֶה [Je 1:11](https://ref.ly/Jer1:11), [Am 7:8](https://ref.ly/Amos7:8) \+ ; [Jl 4:14](https://ref.ly/Joel4:14) מה אתם לי; Ju [18:8](https://ref.ly/Judg18:8) מה אתם vid. Comm., v:[24](https://ref.ly/Judg18:24) מה לי עוד *what* have I still ? [Is 21:11](https://ref.ly/Isa21:11) מה־מלילה *what* (\= how much) of the night (is past) ? \= *as what ?* (*qualem ?*) [Hg 2:3](https://ref.ly/Hag2:3) (cf. מִי [Am 7:2](https://ref.ly/Amos7:2)); to express surprise, [Jb 9:12](https://ref.ly/Job9:12) who shall say to him, מה תעשה *what* doest thou ? [22:13](https://ref.ly/Job22:13), [Ec 8:4](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:4), [Is 45:9](https://ref.ly/Isa45:9), [45:10](https://ref.ly/Isa45:10); sq. כִּי, [Gn 20:10](https://ref.ly/Gen20:10) מה ראית כי עשית *what* hadst thou in view, that thou hast done, etc.? [31:36](https://ref.ly/Gen31:36), [Ex 16:7](https://ref.ly/Exod16:7) וְנַחְנוּ מָה כִּי and *what* are we, that…? [32:21](https://ref.ly/Exod32:21), [Nu 22:28](https://ref.ly/Num22:28), Hb [2:18](https://ref.ly/Hab2:18), etc. Note in partic.—(*a*) מָה is followed sometimes by a subst. in appos. (against Arabic usage, which does not permit this: WAG. ii. § 170\), so that it becomes virtually an adj.: מַה־בֶּצַע *what* profit…? [Gn 37:26](https://ref.ly/Gen37:26), [Psalm 30:10](https://ref.ly/Ps30:10), [Mal 3:14](https://ref.ly/Mal3:14); [Is 40:8](https://ref.ly/Isa40:8) מַה־דְּמוּת, [Mal 1:13](https://ref.ly/Mal1:13), [Psalm 89:48](https://ref.ly/Ps89:48) זְכָר־אֲנִי מֶה חָלֶד (inverted for חֶלֶד אָנִי מַה) remember (of) *what* (short) duration I am, [Ec 1:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:3), [5:10](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:10), [5:15](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:15), [6:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl6:8), [6:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl6:11) (Da:§ 8, R. 2 K?:iii. 23 f.); as exclam., [Psalm 89:48](https://ref.ly/Ps89:48) b, [Jb 26:14](https://ref.ly/Job26:14). And with the subst. idiom. at the end (in Arabic preceded then by مِنْ: WAG. ii. § 49\. 7\), [1 S 26:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:18) ומַה־בידי רעה and what is there in my hand, evil ? [20:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:10) (vid. **3**), [2 S 19:29](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:29), [24:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:13), [1 K 12:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:16), [Je 2:5](https://ref.ly/Jer2:5) מה־מצאו אבותיכם בי עָוֶל, [Ec 11:2](https://ref.ly/Eccl11:2), [Est 6:3](https://ref.ly/Esth6:3). (*b*) מַה־זֶּה *what now ?* [1 S 10:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:11), contr. מַזֶּה † [Ex 4:2](https://ref.ly/Exod4:2) (זֶה **4 c**); [Gn 3:13](https://ref.ly/Gen3:13) מַה־זּאֹת עָשִיתָ; [12:18](https://ref.ly/Gen12:18), [29:25](https://ref.ly/Gen29:25) מַה־זּאֹת עָשִיתָה לִּי; similarly [26:10](https://ref.ly/Gen26:10), [42:28](https://ref.ly/Gen42:28) \+ , either *what, now*, hast thou done ? or *what is this* that thou hast done ? (vid. זֶה **4 d**). (*c*) לְּ מַ־ה *what* to thee ? i.e. *what aileth* thee ? or *what dost thou want ?* [Gn 21:17](https://ref.ly/Gen21:17) מַה־לָּךְ הָגָר; [Jos 15:18](https://ref.ly/Josh15:18), [2 S 14:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:5), [1 K 1:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:16), [2 K 6:28](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:28), Ez [18:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:2) (accents); sq. כִּי, Ju [18:23](https://ref.ly/Judg18:23) מה־לך כי נזעקת, [Gn 20:9](https://ref.ly/Gen20:9) (cf. [1 S 11:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:5) לעם כי יבכו מה), [Is 22:1](https://ref.ly/Isa22:1) מַה־לָּךְ כִּי עָלִיתְ (cf. τί παθὼν, τοῦτο ποιεῖς;), [Psalm 114:5](https://ref.ly/Ps114:5); without כִּי [Is 3:15](https://ref.ly/Isa3:15) (cf. Qor 57:8, [57:10](https://ref.ly/Isa57:10)); with a ptcp., [Jon 1:6](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:6) *what is it to thee* as a sleeper ? (accus.: Da:§ 70 a cites Qor 74:50\), Ez [18:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:2) (if אתם be treated as strengthening לכם). (*d*) פֹה (לִי) מַה־לְּךָ \= *what* hast thou (have I) here ? Ju [18:3](https://ref.ly/Judg18:3), [1 K 19:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:9), [Is 22:16](https://ref.ly/Isa22:16), [52:5](https://ref.ly/Isa52:5). (*e*) in the genit., [Jer 8:9](https://ref.ly/Jer8:9) וְחָכְמַת־מֶה לָהֶם, and wisdom of *what* (\= what kind of wisdom) is theirs ? [Nu 23:3](https://ref.ly/Num23:3) (v. infr. **3**). **b.** often in an *indirect* question, as after ראה, [Gn 2:19](https://ref.ly/Gen2:19) to see מה־יקרא־לו *what* he would call it, [37:20](https://ref.ly/Gen37:20) וְנִרְאֶה מה־יהיו חלֽמֹתיו *to what* his dreams will come, [Nu 13:19](https://ref.ly/Num13:19) f. Hb [2:1](https://ref.ly/Hab2:1); הִכִּיר [Gn 31:32](https://ref.ly/Gen31:32); ידע [39:8](https://ref.ly/Gen39:8), [Ex 2:4](https://ref.ly/Exod2:4) לדעת מה־יֵּעָשֶה לו, [16:15](https://ref.ly/Exod16:15), [32:1](https://ref.ly/Exod32:1), [Jb 34:4](https://ref.ly/Job34:4); שׁמע [Nu 9:8](https://ref.ly/Num9:8) ´וְאשׁמעה מה־יצוה י (cf. [Psalm 85:9](https://ref.ly/Ps85:9)), [2 S 17:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:5); פֹּרַשׁ [Nu 15:34](https://ref.ly/Num15:34); שׁאל [1 K 3:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:5), [2 K 2:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:9); הֵבִין [Jb 6:24](https://ref.ly/Job6:24), [23:5](https://ref.ly/Job23:5): [Nu 13:18](https://ref.ly/Num13:18) מה היא וראיתם את הארץ, [Psalm 39:5](https://ref.ly/Ps39:5), [Is 41:22](https://ref.ly/Isa41:22) הראשׁנות מָה הֵנָּה הַגִּידוּ.—In some such cases it approximates in meaning to the simple rel., as [Jer 7:17](https://ref.ly/Jer7:17), [33:24](https://ref.ly/Jer33:24), [Mi 6:5](https://ref.ly/Mic6:5), [6:8](https://ref.ly/Mic6:8), [Jb 34:33](https://ref.ly/Job34:33). **c.** \= *of what kind ?* (Germ. *was für ein ... ?*), with an insinuation of blame, or reproach, or contempt: [Gn 37:10](https://ref.ly/Gen37:10) מה החלום הזה *what* is this dream which thou hast dreamt ? [44:15](https://ref.ly/Gen44:15), [Jos 22:16](https://ref.ly/Josh22:16), Ju [8:1](https://ref.ly/Judg8:1), [15:11](https://ref.ly/Judg15:11), [20:12](https://ref.ly/Judg20:12), [1 S 29:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam29:3) מָה הָעִבְרִים האלה, [1 K 9:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:13) מה העדים האלה אשׁר נתתה לי, [2 K 9:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:22), [18:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:19). **d.** מה is often used in questions to which the answer *little*, or *nothing*, is expected, and it thus becomes equivalent to a *rhetorical negative* (cf. הֲ **b**, מִי **f** *c*): (*a*) [Gn 23:15](https://ref.ly/Gen23:15) land worth 400 shekels..., מה היא *what is it ?* (i.e. it is something quite insignificant), [27:37](https://ref.ly/Gen27:37), Ju [8:3](https://ref.ly/Judg8:3) עשות ככם ומה־יכלתי, [14:18](https://ref.ly/Judg14:18), [Ho 9:5](https://ref.ly/Hos9:5), [10:3](https://ref.ly/Hos10:3), [Psalm 30:10](https://ref.ly/Ps30:10), [56:5](https://ref.ly/Ps56:5), [Jb 15:9](https://ref.ly/Job15:9), [16:6](https://ref.ly/Job16:6), [21:21](https://ref.ly/Job21:21), [22:13](https://ref.ly/Job22:13), [22:17](https://ref.ly/Job22:17), [La 2:13](https://ref.ly/Lam2:13), Ct [5:9](https://ref.ly/Song5:9) מַהאדּוֺדֵךְ מִדּוֺד *what* is thy beloved (more) than a(nother) beloved ? ‖לא, [1 K 12:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:16) מַה־לָּנוּ חֵלֶק בְּדָוֺד וְלאֹ־נַחֲלָה בְּבֶן יִשַׁי ([2 S 20:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:1) ´אֵין־לָנוּ חֵלֶק וג), [Jb 16:6](https://ref.ly/Job16:6). (*b*) sq. כִּי (כִּי **1 f**), [Gn 20:9](https://ref.ly/Gen20:9), [37:26](https://ref.ly/Gen37:26) מַה־בֶּצַע כִּי נַהֲרֹג *what* profit (is it) that we should slay him ? [Ex 16:7](https://ref.ly/Exod16:7), [Nu 16:11](https://ref.ly/Num16:11), Hb [2:18](https://ref.ly/Hab2:18), [2 K 8:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:13) *what* is thy servant, the dog, *that he should do*, etc.? and often in poetry, as [Psalm 8:5](https://ref.ly/Ps8:5) מָה אנושׁ כי תזכרנו *what* is man *that* thou rememberest him ? [Jb 6:11](https://ref.ly/Job6:11) מַה־כֹּחִי כִּי אֲיַחֵל , v:[11](https://ref.ly/Job6:11) b, [7:17](https://ref.ly/Job7:17), [15:12](https://ref.ly/Job15:12) f; [15:14](https://ref.ly/Job15:14), [16:3](https://ref.ly/Job16:3), [21:15](https://ref.ly/Job21:15) \+ (cf. ´מִ **f** *b*). Hence, (*c*) in the formula of repudiation, or emphatic denial, (וְלָכֶם) מַה־לִּי וָלָךְ *what* is there (common) to me and to thee ? i.e. what have I to do with thee ? † Ju [11:12](https://ref.ly/Judg11:12), [2 S 16:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:10) מה לי ולכם, [19:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:23), [1 K 17:18](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:18), [2 K 3:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:13), [2 Ch 35:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:21); cf. [Jos 22:24](https://ref.ly/Josh22:24), [2 K 9:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:18), [9:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:19); rather differently, without וְ, † [Je 2:18](https://ref.ly/Jer2:18) ´מה לך לדרך מ *what* is there to thee *with reference to* the way to Egypt ? [Ho 14:9](https://ref.ly/Hos14:9) מַהאלּוֺ לַעֲצַבִּים, cf. [Psalm 50:16](https://ref.ly/Ps50:16) (לְסַפֵּר); with את, † [Je 23:28](https://ref.ly/Jer23:28) מַה־לַתֶּבֶן אֶת־הַבָּר *beside* (or *in comparison with*) the wheat ? cf. τί ἐμοὶ (ἡμῖν)καὶ σοί; [Mt 8:29](https://ref.ly/Matt8:29), [Mk 5:7](https://ref.ly/Mark5:7), [Jn 2:4](https://ref.ly/John2:4); and Ar. مَا لِى وَلَهُ **e.** \= *whatsoever* (cf. מִי **g**): (*a*) Ju [9:48](https://ref.ly/Judg9:48) ראיתם עשיתי מהרו עשו כמוני מה אתם, lit. *what* do you see (that) I have done ? hasten, and do like me (\= *whatever* ye see, etc.), [2 S 21:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:4) אעשה לכם מה אתם אמרים, [Jb 6:24](https://ref.ly/Job6:24); with the apod. introd. by וְ, [1 S 20:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:4) מה תאמר נפשׁך ואעשה לך; [Est 5:3](https://ref.ly/Esth5:3) וְיִנָּתֵן לָךְ מַ־ה…בַּקָּשָׁתֵךְ, v:[6](https://ref.ly/Esth5:6), [7:2](https://ref.ly/Esth7:2), [9:12](https://ref.ly/Esth9:12); hence in the late and strange idiom of Ch., it sinks twice almost to the rel. *what*, [1 Ch 15:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:13) כי לְמַבָּרִאשׁוֺנָה לא אתם because ye were not (employed) for *what* was at first (on the former occasion), J. etc., [2 Ch 30:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:3) לְמַדַּי acc. to *what* was sufficient (\= in sufficient numbers), cf. [Est 9:26](https://ref.ly/Esth9:26). (*b*) \-מַה־שֶּׁ (late: frequently in Mishn., etc.), *whatever, what* (cf. מִי אֲשֶׁר, מִי **g** *end*): † [Ec 1:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:9) מַה־שֶּׁהָיָה הוּא שֶׁיִּהְיֶה, lit. *what* is that which hath been ? it is that which shall be (\= *whatever* hath been, it is that which shall be), v:[9](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:9), [3:15](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:15), [3:22](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:22), [6:10](https://ref.ly/Eccl6:10), [7:24](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:24), [8:7](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:7), [10:14](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:14). **2\.** Used adverbially: **a.** as an *interrogative:* (*a*) *how ?* especially in expressing what is regarded as an impossibility, [Gn 44:16](https://ref.ly/Gen44:16) מַה־נִּצְטַדָּק *how* shall we justify ourselves ? [Nu 23:8](https://ref.ly/Num23:8), [23:8](https://ref.ly/Num23:8), [1 S 10:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:27) מַה־יּשִׁעֵנוּ זֶה, [2 K 4:43](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:43), [Jb 9:2](https://ref.ly/Job9:2), [25:4](https://ref.ly/Job25:4), [25:4](https://ref.ly/Job25:4), [31:1](https://ref.ly/Job31:1) I made a covenant with my eyes, ´וּמָה אֶתְבּוֺנֵן עַל ב and *how* should I look upon a maid ? (LXX οὐ, Vulgate *non*), [Pr 20:24](https://ref.ly/Prov20:24) דַּרְכּוֺ וְאָדָם מַה־יָּבִין; in an indirect question, [Ex 10:26](https://ref.ly/Exod10:26), [Psalm 39:5](https://ref.ly/Ps39:5) אֵֽדְעָה מֶה־חָדֵל אָנִי; מַה־זֶּה, *how, now ?* (in surprise), [Gn 27:20](https://ref.ly/Gen27:20) מַה־זֶּה מִהַרְתָּ לִמְצאֹ בְּנִי, Ju [18:24](https://ref.ly/Judg18:24), [1 K 21:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:5), [2 K 1:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs1:5). (*b*) *why ?* [Ex 14:15](https://ref.ly/Exod14:15) אֵלַי מַה תִּצְעַק, [17:2](https://ref.ly/Exod17:2), [17:2](https://ref.ly/Exod17:2), [2 K 6:33](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:33), [7:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs7:3), [Psalm 42:6](https://ref.ly/Ps42:6) מַה־תִּשְׁתּוֺחֲחִי עלי, [Jb 15:12](https://ref.ly/Job15:12) \+ ; Ct [8:4](https://ref.ly/Song8:4) I adjure you מַה־תָּעִירוּ וּמַה־תְּעוֺרֲרוּ, *why* will ye stir up, etc.? (i.e. *do not:* ‖אִם [2:7](https://ref.ly/Song2:7), [3:5](https://ref.ly/Song3:5)).—The transition from the interrog. to the neg., to which in Heb. there is an approx. (v. supr.: esp. [1 K 12:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:16), [Jb 31:1](https://ref.ly/Job31:1), Ct [8:4](https://ref.ly/Song8:4)), is in Ar. complete, مَا being there used constantly in the sense of *not* (cf. WalkerHbr. xii. 244 ff.; Köiii, 478\). **b.** as an *exclam., how ...!* with adjectives and verbs, [Gn 28:17](https://ref.ly/Gen28:17) מַה־נּוֺרָא הַמָּקוֺם הַזֶּה *how* dreadful is this place! [38:29](https://ref.ly/Gen38:29), [Nu 24:5](https://ref.ly/Num24:5) מַה־טֹּבוּ אֹהָלֶיְ, [Is 52:7](https://ref.ly/Isa52:7), [Psalm 3:2](https://ref.ly/Ps3:2) מה רבו, [8:2](https://ref.ly/Ps8:2) מה אדיר, [21:2](https://ref.ly/Ps21:2), [36:8](https://ref.ly/Ps36:8), etc., Ct [4:10](https://ref.ly/Song4:10), [4:10](https://ref.ly/Song4:10), [7:2](https://ref.ly/Song7:2); ironically, [2 S 6:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:20), [Je 2:33](https://ref.ly/Jer2:33), [2:36](https://ref.ly/Jer2:36), [Jb 26:2](https://ref.ly/Job26:2), [26:3](https://ref.ly/Job26:3). **3\.** Indef. pron. *anything, aught*, [Nu 23:3](https://ref.ly/Num23:3) וּדְבַר־מַה־יַּרְאֵנִי וְהִגַּדְתִּי לָךְ and he will shew me the matter of *aught*, and I will tell thee (\= if he shew me... I will, etc., Dr§ 149\), [1 S 19:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:3) וְרָאִיתִי מָה וִהִגַּדְתִּי לָךְ \= and if I see aught, I will, etc., [20:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:10) אוֺ מַה־יַּעַנְִ אָבִיְ קָשָׁה if perchance thy father shall answer thee *aught* that is harsh (order, **1 a** *a*), [2 S 18:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:22) וִיהִי־מָה אָרוּצָה־נָּא but let there happen *what may* (lit.*aught*), I will run, v:[23](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:23) (cf. [Jb 13:13](https://ref.ly/Job13:13) וְיַעֲבֹר עָלַי מָה), v:[29](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:29) וְלאֹ יָדַעְתִּי מָה (cf. [Pr 9:13](https://ref.ly/Prov9:13)), [Pr 25:8](https://ref.ly/Prov25:8). Cf. Köiii. § 65\. **4\.** With preps.: **a.** בַּמָּה:9, בַּמֶּה:19 *wherein ?* [Ex 22:26](https://ref.ly/Exod22:26), Ju [16:5](https://ref.ly/Judg16:5), [1 S 14:38](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:38) (indirect question; We בְּמִי); and so acc. to the various senses of בְּ: *whereby ?* [Gn 15:8](https://ref.ly/Gen15:8), [Ex 33:16](https://ref.ly/Exod33:16), [Mal 1:2](https://ref.ly/Mal1:2), [1:6](https://ref.ly/Mal1:6), [1:7](https://ref.ly/Mal1:7), [2:17](https://ref.ly/Mal2:17), [3:7](https://ref.ly/Mal3:7)–[8](https://ref.ly/Mal3:8); *wherewith ?* [1 S 6:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:2), [2 S 21:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:3), [Mi 6:6](https://ref.ly/Mic6:6); *by what means ?* Ju [16:5](https://ref.ly/Judg16:5) וּבַמֶּה נוּכַל לוֺ; *at what* (worth) ? [Is 2:22](https://ref.ly/Isa2:22); *for what ?* [2 Ch 7:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:21) (‖ [1 K 9:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:8) על־מה). **b.** יַעַן מֶה † [Hg 1:9](https://ref.ly/Hag1:9) *because of what ?* † **c.** כַּמָּה, כַּמֶּה, prop. *the like of what ?* (Ar. كَمْ, Syr. ܟܡܳܐ); hence (*a*) *how much ? how many ?* ´כַּמָּה יְמֵי וגו [Gn 47:8](https://ref.ly/Gen47:8), [2 S 19:35](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:35), [Psalm 119:84](https://ref.ly/Ps119:84), [Jb 13:23](https://ref.ly/Job13:23); עַד כַּמֶּה פְעָמִים [1 K 22:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:16) (\= [2 Ch 18:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:15)); כַּמָּה *how often ?* [Jb 21:17](https://ref.ly/Job21:17) (i.e. how seldom !); in an indirect question, *how much ?* [Zc 2:6](https://ref.ly/Zech2:6), [2:6](https://ref.ly/Zech2:6). As an exclamation, [Zc 7:3](https://ref.ly/Zech7:3) as I have done זֶה כַמֶּה שָׁנִים now (זֶה **4 i**), *how many* years ! [Psalm 78:40](https://ref.ly/Ps78:40) כַּמָּה *how often !* (*b*) *for how long ?* [Psalm 35:17](https://ref.ly/Ps35:17) כמה תראה, [Jb 7:19](https://ref.ly/Job7:19). **d.** לָ֫מָּה, לָמָ֫ה (לָמָ֫ה mostly before the gutturals א, ה, ע and ´י \[i.e. אֲדֹנָי], but twice besides, [Psalm 42:10](https://ref.ly/Ps42:10), [43:2](https://ref.ly/Ps43:2); לָ֫מָּה also occurs before gutt., in five places noted by Mas. on [Psalm 43:2](https://ref.ly/Ps43:2), viz. [1 S 28:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam28:15), [2 S 2:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:22), [14:31](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:31), [Psalm 49:6](https://ref.ly/Ps49:6), [Je 15:18](https://ref.ly/Jer15:18), and before ךְ † [Gn 4:6](https://ref.ly/Gen4:6), [2 S 14:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:13), [24:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:3), [Ec 2:15](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:15)), לָ֫מָה † [Jb 7:20](https://ref.ly/Job7:20), לָ֫מֶה † [1 S 1:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:8), [1:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:8), [1:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:8), *for what reason ? why?* [Gn 4:6](https://ref.ly/Gen4:6) למה חרה לך *why* art thou angry ? [24:31](https://ref.ly/Gen24:31), etc.; often strengthened by זֶה (זֶה **4 e**), [Gn 18:13](https://ref.ly/Gen18:13), [32:30](https://ref.ly/Gen32:30) (\= Ju [13:18](https://ref.ly/Judg13:18)) למה זה תשׁאל לשׁמי, [33:15](https://ref.ly/Gen33:15), [Ex 2:20](https://ref.ly/Exod2:20), [5:22](https://ref.ly/Exod5:22), [17:3](https://ref.ly/Exod17:3), etc., [Je 6:20](https://ref.ly/Jer6:20), [20:18](https://ref.ly/Jer20:18) \+ ; [Gn 25:22](https://ref.ly/Gen25:22) זה אנכי אם כן למ־ה if so, *why, then*, am I ? (why do I continue to live ?); \= *to what purpose* (sq. לִ Pers.), [Gn 27:46](https://ref.ly/Gen27:46) למה לי חיים, [Is 1:11](https://ref.ly/Isa1:11), [Je 6:20](https://ref.ly/Jer6:20), [Am 5:18](https://ref.ly/Amos5:18) למה זה ´לכם יום י, [Jb 30:2](https://ref.ly/Job30:2); in an indirect question, [1 S 6:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:3), [Dn 10:20](https://ref.ly/Dan10:20). Note especially (*a*) in expostulations, [Gn 12:18](https://ref.ly/Gen12:18) למה לא הגדת לי *why* didst thou not tell me, etc. ? v:[19](https://ref.ly/Gen12:19), [29:25](https://ref.ly/Gen29:25), [31:27](https://ref.ly/Gen31:27), [42:1](https://ref.ly/Gen42:1), [43:6](https://ref.ly/Gen43:6), [1 S 21:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:15), [22:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:13), [24:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:10), [Psalm 22:2](https://ref.ly/Ps22:2), [44:24](https://ref.ly/Ps44:24), [44:25](https://ref.ly/Ps44:25), [74:1](https://ref.ly/Ps74:1), [74:11](https://ref.ly/Ps74:11) \+ oft.; (*b*) with an impf., oft. deprecating, or introducing rhetorically, the reason why something should, or should not, be done, *why should…?* [1 S 19:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:5), [19:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:17), [20:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:8) but to thy father (emph.) למה זה תביאני, *why shouldst* thou bring me ? [20:32](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:32) מה עשה למה יומת *why should* he be put to death ? [2 S 13:26](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:26), [16:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:9), [20:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:19), [2 K 14:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:10), etc.: in such cases, it approximates in meaning to *lest* (cf. Ph. CISi. 2, 21 יסגרינם אלנם לָםָ *ne* tradant eos dii), and is in LXX often rendered by μήποτε, as [Gn 27:45](https://ref.ly/Gen27:45) למה אשׁכל *why should* I be bereaved, etc. ? [Ex 32:12](https://ref.ly/Exod32:12) יאמרו מצרים למה [Ne 6:3](https://ref.ly/Neh6:3), [Psalm 79:10](https://ref.ly/Ps79:10), [115:2](https://ref.ly/Ps115:2), [Ec 7:16](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:16), μή, [Jer 40:15](https://ref.ly/Jer40:15), ἵνα μή, [Gn 47:19](https://ref.ly/Gen47:19), [2 S 2:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:22), [2 Ch 25:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:16), [Ec 5:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:5), [7:17](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:17), or ὅπως μή, [Jo 2:17](https://ref.ly/Joel2:17) (in [1 S 19:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:17), [2 S 13:26](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:26), paraph. by εἰ μή); and, connected with the foreg. sentence by אשׁר, or שֶׁ, in late, or dial., Heb. it actually has that mng., [Dn 1:10](https://ref.ly/Dan1:10) אֲשֶׁר לָמָּה יִרְאֶה *lest* he see, Ct [1:7](https://ref.ly/Song1:7) שַׁלָּמָ֫ה אֶהְיֶה *lest* I become (so in Aram. לְמָה דִּי [Ezr 7:23](https://ref.ly/Ezra7:23), דִּילְמָא Targum, Syr. ܕܰܠܡܳܐ, both regularly \= *lest*). **e.** עַד־מָה ([Psalm 4:3](https://ref.ly/Ps4:3) עַד־מֶה) *until when ? how long ?* † [Nu 24:22](https://ref.ly/Num24:22) (aposiop.), [Psalm 4:3](https://ref.ly/Ps4:3), [79:5](https://ref.ly/Ps79:5), [89:47](https://ref.ly/Ps89:47); in indirect qu., [Psalm 74:9](https://ref.ly/Ps74:9) (cf. עד אנה, עד מתי). **f.** עַל־מָה, and עַל־מֶה, *upon what ?* [Jb 38:6](https://ref.ly/Job38:6), [2 Ch 32:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:10); *upon what ground ? wherefore ?* [Nu 22:32](https://ref.ly/Num22:32) עַל־מָה הִכִּיתָ אֶת־אֲתֹנְ, [Dt 29:23](https://ref.ly/Deut29:23) (cf. [1 K 9:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:8), [Je 22:8](https://ref.ly/Jer22:8)), [Is 1:5](https://ref.ly/Isa1:5), [Je 8:14](https://ref.ly/Jer8:14), [9:11](https://ref.ly/Jer9:11), [16:10](https://ref.ly/Jer16:10), Ez [21:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:12), [Psalm 10:13](https://ref.ly/Ps10:13), [Jb 13:14](https://ref.ly/Job13:14) (probably dittogr. from v:[13](https://ref.ly/Job13:13)); על־מה־זה [Ne 2:4](https://ref.ly/Neh2:4). In an indirect question, [Jb 10:2](https://ref.ly/Job10:2) הוֺדִיעֻנִי עַל מַה־תְּרִיבֻנִי, [Est 4:5](https://ref.ly/Esth4:5) לָדַעַת מַה־זֶּה וְעַל־מַה־וֶּה.

## מָהַהּ (id: BDB05066)

\[Str4102] † \[מָהַהּ] **vb.** only **Hithpalp.** linger, tarry (cf. Ar. مَهَهٌ *slow walk, delay* (Ḳam.));—**Hithpalp.** *Pf.* 1 S. הִתְמַהְמָהְתִּי [Psalm 119:60](https://ref.ly/Ps119:60); 3 pl. הִתְמַהְמְהוּ Ju [19:8](https://ref.ly/Judg19:8); 1 pl. הִתְמַהְמָהְנוּ [Gn 43:10](https://ref.ly/Gen43:10); *Impf.* יִתְמַהְמַהּ Hb [2:3](https://ref.ly/Hab2:3); ´וַיִּתְמ [Gn 19:16](https://ref.ly/Gen19:16); *Imv.* mpl. הִתְמַהְמְהוּ [Is 29:9](https://ref.ly/Isa29:9); *Inf.* לְהִתְמַחְמֵהַּ [Ex 12:39](https://ref.ly/Exod12:39); *Pt.* מִתְמַהְמֵהַּ [2 S 15:28](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:28) ;—*linger, tarry, wait*, of Pers., [Gn 19:16](https://ref.ly/Gen19:16) [43:10](https://ref.ly/Gen43:10) (J), [Ex 12:39](https://ref.ly/Exod12:39) (E), Ju [3:26](https://ref.ly/Judg3:26), [19:8](https://ref.ly/Judg19:8), [2 S 15:28](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:28), [Is 29:9](https://ref.ly/Isa29:9), [Psalm 119:60](https://ref.ly/Ps119:60) (c. לֽא, opp. חַ֫שְׁתִּי); subj. חָזוֺן *vision* Hb [2:3](https://ref.ly/Hab2:3)

> \[Str4106–04] מְהוּמָה v. הום. מְהוּמָן v. אמן. p. 54, 223

> \[Str4105] מְהֵיטַבְאֵל v. יטב

## מָהַל (id: BDB05069)

\[Str4107] † \[מָהַל] **vb.** circumcise, weaken (si vera l.; cf. NH מָהַל *circumcise*, Aram. מְהַל *id.*);—only fig. **Qal** *Pt. pass.* סָבְאֵח מָהוּל בַּמָּיִם [Is 1:22](https://ref.ly/Isa1:22) *thy choice wine weakened with water*, so Thes De CheComm Di Du; cf. *vinum castrare*, Plinxix. 53: BaErkl. d. Jes. CheHpt (cf. NöZMG xl.1886, 741\) comp. NH מוהל, *juice* (of fruit, esp. olives); Ar. مُهْلٌ *oil, liquid pitch, suppurating matter;* v. against this Di.

II. מהר); Eth. መሀረ፡ *train teach;* Syr. ܡܗܪ Pa. *id.;* cf. also Egypt. *māhar, trained warrior* Bondi57);—**Niph.** *Pf.* 3 fs. נִמְהָרָה [Jb 5:13](https://ref.ly/Job5:13); *Pt.* נִמְהָר Hb [1:6](https://ref.ly/Hab1:6); pl. נִמְהָרִים [Is 32:4](https://ref.ly/Isa32:4); cstr. נִמְהֲרֵי [Is 35:4](https://ref.ly/Isa35:4); —*be hurried* \= anxious, disturbed נְמְהֲרֵי־לֵב [Is 35:4](https://ref.ly/Isa35:4); *hasty, precipitate*, subj. עֵצָה [Jb 5:13](https://ref.ly/Job5:13); so pt. as subst. ([1 S 28:30](https://ref.ly/1Sam28:30) v. infr.); 3 fpl. תְּמַהֵרְנָה [Je 9:17](https://ref.ly/Jer9:17) etc.; *Imv.* ms. מַהֵר [Gn 19:22](https://ref.ly/Gen19:22) \+ ; מַהֲרָה [1 S 23:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:27), [1 K 22:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:9), etc.; *Inf. cstr.* מַהֵר [Pr 7:23](https://ref.ly/Prov7:23) \+ ; *Pt.* מְמַהֵר [Gn 41:32](https://ref.ly/Gen41:32), [Mal 3:5](https://ref.ly/Mal3:5) (on מַהֵר [Zp 1:14](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:14) \[[1 S 23:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:22)] cf. ## I מו (id: BDB05086)

I. מו a paragogic syllable, attached to בְּ, כְּ, לְ (q. v.), so as to form with them independent words, בְּמוֺ, כְּמוֺ, לְמוֺ (q. v.) These forms, except sometimes כְּמוֺ, are exclusively poetical. In origin מוֺ is identical with מָה *what*, Ar. مَا, which is used similarly, pleonastically, with certain prefixes, e.g. بِمَا, كَمَا, عَمَّا. See Qor 3:153, 71:25; FlKl. Schr. i. 473f., 479, 558; WAG ii. § 70, Rem. *f*; SG 126f.; Köii. 250 f.; and cf. Eth. ከመ፡, Syr. ܐܰܟܡܳܐ, Sab. בנמו \= مِمّمَاء, DHMZDMG 1883, 396 f., HomChrest. § 18\.

## מוּג (id: BDB05092)

\[Str4127] † מוּג **vb.** melt (Targum מוּג Ithp., [Psalm 46:7](https://ref.ly/Ps46:7), [107:26](https://ref.ly/Ps107:26); cf. Ar. مَاجَ *surge* (of the sea), Qor 18:99; مَوْجٌ *a wave*);—**Qal** *Impf*. 3 fs. תָּמוּג [46:7](https://ref.ly/Ps46:7); וַתָּמוֺג [Am 9:5](https://ref.ly/Amos9:5); 2 ms. sf. וַתְּמוּגֵ֫נוּ [Is 64:6](https://ref.ly/Isa64:6) (but rd. prob. וַתְּמַגְּנֵנוּ, v. \[מָגַן] p.171 supr., and cf. Di \> Du וַתְּמַגְּנֵנוּ); *Inf.* לָמוּג Ez [21:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:20) ;—**1\.** *melt*, subj. אֶרֶץ, at touch of ´י [Am 9:5](https://ref.ly/Amos9:5); at voice of ´י [Psalm 46:7](https://ref.ly/Ps46:7); \= *faint* (of heart), Ez [21:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:20). **2\.** trans. *cause to melt* [Is 64:6](https://ref.ly/Isa64:6) (but v. supr.) **Niph.** *Pf.* נָמוֺג [Na 2:7](https://ref.ly/Nah2:7) \+ 2 t.; 3 pl. נָמֹ֫גוּ [Ex 15:15](https://ref.ly/Exod15:15) \+ 3 t.; *Pt.* pl. נְמֹגִים [Psalm 75:4](https://ref.ly/Ps75:4) ;—*melt away*, fig. for be helpless, disorganized (through terror, etc.) [Ex 15:15](https://ref.ly/Exod15:15) (cf. context), [Jos 2:9](https://ref.ly/Josh2:9), [2:24](https://ref.ly/Josh2:24), [1 S 14:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:16) (cf. We Dr), [Je 49:23](https://ref.ly/Jer49:23), cf. [Na 2:7](https://ref.ly/Nah2:7), [Is 14:31](https://ref.ly/Isa14:31), [Psalm 75:4](https://ref.ly/Ps75:4). **Po‘l.** *soften, dissolve*, act.: only Impf. 2 ms. sf. (subj. God): בִּרְבִיבִם תְּמֹגְגֶנָּה [Psalm 65:11](https://ref.ly/Ps65:11)  *thou softenest it* \[the earth] *with showers;* fig., \= *dissipate* וּתְמֹֽגְגֵ֫נִי [Jb 30:22](https://ref.ly/Job30:22)—We rds. וַיְמֹגֵג Hb [3:6](https://ref.ly/Hab3:6) for וַיְמֹדֶד, v. מדד **Po‘. Hithpo‘l.** Pf. 3 pl. הִתְמָֹ\+גגוּ [Na 1:5](https://ref.ly/Nah1:5); *Impf.* 3 fs. תִּתְמוָֺ\+גג [Psalm 107:26](https://ref.ly/Ps107:26); 3 fpl. תִּתְמוֺגַגְנָה [Am 9:13](https://ref.ly/Amos9:13); —*melt*, subj. hills (before ´י) [Na 1:5](https://ref.ly/Nah1:5); subj. נֶפֶשׁ [Psalm 107:26](https://ref.ly/Ps107:26) (in terror); hyperb. for *flow* [Am 9:13](https://ref.ly/Amos9:13) (of fertile hills, ‖הִטִּ֫יפוּ הֶהָרִים).

556:a. מוּג: in view of prevailing fig. use, and of Ar. مَاجَ (موج) *be in tumult, commotion,* of sea, people, *be agitated, perplexed,* it is perh. dub. whether *melt* is orig. meaning; *be moved, agitated* is usu. poss., and even [Am 9:13](https://ref.ly/Amos9:13), [Psalm 65:11](https://ref.ly/Ps65:11) (both late), where (esp. Am) *moistening* is suggested, this may be perh. derived fr. *agitating, loosening, dissolving*.

\[Str4131] † \[מוֺט] **vb.** totter, shake, slip (usu. poet.) (without רֶגֶל) [Pr 24:11](https://ref.ly/Prov24:11) לַהֶרֶד ´מ; לִפְנֵי־רָשָׁע ´מ [25:26](https://ref.ly/Prov25:26); *shake*, intr., subj. יָד [Lv 25:35](https://ref.ly/Lev25:35) (H; of feebleness); subj. גְּבָעוֺת [Is 54:10](https://ref.ly/Isa54:10) (‖מוּשׁ); הָרִים בְלֵב יַמִּים ´מ [Psalm 46:3](https://ref.ly/Ps46:3) (‖הֵמִיר, cf. מור) (both symb. of extreme insecurity); cf. subj. בְּרִית [Is 54:10](https://ref.ly/Isa54:10); subj. מַמְלָכוֺת [Psalm 46:7](https://ref.ly/Ps46:7); subj. אֶרֶץ (\= land) [Psalm 60:4](https://ref.ly/Ps60:4); cf. ## מוּךְ (id: BDB05100)

\[Str4134] †\[מוּךְ] **vb.** be low, depressed, grow poor (NH *id.,* Niph.; Aram. מוּךְ, *sink* or *bend down;* cf. מָכַח, Syr. ܡܰܟ, ܡܟܟ, *be brought low, humble*);—**Qal** *Pf.* consec. וּמָח [Lv 27:8](https://ref.ly/Lev27:8) (P); *Impf.* יָמוּח [Lv 25:25](https://ref.ly/Lev25:25), [25:35](https://ref.ly/Lev25:35), [25:39](https://ref.ly/Lev25:39) (all H); *Pt.* מָח [Lv 25:47](https://ref.ly/Lev25:47) (P): all of impoverished Israelites.

* **Associated Passages:** Leviticus 25:25; Leviticus 27:8; Leviticus 25:35; Leviticus 25:39; Leviticus 25:47


## I מוּל (id: BDB05101)

\[Str4136] †I. מוּל, מוֺל († [Dt 1:1](https://ref.ly/Deut1:1)), מוֺאל († [Ne 12:38](https://ref.ly/Neh12:38)) **subst.** and **prep.** front, in front of (deriv. dub.; acc. to Ol:§ 223 c Sta§ 378 a, for מְאוֺל from אוּל *to be in front:* v. further Köii. 300 f. HptBAS i. 172\):—**1\. subst.** only [1 K 7:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:5) (si vera 1\.: v. StaZAW 1883, 151\) וּמוּל מֶחֱזָה אֶל־מֶחֱזָה and the *front* of (one) window was towards (the other) window, and [Ne 12:38](https://ref.ly/Neh12:38) לְמוֺאל \= *in the opposite direction* (but rd. prob. לִשְמאוֺל, cf. לַיָּמִין v:[31](https://ref.ly/Neh12:31)). **2\.** as **prep.,** [Dt 1:1](https://ref.ly/Deut1:1), [2:19](https://ref.ly/Deut2:19) מוּל בני עמון *in front of* the Ammonites, [3:29](https://ref.ly/Deut3:29) (\= [4:46](https://ref.ly/Deut4:46), [34:6](https://ref.ly/Deut34:6)) מוּל בית פעור, [11:30](https://ref.ly/Deut11:30), [Jos 18:18](https://ref.ly/Josh18:18), [19:46](https://ref.ly/Josh19:46) מוּל יָפוֺ [1 S 14:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:5), [14:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:5); fig. [Ex 18:19](https://ref.ly/Exod18:19) הֱיֵת אַתָּה לעם מול האלהים *in front of* God, i.e. representing God to them. With prefixes:—**a.** אל־מול *towards the front of,* [1 S 17:30](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:30) מֵאֶצְלוֺ אֶל־מוּל אַחֵר וַיִּסֹב *to the front of* another; *on the front of,* [Ex 34:3](https://ref.ly/Exod34:3) (pregn.) וְאַל־יִרְעוּ אל־מול ההר, [Jo 8:33](https://ref.ly/Josh8:33), [8:33](https://ref.ly/Josh8:33), [9:1](https://ref.ly/Josh9:1), [22:11](https://ref.ly/Josh22:11) (v. WAWJPh. xiii. 117 ff.); spec. מוּל פְּנֵי אֶ־ל *towards* or *on the forefront of,* [Ex 26:9](https://ref.ly/Exod26:9), [28:25](https://ref.ly/Exod28:25) (‖ [39:18](https://ref.ly/Exod39:18)), v:[37](https://ref.ly/Exod28:37), [Lv 8:9](https://ref.ly/Lev8:9), [Nu 8:2](https://ref.ly/Num8:2), [8:3](https://ref.ly/Num8:3), [2 S 11:15](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:15) set ye Uriah אל־מול פני המלחמה. **b.** מִמּוּל:—(*a*) *from the front of,* [Mi 2:8](https://ref.ly/Mic2:8) (text dub.: rd. prob. מֵעַל). (*b*) of position, *off* (v. מן **1 c**) *the front of,* [Lv 5:8](https://ref.ly/Lev5:8) מִמּוּל עָרְפּוֺ *off the front of* its neck, i.e. close in front of it, [Nu 22:5](https://ref.ly/Num22:5) והוא יושׁב מִמֻּלִי *close in front of* me, [2 S 5:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:23) ([1 Ch 14:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr14:14)) ממול בכאים ובאת להם come to them *off the front of* the mulberry\-trees, [1 K 7:39](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:39) ([2 Ch 4:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:10)) מִמּוּל נֶגֶב; מִמּוּל פָּנָיו *on the forefront of,* [Ex 28:27](https://ref.ly/Exod28:27) (‖ [39:20](https://ref.ly/Exod39:20)).

557:b. II \[מוּל], 1\. 2: after ‘cut off’ add ‘but only seemingly in the dub. passages (v. III. \[מָלַל] and Comm.) [Jb 14:2](https://ref.ly/Job14:2), [18:16](https://ref.ly/Job18:16), [24:24](https://ref.ly/Job24:24) \[MS Levy], [Psalm 37:2](https://ref.ly/Ps37:2), [90:6](https://ref.ly/Ps90:6).’

> \[Str4463] † \[מָמוֺת] **n.\[m.]** only pl. death (cf. Ar. ܡܡܐܬܢ);—cstr. מְמוֺתֵי תַחֲלֻאִים [Je 16:4](https://ref.ly/Jer16:4) of painful *death by diseases;* חָלָל ´מ Ez [28:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:3).—For הַמְּמוֺתִים (Kt) [2 K 11:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:2), *the slain,* Qr. הַמּוּמָתִים is certainly correct (v. מוּת **Hoph.** supr.)

> \[Str8546] †תְּמוּתָה **n.f.** death;—´בְּנֵי ת *children of death,* those worthy of death and appointed to death, [Psalm 79:11](https://ref.ly/Ps79:11), [102:21](https://ref.ly/Ps102:21) (see מָוֶת **2**).

## מזג (id: BDB05146)

מזג ( √ of foll. \= mix, cf. Syr. ܡܙܰܓ and deriv.; Targum מְזַג *mix, prepare by mixing.*—Ar. مَزَجَ *id.* is denom. fr. مِزَاجٌ v. foll., Frä172).

> \[Str4197] \[מֶ֫זֶג] **n.m.** mixture, i.e. mixed wine (NH *id.,* Aram., and thence מִזְנָא, מְזִיגָא; ܡܘܽܙܳܓܳܐ; Ar. مِزَاجٌ *water mixed with wine, mixture* (Frä l.c.), cf. مَزْجٌ);—אַל־יֶחְסַר הַמָּוֶג Ct [7:3](https://ref.ly/Song7:3) (Tosafoth here *spiced wine:* cf. LevyNHWB iii. 61\).

* **Associated Passages:** Song of Songs 7:3

Suidas μαζουρωθ \= ζωδία);—only ´לַמַּזּ [2 K 23:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:5) as obj. of worship (\+ בֵּעַל, שֶׁמֶשׁ, יָרֵחַ, and כָּל־צְבָא הַשָּׁמָיִם) LXX τοῖς μαζουρωθ; prob.\= מַזַּרוֺת [Jb 38:32](https://ref.ly/Job38:32).

## I מָחָה (id: BDB05175)

\[Str4229] † I. מָחָה **vb.** wipe, wipe out (NH *id.;* Ar. مَحَا, *efface, erase, cancel, obliterate;* Aram. מְחָא (I), cf. Ph. למחת  *acc. to stroke \= exactly* HoffinPh. Inschr., Abh. GGW, xxxvi. May 1889, 9\).—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. מָחָה [Nu 5:23](https://ref.ly/Num5:23) \+ 3 t.; 3 fs. מֽחֲתָה [Pr 30:20](https://ref.ly/Prov30:20) \+ 4 t. *Pf ; Impf.* יִמְחֶה [2 K 21:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs21:13); וַיִּמַח (Baer; var. וַיִּמַּח) [Gn 7:23](https://ref.ly/Gen7:23); Is. sf. אֶמְחֶנּוּ [Ex 32:33](https://ref.ly/Exod32:33); \+ 4 t. Impf.; *Imv.* מְחֵה [Psalm 51:3](https://ref.ly/Ps51:3), [51:11](https://ref.ly/Ps51:11); sf. מְחֵנִי [Ex 32:32](https://ref.ly/Exod32:32); *Inf. abs.* מָחֹה [Ex 17:14](https://ref.ly/Exod17:14); *cstr.* לִמְחוֺת [2 K 14:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:27); *Pt.* מֹחֶה [Is 43:25](https://ref.ly/Isa43:25); fpl. מֹחוֺת(לְ) [Pr 31:3](https://ref.ly/Prov31:3); (Ges Fl Nö De Str for MT לַמְחוֺת);—**1\.** *wipe*, the mouth [Pr 30:20](https://ref.ly/Prov30:20); tears from (מעל) the face [Is 25:8](https://ref.ly/Isa25:8); written curse, into the water (אל־מים) for drinking [Nu 5:23](https://ref.ly/Num5:23) (P); Moses' name from the book (מספר) of God [Ex 32:32](https://ref.ly/Exod32:32), [32:33](https://ref.ly/Exod32:33) (J); וּמָחִיתִי אֶ־ת יְרוּשָׁלִַם כַּאֲשֶׁר יִמְחֶה אֶת־הַצַּלַּחַת מָחָה וְהָפַךְ עַל־פָּנֶיהָ [2 K 21:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs21:13) *and I will wipe Jerusalem as one wipeth a dish,—he doth wipe and turn it* (but rd. prob. מָחֹה וְהָפֹךְ) *upside down.* **2\.** *blot out \= obliterate* from the memory, מִתַּחַת הַשָּׁמַיִם *from under heaven* e.g. the name [Dt 9:14](https://ref.ly/Deut9:14), [29:19](https://ref.ly/Deut29:19), [2 K 14:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:27); the remembrance [Ex 17:14](https://ref.ly/Exod17:14), [17:14](https://ref.ly/Exod17:14) (J), [Dt 25:19](https://ref.ly/Deut25:19); the name for ever [Psalm 9:6](https://ref.ly/Ps9:6); transgressions (פשׁע; no more remembered by God against sinner) [Psalm 51:3](https://ref.ly/Ps51:3), [Is 43:25](https://ref.ly/Isa43:25), [44:22](https://ref.ly/Isa44:22); עָון [Psalm 51:11](https://ref.ly/Ps51:11). **3\.** *blot out \= exterminate*, כל־היקוֺם all existing things [Gn 7:23](https://ref.ly/Gen7:23) (J) and mankind, מֵעַל פְּנֵי הָאֲדָמָה [Gn 6:7](https://ref.ly/Gen6:7), [7:4](https://ref.ly/Gen7:4) (J); מֹחוֺת מְלָכִין [Pr 31:3](https://ref.ly/Prov31:3) (with the text\-change, v. supr.) \= *destroyers of kings* (i.e. impure women), but expression strange and dubious.

 *Niph.* *Pf.* 3 mpl. נִמְחוּ Ez [6:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:6); *Impf.* יִמָּחֶה [Dt 25:6](https://ref.ly/Deut25:6), Ju [21:17](https://ref.ly/Judg21:17); יִמַּח [Psalm 109:13](https://ref.ly/Ps109:13); 3 fs. תִּמָּח [Psalm 109:14](https://ref.ly/Ps109:14); \+ 4 t. Impf.;—**1\.** *be wiped out* מִסֵּפֶר חַיִּים [Psalm 69:29](https://ref.ly/Ps69:29). **2\.** *be blotted out*, מִיִּשְרָאֵל of a name [Dt 25:6](https://ref.ly/Deut25:6), a tribe Ju [21:17](https://ref.ly/Judg21:17); name (‖ posterity) [Psalm 109:13](https://ref.ly/Ps109:13); from memory, of sins [Ne 3:37](https://ref.ly/Neh3:37), [Psalm 109:14](https://ref.ly/Ps109:14); reproach, [Pr 6:33](https://ref.ly/Prov6:33). **3\.** *be exterminated,* מִן־הָאָרֶץ [Gn 7:23](https://ref.ly/Gen7:23) (J); of idolatrous works of Israel Ez [6:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:6). **Hiph.** *Impf.* 2 ms. תֶּ֫מַח [Ne 13:14](https://ref.ly/Neh13:14); תֶָּמְחִי [Je 18:23](https://ref.ly/Jer18:23) (but tone suggests תֶּ֫מַח cf. Ges§ 75\. R. 17; Gie reads Qal and doubts Hiph. altogether); *Inf.* לַמְחוֺת [Pr 31:3](https://ref.ly/Prov31:3) ( \= לְהַמְחוֺת but see **Qal 3**);—*blot out*, from the memory; pious acts [Ne 13:14](https://ref.ly/Neh13:14); sins [Je 18:23](https://ref.ly/Jer18:23).* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 25:8; Genesis 7:23; Exodus 32:33; Psalms 51:11; Psalms 51:3; Psalms 109:14; Isaiah 44:22; Jeremiah 18:23; Numbers 5:23; Exodus 17:14; Proverbs 30:20; Exodus 32:32; Deuteronomy 29:19; Isaiah 43:25; Nehemiah 13:14; Proverbs 31:3; Genesis 6:7; 2 Kings 21:13; Psalms 109:13; Proverbs 6:33; Psalms 69:29; Ezekiel 6:6; 2 Kings 14:27; Psalms 9:6; Deuteronomy 25:6; Deuteronomy 9:14; Judges 21:17; Deuteronomy 25:19; Genesis 7:4; Nehemiah 3:37

## מחח (id: BDB05185)

מחח (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. مخّ IV. *be fat*, also *contain marrow*).

> \[Str4220] † \[מֵחַ] **n.\[m.]** fatling (Ph. מח *fat* (adj.); NH מֵיחָא fig. of *choice flour*);—only pl. abs.: עֹלוֺת מֵיחִים [Psalm 66:15](https://ref.ly/Ps66:15) *burnt offerings of fatlings;* so מֵחִים [Is 5:17](https://ref.ly/Isa5:17) (‖כְּבָשִים) acc. to Hi Du CheHpt (rd. חֳרָבוֺת and del. גָּרִים); most, *fat ones*, fig. for *rich men.*

## מָחַץ (id: BDB05213)

\[Str4272] †מָחַץ **vb.** smite through, wound severely, shatter (poet.) (As. *maḫâṣu, smite, wound,* DlHWB 398; Ar. مَخَضَ *churn; beat or agitate* the milk, etc.; Sab. מחַץַ DHMZXG xxx. 1876, 684; xxxvii. 1883, 338; Burgen u. Schlösser (SB. xcvii. 1881\), 965 (of stone\-cutter's work), HomChrestom. 127; Eth. ምሕፅ);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. ´מ [Jb 26:12](https://ref.ly/Job26:12) \+ 3 t.; וּמֽחֲצָה Ju [5:26](https://ref.ly/Judg5:26) etc.; *Impf.* יִמְחַץ [Jb 5:18](https://ref.ly/Job5:18), [Psalm 68:22](https://ref.ly/Ps68:22); 1 s. sf. אֶמְחָצֵם [Psalm 18:39](https://ref.ly/Ps18:39); ´וָא [2 S 22:39](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:39); —*smite through,* sq. ראֹשׁוֺ, of Jael, Ju [5:26](https://ref.ly/Judg5:26) (‖הָלַם, מָחַק, חָלַף); of Isr., *shattering* enemies, ´חִצָּיו יִמ [Nu 24:8](https://ref.ly/Num24:8) (obj. om.; ‖עַצְמֹתֵיהֶם יְגָרֵם) cf. v:[17](https://ref.ly/Num24:17) (obj פַּֽאֲתֵי מוֺאָב); cf. [Psalm 18:39](https://ref.ly/Ps18:39) \= [2 S 22:39](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:39); of ´י,*smiting through or shattering* his foes, [Psalm 110:5](https://ref.ly/Ps110:5); their heads v:[6](https://ref.ly/Ps110:6), [68:22](https://ref.ly/Ps68:22), Hb [3:13](https://ref.ly/Hab3:13), loins Dt 33:l; cf. [Jb 26:12](https://ref.ly/Job26:12) obj. רַהַב, q.v.; abs., [Dt 32:39](https://ref.ly/Deut32:39) (opp. רָפָא; ‖ cl. אָמִית וַאֲחַיֶּה) cf. [Jb 5:18](https://ref.ly/Job5:18) (opp. רָפָא, ‖ cl. יַכְאִיב וְיֶחְבָּשׁ). For תִּמְחַץ רגלך בְּדָם [Psalm 68:24](https://ref.ly/Ps68:24), rd. תִּרְחַץ ([58:11](https://ref.ly/Ps58:11)), Ol Br Che Bae We.

> \[Str4273] †מַ֫חַץ **n.\[m.]** severe wound;—only cstr. מַחַץ מַכָּתוֺ יִרְפָּא [Is 30:26](https://ref.ly/Isa30:26) of ´י's restoration of his people (‖חָבַשׁ שֶׁבֶר עַמּוֺ).

## מחר (id: BDB05218)

מחר (√ of foll.; *be in front of meet,* cf. As. *maḫâru,* DlHWB 400 *come to meet, receive* \[cf. קִכֵּל], and IdProl 93; Sab. מחַר *receive*, etc., n. *id., token of favour,* ‖ *gift,* נחלת; DHMZMG xxxvii. 1883, 408\).

> \[Str4279] מָחָר:52 **n.m.,** used oft.as **adv.,** to\-morrow in time to come (Aram. מְחַר ܡܚܰܪ; prob. properly *time in front,* Köii. 263, cf. √, and As. *maḫru, front,* oft. of time, though alw. of *former time, of old,* DlHWB 402\);—**1\.***to\-morrow* (the day following the *present* day),**a.** as *subst.* [Ex 16:23](https://ref.ly/Exod16:23) מָחָר ´שַׁבָּתוֺן שַׁבַּת קֹדֶשׁ לְי *to\-morrow* is a sabbath, etc., [1 S 20:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:5) חֹדֶשׁ מָחָר *to\-morrow* is the new moon, v:[18](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:18); in יוֺם מָחָר †[Gn 30:33](https://ref.ly/Gen30:33), [Pr 27:1](https://ref.ly/Prov27:1), [Is 56:12](https://ref.ly/Isa56:12); in the idiom. phr. כָּעֵת מָחָר at the like of the time, (being) *to\-morrow, \= about to\-morrow* (v. כְּ **1 a**) † [Ex 9:18](https://ref.ly/Exod9:18), [1 S 9:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:16), [20:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:12), [1 K 19:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:2), [20:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:6), [2 K 7:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs7:1), [7:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs7:18), [10:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:6); and in לְמָחָר † [Ex 8:19](https://ref.ly/Exod8:19) לְמָחָר יִהְיֶה הָאֹת הַזֶּה, \= *for, against, to\-morrow*, [Ex 8:6](https://ref.ly/Exod8:6), [Nu 11:18](https://ref.ly/Num11:18), [Jos 7:13](https://ref.ly/Josh7:13), [Est 5:12](https://ref.ly/Esth5:12). **b.** as *adv. acc.* [Ex 8:25](https://ref.ly/Exod8:25), [9:5](https://ref.ly/Exod9:5) מָחָר יַעֲשֶה הַדָּבָר הַזֶּה ´י, [10:4](https://ref.ly/Exod10:4) הִנְנִי מֵבִיא מָחָר אַרְבֶּה, [17:9](https://ref.ly/Exod17:9), [32:5](https://ref.ly/Exod32:5), [Nu 14:25](https://ref.ly/Num14:25), [1 S 11:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:9) etc., [Jos 11:6](https://ref.ly/Josh11:6) מָחָר כָּעֵת הַזּאֹת; opp. to הַיּוֺם [Ex 19:10](https://ref.ly/Exod19:10) הַיּוֺם וּמָחָר, [Jos 22:18](https://ref.ly/Josh22:18), [2 S 11:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:12) a; rhetorically, [Is 22:13](https://ref.ly/Isa22:13) כִּי מָחָר נָמוּת. **2\.** \= *in future time*, [Ex 13:14](https://ref.ly/Exod13:14) כִּי יִשְׁאָֽלְ בִנְ מָחָר לֵאמֹר \= [Dt 6:20](https://ref.ly/Deut6:20), [Jos 4:6](https://ref.ly/Josh4:6), [4:21](https://ref.ly/Josh4:21), [22:24](https://ref.ly/Josh22:24), [22:27](https://ref.ly/Josh22:27), [22:28](https://ref.ly/Josh22:28); so בְּיוֺם מָחָר [Gn 30:33](https://ref.ly/Gen30:33).

> \[Str4283] †מָחֳרָת n.f. the morrow (the day following *a past* day)—abs. ´מ [Gn 19:34](https://ref.ly/Gen19:34) \+, cstr. מָחֳרַת [Lv 23:11](https://ref.ly/Lev23:11) \+;—*the morrow,* [Nu 11:32](https://ref.ly/Num11:32) וְכֹל יוֺם הַמָּחֳרָת; מִמָּחֳרָת \= *on the morrow*, so וַיְהִי מִמָּחֳרָת, [Gn 19:34](https://ref.ly/Gen19:34), [Ex 18:13](https://ref.ly/Exod18:13), [32:30](https://ref.ly/Exod32:30), [Nu 17:23](https://ref.ly/Num17:23), Ju [9:42](https://ref.ly/Judg9:42), [21:4](https://ref.ly/Judg21:4), [1 S 11:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:11), [18:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:10), [31:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam31:8) (\= [1 Ch 10:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr10:8)) [2 K 8:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:15), [Je 20:3](https://ref.ly/Jer20:3); (וַיַּשְׁכִּימוּ) וַיַּשְׁכֵּם מִמָּחֳרָת, [Ex 32:6](https://ref.ly/Exod32:6), Ju [6:38](https://ref.ly/Judg6:38), [1 S 5:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:3), [5:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:4); [Ex 9:6](https://ref.ly/Exod9:6) את ´ויעש י ´הדבר הזה ממ, [Nu 17:6](https://ref.ly/Num17:6), [Jos 5:12](https://ref.ly/Josh5:12); [2 S 11:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:12) b: מִמָּחֳרַת הַשַּׁבָּת *on the morrow of (\= after)* the sabbath, [Lv 23:11](https://ref.ly/Lev23:11), [23:15](https://ref.ly/Lev23:15), [23:16](https://ref.ly/Lev23:16), הַפֶּסַח ´ממ [Nu 33:3](https://ref.ly/Num33:3), [Jos 5:11](https://ref.ly/Josh5:11), מִמָּחֳרַת הַשֵּׁנִי \[בַּיּוֺם LXX] הַחֹדֶשׁ (MT falsely מִמָּחֳרָת) [1 S 20:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:27); of the morrow after a day specified in a law, [Lv 19:6](https://ref.ly/Lev19:6) בְּיוֺם זִבְחֲכֶם יֵאָכֵּל וּמִמָּחֳרָת, [7:16](https://ref.ly/Lev7:16). Twice (late) with לְ, [Jon 4:7](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:7) לַמָּחֳרָת *on the morrow*, [1 Ch 29:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:21) לְמָחֳרַת הַיּוֺם הַהוּא.—[1 S 30:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:17) strangely וַיַּכֵּם דָּוִד מֵהַנֶּשֶׁף וְעַד־הָעֶרֶב לְמָחֳרָתָם from the dawn to the evening *of their following day* (We Bu לְהַחֲרִימָם).

> \[Str4242] † I. מְחִיר **n.m.** price, hire (as that which is *placed in front* of one, *presented, offered;* As. *maḫîru, price; maḫirânu, buyer*, DlHWB 404, Prol.94\);—´מ abs. [Jb 26:5](https://ref.ly/Job26:5), [Psalm 136:6](https://ref.ly/Ps136:6). **f.** in clouds [Je 10:13](https://ref.ly/Jer10:13), [Jb 26:8](https://ref.ly/Job26:8), [Psalm 29:3](https://ref.ly/Ps29:3), [29:3](https://ref.ly/Ps29:3), [104:3](https://ref.ly/Ps104:3), [148:4](https://ref.ly/Ps148:4), etc. **g.** of rain Ju [5:4](https://ref.ly/Judg5:4), [2 S 21:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:10), [Dt 11:11](https://ref.ly/Deut11:11), [Psalm 65:10](https://ref.ly/Ps65:10), [Jb 5:10](https://ref.ly/Job5:10) (‖מָטָר), etc. **h.** of dew Ju [6:38](https://ref.ly/Judg6:38). **i.** primæval deep [Gn 1:2](https://ref.ly/Gen1:2), [1:6](https://ref.ly/Gen1:6) (P). **2\.** c. nomm. pr. loc. (q. v.), to denote springs, streams, or seas belonging to, near, such places: מֵימֵי מִצְרַיִם [Ex 7:19](https://ref.ly/Exod7:19), [8:2](https://ref.ly/Exod8:2); מֵי מְרִיבָה (v. supr. **1 a**); מֵי מֵרוֺם [Jos 11:5](https://ref.ly/Josh11:5), [11:7](https://ref.ly/Josh11:7); מֵי עֵין־שֶׁמֶשׁ [Jos 15:7](https://ref.ly/Josh15:7); מֵי נֶפְתּוֺחַ [15:9](https://ref.ly/Josh15:9), [18:15](https://ref.ly/Josh18:15); מֵי יְרִיחוֺ [16:1](https://ref.ly/Josh16:1); מֵי מְגִּדּוֺ Ju [5:19](https://ref.ly/Judg5:19); ם(י)מֵי נִמְרִ [Is 15:6](https://ref.ly/Isa15:6), [Je 48:34](https://ref.ly/Jer48:34); מֵי דִימוֺן [Is 15:9](https://ref.ly/Isa15:9); cf. מֵי הַשִּׁלֹּחַ [Is 8:6](https://ref.ly/Isa8:6); מֵימֵי גִּיחוֺן הָעֶלְיוֺן [2 Ch 32:30](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:30); cf. also n. pr. loc. מִשְרְפוֺת מַיִם [Jos 11:8](https://ref.ly/Josh11:8), [13:6](https://ref.ly/Josh13:6). **3\.** special uses are: מֵי רַגְלַיִם *water of the feet* \= urine [2 K 18:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:27) \= [Is 36:12](https://ref.ly/Isa36:12); cf. מַדְמֵנָה בְּמֵי [Is 25:10](https://ref.ly/Isa25:10) Kt *in water of a dunghill* (\> Qr בְּמוֺ); מַיִם לַחַץ *water* (which is) *distress*, because drunk in imprisonment [1 K 22:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:27) (‖לֶחֶם לַחַץ) \= [2 Ch 18:26](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:26); בְּמֵי־שָׁלֶג *in snow\-water* [Jb 9:30](https://ref.ly/Job9:30) Qr (Kt במו; Me Hoffm Siegfr כְּמוֺ), מֵימֵי שֶׁלֶג [24:19](https://ref.ly/Job24:19); מַיִם קְדשִׁים † [Nu 5:17](https://ref.ly/Num5:17) *holy water* (cf. RS Sem.165, 2nd ed.181, but text dubious, v. Di); מֵי הַמָּרִים *bitter water* †[Nu 5:18](https://ref.ly/Num5:18), [5:19](https://ref.ly/Num5:19), [5:23](https://ref.ly/Num5:23), [5:24](https://ref.ly/Num5:24); cf. [Ex 15:23](https://ref.ly/Exod15:23) (כִּי מָרִים הֵם), opp. וַיִּמְתְּקוּ הַמַּיִם v:[25](https://ref.ly/Exod15:25) *and the waters were sweetened;* מֵי חַטָּאת, מֵי נִדָּה v. supr. **1 a;** מַיִם חַיִּים *living* (i.e. running) *water* [Lv 14:5](https://ref.ly/Lev14:5), [14:6](https://ref.ly/Lev14:6), [14:50](https://ref.ly/Lev14:50), [14:51](https://ref.ly/Lev14:51), [14:52](https://ref.ly/Lev14:52), [Nu 19:17](https://ref.ly/Num19:17), also (fig.) [Zc 14:8](https://ref.ly/Zech14:8) (cf. Ez 47), and (fig.) [Je 2:13](https://ref.ly/Jer2:13), [17:13](https://ref.ly/Jer17:13). See also infr. (cf. [Nu 21:17](https://ref.ly/Num21:17), [21:18](https://ref.ly/Num21:18); v. RS Sem.i.127\.157; 2nd ed.135,176\). **4\.** among fig. uses are: **a.** of distress [Is 43:2](https://ref.ly/Isa43:2), [Psalm 66:12](https://ref.ly/Ps66:12) (‖אֵשׁ), [69:2](https://ref.ly/Ps69:2), [La 3:54](https://ref.ly/Lam3:54); usu. *many waters* [2 S 22:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:17) \= [Psalm 18:17](https://ref.ly/Ps18:17), cf. [Jb 22:11](https://ref.ly/Job22:11), [Psalm 32:6](https://ref.ly/Ps32:6), [144:7](https://ref.ly/Ps144:7), or *deep waters* [Psalm 69:3](https://ref.ly/Ps69:3), [69:15](https://ref.ly/Ps69:15), cf. v:[16](https://ref.ly/Ps69:16); also מֵי־ראֹשׁ *water of gall* [Je 8:14](https://ref.ly/Jer8:14), [9:14](https://ref.ly/Jer9:14), [23:15](https://ref.ly/Jer23:15). **b.** פֶּרֶץ מַיִם [2 S 5:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:20) \= [1 Ch 14:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr14:11) of outbursting force. **c.** מַיִם שְׁאוֺן [Is 17:12](https://ref.ly/Isa17:12), [17:13](https://ref.ly/Isa17:13) of rushing nations. **d.** of what is impetuous, violent, overwhelming [Is 28:2](https://ref.ly/Isa28:2), [28:17](https://ref.ly/Isa28:17), [Ho 10:7](https://ref.ly/Hos10:7), [Jb 27:20](https://ref.ly/Job27:20), [Psalm 124:4](https://ref.ly/Ps124:4), [124:5](https://ref.ly/Ps124:5), [88:18](https://ref.ly/Ps88:18); sim.of impetuousness פַּחַז כַּמַּיִ ם [Gn 49:4](https://ref.ly/Gen49:4). **e.** (as running away) of heart in timidity [Jos 7:5](https://ref.ly/Josh7:5), cf. [Psalm 22:16](https://ref.ly/Ps22:16), of knees Ez [7:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:17), [21:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:12). **f.** of transitoriness [Jb 11:16](https://ref.ly/Job11:16), [24:18](https://ref.ly/Job24:18), [Psalm 58:8](https://ref.ly/Ps58:8). **g.** of refreshment [Is 32:2](https://ref.ly/Isa32:2), [49:10](https://ref.ly/Isa49:10); spiritual [12:3](https://ref.ly/Isa12:3). **h.** מֵי מְנֻחוֺת [Psalm 23:2](https://ref.ly/Ps23:2) in fig. of rest and peace, cf. [Je 31:9](https://ref.ly/Jer31:9). **i.** of reckless bloodshed (poured out like water) [Psalm 79:3](https://ref.ly/Ps79:3); cf. pouring away (and not eating) blood [Dt 12:16](https://ref.ly/Deut12:16), [12:24](https://ref.ly/Deut12:24), [15:23](https://ref.ly/Deut15:23). **j.** בְּאֵר מַיִם חַיִּים Ct [4:15](https://ref.ly/Song4:15) fig. of bride; מַיִם מִבּוֺרְֶָ [Pr 5:15](https://ref.ly/Prov5:15) of enjoyment of one's own wife; of harlotry מַיִם גְּנוּבִים [Pr 9:17](https://ref.ly/Prov9:17) *stolen waters.* **k.** of outpoured wrath of ´י [Ho 5:10](https://ref.ly/Hos5:10). **1\.** of abundant justice [Am 5:24](https://ref.ly/Amos5:24) **m.** of outpoured feelings [Jb 3:24](https://ref.ly/Job3:24); שִׁפְכִי כַמַּיִם לִבֵח [La 2:19](https://ref.ly/Lam2:19).—[Is 48:1](https://ref.ly/Isa48:1) rd. prob. מִמְּעֵי for MT מִמֵּי, so Secker Du CheHpt Skinner, cf. Di.

> \[Str4314] †מֵי זָהָב **n.pr.m.** an Edomite [Gn 36:39](https://ref.ly/Gen36:39) (LXX Μεζοοβ, LXXL Μαιζοοβ) \= [1 Ch 1:50](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:50) (LXXL Μαιζααβ).
