## מִי (id: BDB05245)

\[Str4310] מִי:423 **pron. interrog.** who ? of persons, as מָה (q.v.) of things (Eth. ሚ፡ but only \= *What ?* and *How ?* As. *mî, me* (rare) JägerBAS. ii. 277, the usu. Sem. form for *Who ?* has n, viz. As. *mannu, manu* (DlHWB 419\), Eth. مَنُ: *manū*, Aram. ܡܰܢ Arab. مَنْ, vulg.–Arab. *min, mīn*, from a form resembling which, by rejection of *n*, prob. Heb. מִי: WSG 123 f.):—*who ?* (τίς; *quis ?*) [Gn 3:11](https://ref.ly/Gen3:11) הִגִּיד לְ מִי who told thee ? [24:65](https://ref.ly/Gen24:65) מִי הָאִישׁ הַלָּזֶה *who* is this man ? [27:18](https://ref.ly/Gen27:18) מי אתה בני *who* art thou, my son ? [Nu 22:9](https://ref.ly/Num22:9), Ju [1:1](https://ref.ly/Judg1:1), [6:29](https://ref.ly/Judg6:29), [15:6](https://ref.ly/Judg15:6), [18:3](https://ref.ly/Judg18:3), [20:18](https://ref.ly/Judg20:18), [2 S 1:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:8) \+ oft.; [Ru 3:9](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:9) מִי אָתְּ *who* art thou, (fem.) ? [Is 51:12](https://ref.ly/Isa51:12); of more than one, מִי אֵלָּה *who* are these ? [Gn 33:5](https://ref.ly/Gen33:5), [48:8](https://ref.ly/Gen48:8), [Is 60:8](https://ref.ly/Isa60:8); מי אתם [2 K 10:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:13); once, more explicitly, מִי זָמִי הַהֹלְכִים [Ex 10:8](https://ref.ly/Exod10:8); \= *as who ?* (i.e. in what condition, or capacity), [Am 7:2](https://ref.ly/Amos7:2) יעקב מי יקום v:[5](https://ref.ly/Amos7:5), [Is 51:19](https://ref.ly/Isa51:19) מִי אֲנַחֲמֵךְ (but Vrss Che Lag Brd Du ´מִי יְ, as ‖מִי יָנוּד), [Ru 3:16](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:16) (Be Ke Köiii. 388\). Note in partic.: **a.** מִי is rarely used of *things*, and usually where persons are understood or implied: [Gn 33:8](https://ref.ly/Gen33:8) מי לך כל־המחנה הזה *who* (or *what*) to thee is all this camp ? [Dt 4:7](https://ref.ly/Deut4:7), Ju [9:28](https://ref.ly/Judg9:28) שְׁכֶם מִי *who* is Shechem (i.e. the Shechemites) ? [Mi 1:5](https://ref.ly/Mic1:5) מִי־פֶשַׁע יַעֲקֹב הֲלאֹ שֹׁמְרוֺן, vb Ct [3:6](https://ref.ly/Song3:6), Ju [13:17](https://ref.ly/Judg13:17) מִי שְׁמְֶָ ( but מה [Gn 32:28](https://ref.ly/Gen32:28), [Ex 3:13](https://ref.ly/Exod3:13): with מי cf. מַן in Aram. [Ezr 5:4](https://ref.ly/Ezra5:4) Gn Ex.*ll*. cc. Targum; and see NöM. p. 341\). On [1 S 18:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:18), v. II. חַי **b.** in the gen., בַּת־מִי אַתְּ *whose* daughter art thou ? [Gn 24:23](https://ref.ly/Gen24:23), [24:47](https://ref.ly/Gen24:47), [1 S 12:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:3), [12:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:3), [12:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:3), [17:55](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:55), [Je 44:28](https://ref.ly/Jer44:28), [Jb 26:4](https://ref.ly/Job26:4), [33:28](https://ref.ly/Job33:28); after various preps., as אַחֲרֵי מִי [1 S 24:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:15), [24:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:15); אֶל־מִי [1 S 6:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:20), [2 K 9:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:5) \+; בְּמִי by *whom ?* † [1 K 20:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:14), לְמִי:20 *to whom ? whose ?* [Gn 32:18](https://ref.ly/Gen32:18) לְמִי אַתָּה, [38:25](https://ref.ly/Gen38:25), [Pr 20:20](https://ref.ly/Prov20:20) (6 t.) \+; מִמִּי † Ez [32:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:19), [Psalm 27:1](https://ref.ly/Ps27:1), [27:1](https://ref.ly/Ps27:1); עַל־מִי [2 K 18:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:20), [19:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:22), [Is 57:4](https://ref.ly/Isa57:4) \+; אֶת־מִי (so alw. in acc.), [1 S 12:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:3), [12:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:3), [28:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam28:11), [Is 6:8](https://ref.ly/Isa6:8); \= *with whom ?* [Jb 12:3](https://ref.ly/Job12:3). **c.** in an indirect qu., as [Gn 21:26](https://ref.ly/Gen21:26), [43:22](https://ref.ly/Gen43:22) לא ידענו מי שם we do not know *who put*, etc., [Dt 21:1](https://ref.ly/Deut21:1), [Psalm 39:7](https://ref.ly/Ps39:7), [Jos 24:15](https://ref.ly/Josh24:15) אֶת־מִי תַעֲבֹדָוּן …בַּחֲרוּ choose *whom* ye will serve, after ראה [1 S 14:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:17), שׁאל [17:56](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:56), הגיד [1 K 1:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:20), הודיע v:[27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:27). **d.** ´מי ב *who among ... ?* [1 S 22:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:14), [Is 36:20](https://ref.ly/Isa36:20); [43:9](https://ref.ly/Isa43:9), [48:14](https://ref.ly/Isa48:14) (בהם) [42:23](https://ref.ly/Isa42:23), [50:10](https://ref.ly/Isa50:10), [Hg 2:3](https://ref.ly/Hag2:3) ( all בכם); מי מן *who of ... ?* Ju [21:8](https://ref.ly/Judg21:8), [2 K 6:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:11), [1 S 50:1](https://ref.ly/Isa50:1). **e.** strengthened and emph. forms of interrog.: (*a*) מי זה (v. זה **4 b**), מי הוא (v. הוא **4 b** β), הוא זה מי † [Psalm 24:10](https://ref.ly/Ps24:10), [Je 30:21](https://ref.ly/Jer30:21), [Est 7:5](https://ref.ly/Esth7:5) (v. *ibid.*); (*b*) אֶחָד מִי *what single one ... ?* Ju [21:8](https://ref.ly/Judg21:8), cf. [2 S 7:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:23) ( \= [1 Ch 17:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:21)). **f.** various *rhetorical* uses ( oft. *repeated* in ‖ cl., as [Psalm 15:1](https://ref.ly/Ps15:1), [18:32](https://ref.ly/Ps18:32), [Is 28:9](https://ref.ly/Isa28:9), [29:15](https://ref.ly/Isa29:15)):—*(a)* sq. impf. it expresses a *wish* ( the question implying a desire that the person asked for were present ), [2 S 15:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:4) מִי יְשִמֵנִי שֹׁפֵט *who* will set me judge ? i.e. *would that* some one would make me judge ! [23:15](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:15) מִי יַשְׁקֵנִי מַיִם \= *O that* one would give me to drink, etc. ! cf. [Nu 11:4](https://ref.ly/Num11:4), [11:18](https://ref.ly/Num11:18), [Mal 1:10](https://ref.ly/Mal1:10), [Psalm 4:7](https://ref.ly/Ps4:7), [60:11](https://ref.ly/Ps60:11), [94:16](https://ref.ly/Ps94:16), [Is 42:23](https://ref.ly/Isa42:23), and very oft. in the phr. מִי יִתֵּן *who will give?* i.e. *would that there were… !* as [Nu 11:29](https://ref.ly/Num11:29) נביאים ´ומי יתן כל עם י \= and *would that* all ´י's people were prophets! [Dt 28:67](https://ref.ly/Deut28:67) מי יתן ערב \= *would it were* even! Ju [9:29](https://ref.ly/Judg9:29) \+ (v. נָתַן). *(b)* it expresses *contempt (who ?* implying the answer *no one at all*), [Ex 5:2](https://ref.ly/Exod5:2) ´אשׁר אשׁמע בקולו מי י *who* is ´י that I should listen to his voice ? Ju [9:28](https://ref.ly/Judg9:28) מִי אֲבִימֶלֶךְ וּמִי שְׁכֶם כִּי נַעַבְדֶנּוּ, v:[38](https://ref.ly/Judg9:38), [1 S 17:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:26) *who* is this Phil. that he should have reproached, etc.? [25:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:10) מי דוד ומי בן ישׁי [Is 28:9](https://ref.ly/Isa28:9), [Jb 26:4](https://ref.ly/Job26:4), [Pr 30:9](https://ref.ly/Prov30:9) ´מי י; or *modesty* (real or assumed), [Ex 3:11](https://ref.ly/Exod3:11) מִי אָנֹכִי כִּי אֵלֵךְ אֶל־פַּרְעֹה [1 S 18:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:18) ומי חיי מי אנכי who am I, and who is my clan (חַיִּי), that I should be the king's son\-in\-law ? [2 S 7:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:18), [1 Ch 29:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:14), [2 Ch 2:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:5); or *surprise*, [Is 44:10](https://ref.ly/Isa44:10), [49:21](https://ref.ly/Isa49:21). *(c)* in impassioned prose, and esp. in poetry, implying the answer *few* or *none*, it is equiv. to a rhetorical negative, [Dt 9:2](https://ref.ly/Deut9:2) מִי יִתְיַצֵּב לִפְנֵי בְנֵי עֲנָק *who* can stand, etc.? [30:12](https://ref.ly/Deut30:12) מִי יַעֲלֶה־לָּנוּ הַשָּׁמַיְמָה v:[13](https://ref.ly/Deut30:13), [1 S 4:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:8) ´מִי יַצִּילֵנוּ וג, [6:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:20), [22:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:14), [26:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:9), [26:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:15), [2 S 16:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:10); מי יאמר *who* shall (or can) say ? [Jb 9:12](https://ref.ly/Job9:12), [Pr 20:9](https://ref.ly/Prov20:9), [Ec 8:4](https://ref.ly/Eccl8:4); with the perfect (of experience), *who has ever…?* [Nu 23:10](https://ref.ly/Num23:10), [Dt 5:23](https://ref.ly/Deut5:23), [Is 36:20](https://ref.ly/Isa36:20), [66:8](https://ref.ly/Isa66:8), [66:8](https://ref.ly/Isa66:8), [Je 18:13](https://ref.ly/Jer18:13) מִי שָׁמַע כָּאֵלֶּה, [23:18](https://ref.ly/Jer23:18) (MT), [30:21](https://ref.ly/Jer30:21), [Jb 4:7](https://ref.ly/Job4:7), [9:4](https://ref.ly/Job9:4) מִי הִקְשָׁה אֵלָיו וַיִּשְׁלָם, [41:3](https://ref.ly/Job41:3), [Pr 30:4](https://ref.ly/Prov30:4), [La 3:37](https://ref.ly/Lam3:37); poet., very oft., as [Gn 49:9](https://ref.ly/Gen49:9) מִי יְקִימֶנּוּ וּכְלָבִיא *who* can rouse him up ? (i.e. no one), [Ex 15:11](https://ref.ly/Exod15:11), [15:11](https://ref.ly/Exod15:11) מִי כָמוְֺ (so [Psalm 35:10](https://ref.ly/Ps35:10) al.), [Nu 24:9](https://ref.ly/Num24:9), [24:23](https://ref.ly/Num24:23), [1 S 2:25](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:25) מי יתפלל לו *who* shall intercede for him ? (i.e. no one), [Is 1:12](https://ref.ly/Isa1:12), [10:3](https://ref.ly/Isa10:3), [40:13](https://ref.ly/Isa40:13), [40:14](https://ref.ly/Isa40:14), [40:18](https://ref.ly/Isa40:18), [41:26](https://ref.ly/Isa41:26), [42:19](https://ref.ly/Isa42:19), [43:9](https://ref.ly/Isa43:9), [48:14](https://ref.ly/Isa48:14), [50:1](https://ref.ly/Isa50:1), [53:1](https://ref.ly/Isa53:1) מִי הֶאֱמִין לִשְׁמֻעָתֵנוּ (i.e. few or none), etc.; [Psalm 18:32](https://ref.ly/Ps18:32) ´מִי אֱלוֺהַּ מִבַּלְעֲדֵי י, [76:8](https://ref.ly/Ps76:8), [89:7](https://ref.ly/Ps89:7), [130:3](https://ref.ly/Ps130:3), [Jb 21:31](https://ref.ly/Job21:31), [34:29](https://ref.ly/Job34:29), [Je 17:9](https://ref.ly/Jer17:9), [Jo 2:11](https://ref.ly/Joel2:11), [Mal 3:2](https://ref.ly/Mal3:2); …מִי לאֹ [Am 3:8](https://ref.ly/Amos3:8), [Je 10:7](https://ref.ly/Jer10:7), [Jb 12:9](https://ref.ly/Job12:9) (cf. [25:3](https://ref.ly/Job25:3), [Na 3:19](https://ref.ly/Nah3:19)); (יְשִׁיבֶנָּה) מִי יְשִׁיבֶנּוּ  *who* shall (can) turn it back ? † [Jb 9:12](https://ref.ly/Job9:12), [11:10](https://ref.ly/Job11:10), [23:13](https://ref.ly/Job23:13), [Is 14:27](https://ref.ly/Isa14:27), [43:13](https://ref.ly/Isa43:13), [Je 2:24](https://ref.ly/Jer2:24); implying the answer, *no one but God*, [Is 40:12](https://ref.ly/Isa40:12), [41:2](https://ref.ly/Isa41:2), [Jb 38:5](https://ref.ly/Job38:5), [38:6](https://ref.ly/Job38:6), [38:25](https://ref.ly/Job38:25), etc.; defiantly, [Is 50:9](https://ref.ly/Isa50:9) מִי הוּא יַרְשִׁיעֵנִי, [Je 21:13](https://ref.ly/Jer21:13), [49:4](https://ref.ly/Jer49:4) מִי יָבוֺא אֵלַי, v:[19](https://ref.ly/Jer49:19), Ob:[3](https://ref.ly/Obad1:3), [Psalm 12:5](https://ref.ly/Ps12:5) מִי אָדוֺן לָנוּ, [59:8](https://ref.ly/Ps59:8), [64:6](https://ref.ly/Ps64:6), [Jb 9:19](https://ref.ly/Job9:19) מִי יוֺעִידֵנִי וְאִם לְמִשְׁפָּט, [13:19](https://ref.ly/Job13:19) מִי הוּא יָרִיב עִמָּדִי *who* is he that will contend with me ? [17:3](https://ref.ly/Job17:3), [41:2](https://ref.ly/Job41:2). Notice in examples of this kind the freq. *order* of words: [Pr 20:6](https://ref.ly/Prov20:6) ואישׁ אמוּנים מי ימצא, [24:22](https://ref.ly/Prov24:22) יודע ופיד שׁניהם מי, [31:10](https://ref.ly/Prov31:10), [Na 1:6](https://ref.ly/Nah1:6) לפני זעמו מי יעמד, [Psalm 147:17](https://ref.ly/Ps147:17) קֹרָתוֺ מי יעמד לפני, [Jb 4:2](https://ref.ly/Job4:2) וַעְצֹר בְּמִלִּין מִי יוּכָל, [26:14](https://ref.ly/Job26:14), [38:37](https://ref.ly/Job38:37), [39:5](https://ref.ly/Job39:5), [41:5](https://ref.ly/Job41:5), [41:6](https://ref.ly/Job41:6), [Psalm 6:6](https://ref.ly/Ps6:6) בִּשְׁאוֺל מִי יוֺדֶה־לָּךְ, [19:13](https://ref.ly/Ps19:13); and with the *nom. pendens*, [Je 2:24](https://ref.ly/Jer2:24) תַּאֲנָתָהּ מִי יְשִׁיבֶנָּה, [Pr 18:14](https://ref.ly/Prov18:14), [Jb 17:15](https://ref.ly/Job17:15) וְתִקְוָתִי מִי יְשׁוּרֶנָּה, [38:29](https://ref.ly/Job38:29) וּכְפֹר שָׁמַיִם מִי יְלָדוֺ. (*d*) מִי יוֺדֵעַ *who knoweth ?* ([Psalm 90:11](https://ref.ly/Ps90:11), [Pr 24:22](https://ref.ly/Prov24:22)), sq. a verbal clause, becomes (cf. *nescio an*) \=  *it may be, perchance*, † [2 S 12:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:22) ´מִי יוֺדֵעַ יְחָנֵּנִי י (Qr וְחַנַּנִי), [Jo 2:14](https://ref.ly/Joel2:14) ( \= [Jon 3:9](https://ref.ly/Jonah3:9)) מי יודע ישׁוב ונחם, [Est 4:14](https://ref.ly/Esth4:14) יודע אם מי. (*e*) especially in poetry, a question with מי, to which the answer follows, is an effective mode of affirming a fact, or introducing a description: [Psalm 15:1](https://ref.ly/Ps15:1); [15:1](https://ref.ly/Ps15:1) (see v:[2](https://ref.ly/Ps15:2)–[5](https://ref.ly/Ps15:5)), [24:3](https://ref.ly/Ps24:3), [24:8](https://ref.ly/Ps24:8), [24:10](https://ref.ly/Ps24:10), [Is 23:8](https://ref.ly/Isa23:8) מי יעץ ´זאת וג (v:[9](https://ref.ly/Isa23:9) the answ., יְעָצָהּ ´ח ´י), [33:14](https://ref.ly/Isa33:14) (see v:[15](https://ref.ly/Isa33:15) f.), [37:23](https://ref.ly/Isa37:23), [41:2](https://ref.ly/Isa41:2), [41:4](https://ref.ly/Isa41:4), [60:8](https://ref.ly/Isa60:8), [63:1](https://ref.ly/Isa63:1), [Je 46:7](https://ref.ly/Jer46:7), Ct [3:6](https://ref.ly/Song3:6), cf. [6:10](https://ref.ly/Song6:10), [8:3](https://ref.ly/Song8:3); answered by הֲלאֹ [Ex 4:11](https://ref.ly/Exod4:11), [Is 42:24](https://ref.ly/Isa42:24), [45:21](https://ref.ly/Isa45:21), [Mi 1:5](https://ref.ly/Mic1:5), [1:5](https://ref.ly/Mic1:5). **g.** מִי may sometimes be rendered  *whosoever*, though, as the examples will shew, it does not really mean it: [Ex 24:14](https://ref.ly/Exod24:14) מי בעל דברים יִגַּשׁ אליהם, lit. *Who* hath a cause ? let him draw nigh unto them, i.e. *whoso* hath a cause, let him, etc., [Is 50:8b](https://ref.ly/Isa50:8) מִי בַעַל מִשְׁפָּטִי יִגַּשׁ אֵלַי, [54:15](https://ref.ly/Isa54:15), Ju [7:3](https://ref.ly/Judg7:3) מִי יָרֵא וְחָרֵד יָשֹׁב ´וג *who* is fearful and trembling ? let him return, [Pr 9:4](https://ref.ly/Prov9:4), [9:16](https://ref.ly/Prov9:16) מִי־פֶ֭תִי יָסֻר הֵגָּה, Ezr [1:3](https://ref.ly/Ezra1:3) (cf. with וְ in apod. [Je 9:11](https://ref.ly/Jer9:11), [Ho 14:10](https://ref.ly/Hos14:10), [Psalm 107:43](https://ref.ly/Ps107:43); [Zc 4:10](https://ref.ly/Zech4:10) is dubious, on account of anom. tense and construct.); so with …מי האישׁ אשׁר [Dt 20:5](https://ref.ly/Deut20:5), [20:6](https://ref.ly/Deut20:6), [20:7](https://ref.ly/Deut20:7), Ju [10:18](https://ref.ly/Judg10:18), cf. [Psalm 25:12](https://ref.ly/Ps25:12). With 1 ps. in apod., [Ex 32:33](https://ref.ly/Exod32:33) חָטָא לִי אֶמְחֶגּוּ מִסִּפְרִי מִי אֲשֶׁר, [Je 49:19](https://ref.ly/Jer49:19) ( \= [50:44](https://ref.ly/Jer50:44)) אֵלָיהָ אֶפְקֹד וּמִי בָחוּר and *who* is chosen ? I will appoint him over her! [Is 50:8a](https://ref.ly/Isa50:8); with an imv. in the apod. [Ex 32:24](https://ref.ly/Exod32:24) (against accents; v. Ra) התפרקו למי זהב *who* hath gold ? break it off you! [Gn 19:12](https://ref.ly/Gen19:12), [1 S 11:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:12), cf. [Psalm 34:13f](https://ref.ly/Ps34:13).; without a verb, [Ex 32:26](https://ref.ly/Exod32:26) אלי ´מי לי *who* is on J.'s side ? (let him come) to me! so [2 S 20:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:11) (מי אשׁר); [Ec 5:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:9), [9:4](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:4) (מי אשׁר). **h.** once, following a verb (cf. מָה **3**), *any one,* [2 S 18:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:12) שִׁמְרוּ־מִי בַּנַּעַר באבשׁלום Have a care, *whosoever ye be*, of, etc. (but LXX SyrVer Vulgate Bu לִי; cf. v:[5](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:5)).

> \[Str4317] †מִיכָאֵל **n.pr.m.** Michael, LXX Μειχαηλ, Μιχαηλ (*Who is like God ?* cf. מִיכָיָהוּ, and As. proper names, as *Mannu\-kî\-Rammân,* ‘Who is like Rammân ?’ *Mannu\-kî\-ilu\-rabu,* ‘Who is like the great God ?’ DlProl. 210: HWB 419; SchrCOT 478; cf. CookAram. Gloss. 74\) :—**1\.** [Nu 13:13](https://ref.ly/Num13:13) (P). **2\.** [1 Ch 5:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:13) **3\.** [5:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:14). **4\.** [6:25](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:25). **5\.** [7:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:3). **6\.** [8:16](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:16). **7\.** [12:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:21). **8\.** [27:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:18). **9\.** [2 Ch 21:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr21:2). **10\.** [Ezr 8:8](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:8). **11\.** the ‘prince,’ or patron\-angel, of Israel, [Dn 10:13](https://ref.ly/Dan10:13), [10:21](https://ref.ly/Dan10:21), [12:1](https://ref.ly/Dan12:1) (cf. Jude:9, [Rev 12:7](https://ref.ly/Rev12:7); LevyNHWB iii.100\).

> \[Str4322] †מִיכָיָ֫הוּ **n.pr.** (*Who is like Yah?* cf. מִיכָאֵל):—**1\. m.** a prince under Jehosh. [2 Ch 17:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr17:7). **2\. f.** [2 Ch 13:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:2) f. l. for מִעֲכָה **2 b,** q. v. Of this name, the following are abbreviated, or softened, forms —

> \[Str4321,4319] †מִיכָֽיְהוּ **n.pr.m.** (so on an Isr. scarab, GanneauJAS 1883, 156, No. 42\)—**1\.** an Ephraimite, Ju [17:1](https://ref.ly/Judg17:1), [17:4](https://ref.ly/Judg17:4) (in [17:5](https://ref.ly/Judg17:5) ff. [18:2](https://ref.ly/Judg18:2) ff called מִיכָה). **2\.** a prophet, the son of Imlah (LXX Μειχαίας), [1 K 22:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:8)–[26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:26) (9 t.), ‖ [2 Ch 18:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:7)–[25](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:25) (7 t.) \+ v:[8](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:8) Qr (Kt מיכהו). **3\.** a contemp. of Jer., [Je 36:11](https://ref.ly/Jer36:11) (מִכָֽיְהוּ), v:[13](https://ref.ly/Jer36:13).

> \[Str4320] †מִיכָיָה **n.pr.m. 1\.** the canon. prophet, Micah, [Je 26:18](https://ref.ly/Jer26:18) Kt (Qr מִיכָה, as [Mi 1:1](https://ref.ly/Mic1:1)). **2\.** [Ne 12:35](https://ref.ly/Neh12:35) (called מִיכָא [11:17](https://ref.ly/Neh11:17), [11:22](https://ref.ly/Neh11:22)). **3\.** [Ne 12:41](https://ref.ly/Neh12:41) **4\.** [2 K 22:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:12) (‖ [2 Ch 34:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:20) מִיכָה).

> \[Str4318] †מִיכָה **n.pr.m. 1\.** Micha, the Ephraimite (LXX Μειχαίας), Ju [17:5](https://ref.ly/Judg17:5), [17:8](https://ref.ly/Judg17:8), [17:9](https://ref.ly/Judg17:9), [17:10](https://ref.ly/Judg17:10), [17:12](https://ref.ly/Judg17:12), [17:12](https://ref.ly/Judg17:12), [17:13](https://ref.ly/Judg17:13), [18:2](https://ref.ly/Judg18:2) ff. (12 t.), called מִיכָֽיְהוּ [17:1](https://ref.ly/Judg17:1), [17:4](https://ref.ly/Judg17:4). **2\.** the prophet Micah (LXX Μειχαίας), [Mi 1:1](https://ref.ly/Mic1:1) ([Je 26:18](https://ref.ly/Jer26:18) Kt מִיכָיָה). **3\.** [1 Ch 5:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:5). **4\.** son of Meribbaal (Mephibosheth) [8:34f](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:34). (LXX Μιχια), [9:40f](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:40). (LXX Μειχα), called מִיכָא [2 S 9:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam9:12). **5\.** [23:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:20) (LXX Μειχας), [24:24](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:24), [24:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:25) (LXX Μειχα). **6\.** [2 Ch 18:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:14) ( \= מִיכָֽיְהוּ **2**). **7\.** [34:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:20) (‖ [2 K 22:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:12) מִיכָיָה).

> \[Str4316] †מִיכָא **n.pr.m.** (so in Palm., VogNo. 36a). **1\.** son of Mephibosheth (LXX Μειχα), [2 S 9:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam9:12) ( \= מִיכָה **4**) **2\.** [Ne 10:2](https://ref.ly/Neh10:2). **3\.** [Ne 11:7](https://ref.ly/Neh11:7) ( \= [1 Ch 9:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:15)), v:[22](https://ref.ly/Neh11:22) (called מִיכָיָה [12:35](https://ref.ly/Neh12:35)).

> \[Str4332] †מִישָׁאֵל **n.pr.m.** (*Who is what God is ?* Ass. form., cf. מְתוּשָׁאֵל: note also in Thothmes’ list of towns Bai\-ti\-š\-’\-ra,i.e prob. בֵּיתְשָׁאֵל, Heb. בֵּיתְאֵל WMMAs. Eur.193\):—**1\.** a cousin of Moses [Ex 6:22](https://ref.ly/Exod6:22), [Lv 10:4](https://ref.ly/Lev10:4) (P). **2\.** one of Daniel's companions [Dn 1:5](https://ref.ly/Dan1:5), [1:7](https://ref.ly/Dan1:7), [1:11](https://ref.ly/Dan1:11), [1:19](https://ref.ly/Dan1:19) \+ [2:17](https://ref.ly/Dan2:17) (Aram.), elsewhere in c. 2–3 called מֵישַׁח ([1:7](https://ref.ly/Dan1:7)). **3\.** [Ne 8:4](https://ref.ly/Neh8:4).

> \[Str4311] †מֵידְבָא **n.pr.loc** city in Moab (MI8 מהדבה)—[Nu 21:30](https://ref.ly/Num21:30), [Is 15:2](https://ref.ly/Isa15:2); [Jos 13:9](https://ref.ly/Josh13:9), [13:16](https://ref.ly/Josh13:16) assigned to Reuben (LXX Δαιδαβαν, Μαιδαβα LXXL Μεδαβα); [1 Ch 19:7](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:7) meeting\-place of Ammonites (LXX Μαιθαβα, A LXXL Μηδαβα).

> \[Str4317] מִיכָאֵל v. מִי

> \[Str4323] † \[מִיכָל] **n.**\[**m.**] brook, stream (?) (dubious word, cf. Drad loc. HPS), cstr. מִיכַל הַמָּיִם [2 S 17:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:20).

> \[Str4324] † מִיכַל **n.pr.f.** (acc. to Ol:§ 277 f., contr. from מִיכָאֵל):—younger daughter of Saul, wife of David (LXX Μελχολ), [1 S 14:49](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:49), [18:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:20), [18:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:27), [18:28](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:28), [19:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:11), [19:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:12), [19:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:13), [19:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:17), [19:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:17), [25:44](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:44), [2 S 3:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:13), [3:14](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:14), [6:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:16) \= [1 Ch 15:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:29), [2 S 6:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:20), [6:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:21), [6:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:23); also [21:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:8) (LXX Μιχολ), but err. for מֵרַב (LXXL Μεροβ).

> \[Str4325] מַ֫יִם v. מַי

> \[Str4326,4509] † מִיָּמִן, מִיָּמִין, מִנְיָמִן, מִנְיָמִין **n.pr.m. 1\.** priestly name: **a.** מִיָּמִן assigned to David's time, [1 Ch 24:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:9). **b.** Neh.'s time [Ne 10:8](https://ref.ly/Neh10:8) \= מִנְיָמִין [12:17](https://ref.ly/Neh12:17), [12:41](https://ref.ly/Neh12:41). **c.** מִנְיָמִן assigned to Hezekiah's time [2 Ch 31:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:15). **d.** מִיָּמִין went up with Zerub. [Ne 12:5](https://ref.ly/Neh12:5). **2\.** one of those who took strange wives [Ezr 10:25](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:25).—LXX Βενιαμειν and (esp. A LXXL) Μιαμειν.

## מין (id: BDB05264)

מין, מון (√ of foll.; dubious; Fl in LevyNHWB iii. 310, comp. Ar. مَانَ (med. *i*) *split* the earth (in ploughing), cf. Dr:[Dt 14:13](https://ref.ly/Deut14:13)).

568:a. \[מִין], del. ‘so Syr. (Edessa) ܡܝܢܐ,’ ref. due to early text. err. (GFM, privately, Dec. 28, 1899\).

> \[Str4327] † \[מִין] **n.**\[**m.**] kind, species (NH *id.* ([Ecclus 43:25](https://ref.ly/Sir43:25) *id.*), also *schismatic, heretic;* Jewish\-Aram. מִינָא, *species*, so Syr. (Edessa) ܡܝܢܐ; Palest. Syr. \= *nation* PS2094 SchwallyIdioticon 50; yet not Aram. loan\-wd. in Heb.; v. LagBN 183 f.);—alw. sf. \+ prep. לְמִינֵהוּ [Gn 1:12](https://ref.ly/Gen1:12), [1:12](https://ref.ly/Gen1:12) \+ 2 t.; לְמִינוֺ [Gn 1:11](https://ref.ly/Gen1:11) (om. LXX) \+ 3 t.; לְמִינָהּ [Gn 1:24](https://ref.ly/Gen1:24), [1:24](https://ref.ly/Gen1:24) \+ 9 t.; לְמִינָהַ Ez [47:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:10) (del. Co); לְמִינֵהֶם [Gn 1:21](https://ref.ly/Gen1:21); —*kind, species*, of plant [Gn 1:11](https://ref.ly/Gen1:11), [1:12](https://ref.ly/Gen1:12), [1:12](https://ref.ly/Gen1:12); usu. of animal (beast, bird, fish, insect) [Gn 1:21](https://ref.ly/Gen1:21), [1:22](https://ref.ly/Gen1:22), [1:24](https://ref.ly/Gen1:24), [1:24](https://ref.ly/Gen1:24), [1:25](https://ref.ly/Gen1:25), [1:25](https://ref.ly/Gen1:25), [1:25](https://ref.ly/Gen1:25), [6:20](https://ref.ly/Gen6:20), [6:20](https://ref.ly/Gen6:20), [6:20](https://ref.ly/Gen6:20), [7:14](https://ref.ly/Gen7:14), [7:14](https://ref.ly/Gen7:14), [7:14](https://ref.ly/Gen7:14), [7:14](https://ref.ly/Gen7:14), [Lv 11:14](https://ref.ly/Lev11:14), [11:15](https://ref.ly/Lev11:15), [11:16](https://ref.ly/Lev11:16), [11:19](https://ref.ly/Lev11:19), [11:22](https://ref.ly/Lev11:22), [11:22](https://ref.ly/Lev11:22), [11:22](https://ref.ly/Lev11:22), [11:22](https://ref.ly/Lev11:22), [11:29](https://ref.ly/Lev11:29) (all P); also [Dt 14:13](https://ref.ly/Deut14:13), [14:14](https://ref.ly/Deut14:14), [14:15](https://ref.ly/Deut14:15), [14:18](https://ref.ly/Deut14:18) (cf. ## מיץ (id: BDB05270)

† \[מיץ] (√ foll. \= *press, squeeze*, cf. NH *id., press, suck;* Ar. مَاصَ (med. ו) is *wash gently, rub* with the hand).

> \[Str4160] † מֵץ **n.m.** squeezer, i.e. extortioner, oppressor, only הַמֵּץ [Is 16:4](https://ref.ly/Isa16:4).

> \[Str4330] † מִיץ **n.m.** squeezing, pressing, wringing (3 t.) ־אַף יוֺצִיא דָם´מִיץ חָלָב יוֺצִיא חֶמְאָה וּמ :אַפַּיִם יוֺצִיא רִיב ´וּמ [Pr 30:33](https://ref.ly/Prov30:33) *the squeezing of milk produceth curd* (v. ThomsonLand and Book, Centr. Pal. 456 RobBR i. 485; ii. 418\), *and the squeezing of the nose produceth blood*, and (so) *the squeezing of anger* (i.e. pressure, insistence) *produceth strife*.

> \[Str4335] † מֵישַׁךְ **n.pr.m.** (acc. to DlBaer, Dan. xi for *Mîsha\-aku* ‘ who is what Aku (the moon\-god) is ? ’ cf. מִישָׁאֵל, p. 567\);—the new (Bab.) name given to Mishael, [Dn 1:7](https://ref.ly/Dan1:7) (\+ Aram. [2:49](https://ref.ly/Dan2:49) and c. 3).

> 571:b. מִלּוֺא: SchwallyZMG lii (1898\), 137 cp. As. *mulu* \= *earthwork, mound, terrace,* D1411 M\-A544.

> \[Str4407,1037] † מִלּוֺא **n.pr.loc.** (Thes *‘ fill ’* of earth, earthwork, but v. GFM: Ju [9:6](https://ref.ly/Judg9:6));—**1\.** בֵּית מִלּוֺא Ju [9:6](https://ref.ly/Judg9:6) (‖בַּעֲלֵי שְׁכֶם) so v:[20](https://ref.ly/Judg9:20), v:[20](https://ref.ly/Judg9:20); near Shechem, site unknown; LXX ὀ͂κος Βηθμααλων. **2\.** citadel in Jerus. הַמִּלּוֺא [2 S 5:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:9), [1 K 9:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:15), [9:24](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:24), [11:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:27), [1 Ch 11:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:8), [2 Ch 32:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:5). LXX usu. ἡ ἄκρα (A [1 K 9:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:15), [9:24](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:24) also Μελω). **3\.** בֵּית מִלּאֹ [2 K 12:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:21) (\+ הַיֹּרֵד סִלָּא) dub.; \= **2\.** ? LXX ὄκῳ Μααλω.

> \[Str4402] † מִלֵּאת **n.f.** dub.: only ´ישְׁבוֺת עַל־מ Ct [5:12](https://ref.ly/Song5:12) of lover's eyes; AE De BaeKau setting, perh. better border, rim, Öt Wild *fulness, abundance*, of surrounding of eye.

> \[Str3229] † יִמְלָה, יִמְלָא **n.pr.m.** father of prophet Micaiah of Isr.:—יִמְלָה [1 K 22:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:8), [22:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:9) LXX Ιεμια, LXXL Ναμαλι; \= יִמְלָא [2 Ch 18:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:7), [18:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:8) LXX Ιεμαα, A Ιεμλα, LXXL Ναμαλι.

## I מָלַח (id: BDB05325)

\[Str4414] † I. \[מָלַח] **vb.** tear away, fig. dissipate (cf. Ar. مَلَخَ *pull out* eye, tooth, \&c.; esp. Dozy *tear off branch* to plant it, \&c. so Eth. መልኀ፡ *evellere*);—**Niph.** fig. *be dispersed in fragments, dissipated, Pf.* שָׁמַיִם כֶּעָשָׁן נִמְלָחוּ [Is 51:6](https://ref.ly/Isa51:6).

> \[Str4418] † I. \[מֶ֫לַח or מָלָח] **n.**\[**m.**] rag; only pl. מְלָחִים [Je 38:11](https://ref.ly/Jer38:11), [38:12](https://ref.ly/Jer38:12) (‖סְחָבוֺת).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 51:6; Jeremiah 38:12; Jeremiah 38:11


## II מלח (id: BDB05327)

II. מלח (√ of foll.; meaning dubious).

> \[Str4417] † II. מֶ֫לַח **n.m.** salt (NH  *id.;* Ar. مِلْحٌ; Aram. ܡܶܠܚܳܐ, מִלְחָא);—´מ abs. [Gn 19:26](https://ref.ly/Gen19:26) \+; cstr. [Lv 2:13](https://ref.ly/Lev2:13); —*salt* for seasoning food [Jb 6:6](https://ref.ly/Job6:6); offering [Lv 2:13](https://ref.ly/Lev2:13), [2:13](https://ref.ly/Lev2:13), [2:13](https://ref.ly/Lev2:13) cf. Ez [43:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:24); בְּרִית מֶ֫לַח [Nu 18:19](https://ref.ly/Num18:19), [2 Ch 13:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:5) (cf. [Lv 2:13](https://ref.ly/Lev2:13) v. Di); used in purifying waters [2 K 2:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:20), [2:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:21); strewn on site of devoted city Ju [9:45](https://ref.ly/Judg9:45) cf. [Zp 2:9](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:9) (´מִכְרֵה־מ) [Dt 29:22](https://ref.ly/Deut29:22) (‖גָּפְרִית); produced in marshes reserved for purpose Ez [47:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:11); pillar of salt ´נְצִיב מ [Gn 19:26](https://ref.ly/Gen19:26); usu. in combin. הַמֶּלַח (־) יָם [Gn 14:3](https://ref.ly/Gen14:3) (עֶ֫מֶק הַשִּדִּים), [Nu 34:3](https://ref.ly/Num34:3), [34:12](https://ref.ly/Num34:12), [Dt 3:17](https://ref.ly/Deut3:17) (‖יָם הָעֲתָבָה), [Jos 3:16](https://ref.ly/Josh3:16), [12:3](https://ref.ly/Josh12:3), [15:2](https://ref.ly/Josh15:2), [15:5](https://ref.ly/Josh15:5), [18:19](https://ref.ly/Josh18:19); also ´גֵיא־מ [2 S 8:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:13), [2 K 14:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:7) Qr, [Psalm 60:2](https://ref.ly/Ps60:2) (title), ´גֵּיא הַמּ [1 Ch 18:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr18:12), [2 Ch 25:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:11), [2 K 14:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:7) Kt.

> \[Str4414] † III. \[מָלַח] **vb. denom.** salt, season; **Qal** *Impf.* 2 ms. תִּמְלָךְ sq. acc. [Lv 2:13](https://ref.ly/Lev2:13) (obj. offering). **Pu.** *Pt.* מְמֻלָּח [Ex 30:35](https://ref.ly/Exod30:35) *salted*, i.e. the incense (v. Di.) **Hoph.** *Pf.* 2 fs. \+ *Inf. abs.* הָמְלֵחַ לאֹ הֻמְלַ֫חַתּ Ez [16:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:4), i.e. *rubbed* or *washed with salt* (of infant, in personification).

> \[Str4420] † מְלֵחָה **n.f.** saltness, barrenness;—[Jb 39:6](https://ref.ly/Job39:6) (‖ עֲרָבָה) as dwelling\-place of wild ass, *salt\-plain;* אֶרֶץ מְלֵחָה [Je 17:6](https://ref.ly/Jer17:6) (‖לאֹ תֵשֵׁב); [Psalm 107:34](https://ref.ly/Ps107:34) (opp. אֶרֶץ פְּרִי).

> \[Str4408] † מַלּוּחַ **n.**\[**m.**] mallow; plant growing in salt\-marsh; [Jb 30:4](https://ref.ly/Job30:4) (cf. NowArch. i. 67, 112\).

> \[Str4419] † \[מַלָּח] **n.m.** mariner (loan\-word from As. *malaḫu* DlHWB 412 cf. IdPro1. 178; HalZA iv.1880, 53; so also Aram. ܡܰܠܳܚܳܐ (v. Brock.), whence Ar. مَلَّاحٌ Frä221);—pl. מַלָּחִים [Jon 1:5](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:5), Ez [27:29](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:29); מַלָּחַיִח Ez [27:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:27); מַלָּחֵיהֶם v:[9](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:9) (BaNB 49 Anm. 3\);—*mariners, sailors* of ship going to Tarshish [Jon 1:5](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:5) (cf. רַב הַחֹבֵל v:[6](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:6)); to Tyre Ez [27:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:9), cf. v:[27](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:27), v:[29](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:29) (‖חֹבְלִים).

> \[Str4421] מִלְחָמָה v. I. לחם

[Am 2:15](https://ref.ly/Amos2:15), [Psalm 33:17](https://ref.ly/Ps33:17), [Jb 20:20](https://ref.ly/Job20:20).—(This form not in Hex., Ju., Ch., Is.:l) **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. הִמְלִיט [Is 31:5](https://ref.ly/Isa31:5); 3 fs. הִמְ֫לִיטָה [Is 66:7](https://ref.ly/Isa66:7). **1\.** *give birth to* a male child [Is 66:7](https://ref.ly/Isa66:7) (‖יָֽלְדָה, cf. **Pi. 1**). **2\.** *deliver :* abs. [Is 31:5](https://ref.ly/Isa31:5) (‖גָּנַן, הִצִּיל, פָּסַח). **Hithp.** *Impf.* 1 s. וָאֶתְמַלְּטָה [Jb 19:20](https://ref.ly/Job19:20); 3 mpl. יִתְמַלְּטוּ [Jb 41:11](https://ref.ly/Job41:11). **1\.** *slip forth, escape* (sparks of fire from jaws of crocodile; RV *leap forth*) [Jb 41:11](https://ref.ly/Job41:11). **2\.** *escape* (with the skin of the teeth, the gums; only these left unattacked by leprosy) [Jb 19:20](https://ref.ly/Job19:20).

> \[Str4423] † מֶ֫לֶט **n.**\[**m.**] mortar, cement (etym. dub.; Aram. ܡܠܳܛܳܐ *id.*, whence Ar. مِلَاطٌ Frä10);—[Je 43:9](https://ref.ly/Jer43:9).

> \[Str4424] † מְלַטְיָה **n.pr.m.** (*Yahweh delivered*, cf. GrayProp. N. 267, 293\) a Gibeonite, a builder at the wall [Ne 3:7](https://ref.ly/Neh3:7); LXXL Μαλτιας.

> \[Str4426] מְלִיצָה v. ליץ

## I מלך (id: BDB05338)

I. מלך (√ of foll.; mng. dub.; Thes comp. Ar. مَلَكَ *possess, own exclusively*, cf. Eth. መለከ፡ so BaeRel. 144 (cf. בַּעַל), and NöZMG xl.1886, 727; LyonBib. Sacr., Apr. 1884 DlProl. 30 al. comp. As. *malâku, counsel, advise* DlHWB 412, BH and NH מָלַךְ Aram. ܡܠܰܟ, מְלַךְ, whence king as orig. *counsellor*, he whose opinion is decisive).

Gie:[Je 7:18](https://ref.ly/Jer7:18) BaNB 165, Anm. 2; ZMG xii. 1887, 606\);—only cstr. הַשָּׁמַיִם ´מ [Je 7:18](https://ref.ly/Jer7:18) \+ 4 t. SchrCOT [Je 7:18](https://ref.ly/Jer7:18); SBAk 1886, 477 ff., ZA iii. 1888, 353 ff., iv. 1889, 74 ff. StaZAW l886,123 ff. 289 ff. KueGes. Abh. l86 ff. WeSkizzen iii. 38 NöZMG xli. 1889, 710 f. RS Sem. i. 57, 172 (ed. 2, 57, 189\) GrünbZMG xiii.1888, 45\);—only as worshipped by people of Jerusalem ´הַשּׁ ´לַעֲשוֺת כַּוָּנִים לִמְ [Je 7:18](https://ref.ly/Jer7:18) (‖הַסֵּח נְסָכִים לֵאלֽהִים אֲחֵרִים); ´הַשּׁ ´לְקַטֵּר לִמ [Je 44:17](https://ref.ly/Jer44:17), [44:18](https://ref.ly/Jer44:18), [44:19](https://ref.ly/Jer44:19), [44:25](https://ref.ly/Jer44:25) (all ‖הַסֵּח לָהּ נְסָכִים).

> \[Str4432,1322,4428] † מֹ֫לֶךְ **n.pr.div.** Molech (LXX Μολοχ, Vulgate *Moloch*) (\= מֶלֶח i.e. (*divine*) *King*, with vowels of בּשֶׁת to denote abhorrence, HoffmZAW iii. 1883, 124 RS Sem. i. 353; 2nd ed. 372; cf. In [1 K 11:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:7) rd. prob. מִלְכֹּם, q. v.

N. 119 f., 146 al.);—LXX Μελχ(ε)ια(ς).—**1\.** father of Pashhur [Je 21:1](https://ref.ly/Jer21:1), [38:1](https://ref.ly/Jer38:1), cf. [1 Ch 9:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:12) (priest), [Ne 11:12](https://ref.ly/Neh11:12). **2\.** a prince [Je 38:6](https://ref.ly/Jer38:6). **3\.** priests: **a.** [1 Ch 24:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:9). **b.** [Ne 12:42](https://ref.ly/Neh12:42). **4\.** one of the sealed [Ne 10:4](https://ref.ly/Neh10:4); perh. also [8:4](https://ref.ly/Neh8:4). **5\.** a Levite [1 Ch 6:25](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:25). **6\.** various men: **a.** [Ezr 10:25](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:25) a. **b.** [Ezr 10:25](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:25) b. **c.** [Ezr 10:31](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:31) \= [Ne 3:11](https://ref.ly/Neh3:11). **d.** Ne 3:l4\. **e.** [Ne 3:31](https://ref.ly/Neh3:31).

> \[Str4442,6664,4428] † מַלְכִּי־צֶ֫דֶק **n.pr.m.** king of שָׁלֵם (q.v.), (*my king is Ṣedeḳ*)—[Gn 14:18](https://ref.ly/Gen14:18). [Psalm 110:4](https://ref.ly/Ps110:4). LXX Μελχισεδεκ.

> \[Str4443,4428,7311] † מַלְכִּירָם **n.pr.m.** son of Jeconiah (\= Jehoiakin) (*my king* (\= Yahweh) *is high*, cf. GrayProp. N. 146 f.; Ph. n. pr. מלכרם);—[1 Ch 3:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr3:18). LXX Μελχ(ε)ιραμ.

> \[Str4428,4444,7769] † מַלְכִישׁוּעַ **n.pr.m.** son of Saul *my king* (\= ´י) *is opulence*, cf. GrayProp. N. 147\);—[1 S 14:49](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:49), [31:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam31:2), [1 Ch 8:33](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:33), [9:39](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:39), [10:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr10:2) (in Ch. מַלְכִּי־שׁוּעַ). LXX Μελχεισα, Μελχεσουε, etc.

> \[Str4445] † מַלְכָּם **n.pr.m.** a Benjamite [1 Ch 8:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:9). LXX B Μελχας, A Μελχαμ, LXXL Μελχομ. On [Je 49:1](https://ref.ly/Jer49:1), [49:3](https://ref.ly/Jer49:3), [Zp 1:5](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:5) v. מִלְכֹּם.

> \[Str4445] † מִלְכֹּם **n.pr.div.** Milcom, god of Ammonites (cf. RS Sem. i. 67\)—שִׁקֻּץ עַמֹּנִים ´מ [1 K 11:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:5) (‖עַשְׁתֹּרֶת); cf. אֱלֽהֵי בְנֵי עַמּוֺן ´מ v:[33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:33) (‖´ע \+ כְּמוֺשׁ), so rd. also (for MT מֹלֶח) v:[7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:7) (‖כְּמוֺשׁ); תּוֺעֲבַת ´מ בְּנֵי־עַמּוֺן [2 K 23:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:13) (‖ as [1 K 11:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:33)); rd. מִלְכֹּם also for מַלְכָּם [Je 49:1](https://ref.ly/Jer49:1), [49:3](https://ref.ly/Jer49:3) (LXX Μελχολ, Μελχομ), cf. Gie, and [Zp 1:5](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:5) (SyrVer Vulgate, cf. Now), prob. [2 S 12:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:30) \= [1 Ch 20:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr20:2) (v. מֶלֶח **5 d**), and perh. [Am 1:15](https://ref.ly/Amos1:15) (whence [Je 49:3](https://ref.ly/Jer49:3); cf. Dr Now;—not We GASm).

> \[Str3230] †יַמְלֵךְ **n.pr.m.** a Simeonite (cf. Palm. n.pr. ימלכו VogPalm. 36a);—[1 Ch 4:34](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:34). LXX Ιεμολοχ.

> \[Str4409] †מַלּוּךְ **n.pr.m.** LXX Μαλωχ, Μαλουχ, etc. **1\.** Levites: **a.** [1 Ch 6:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:29). **b.** [Ne 12:2](https://ref.ly/Neh12:2) (priest ?). **2\.** various men: **a.** [Ezr 10:29](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:29). **b.** [Ezr 10:32](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:32). **c.** [Ne 10:5](https://ref.ly/Neh10:5). **d.** [Ne 10:28](https://ref.ly/Neh10:28).

> \[Str4409] †מלוכי Kt, מְלִיכוּ Qr **n.pr.m.** a priest [Ne 12:14](https://ref.ly/Neh12:14). LXX Μαλουχ, LXXL Μαλουκ.

## II מָלַךְ (id: BDB05362)

\[Str4427] †II. \[מָלַךְ] **vb.** counsel, advise (loan\-wd. from Aram . ܡܠܰܟ, מְלַח; cf. As. *malâku*, DlHWB 412\);—only **Niph.** (so NH) *Impf.* לִבִּי עָלַי וַיִּמָּלְֵ i. e. I considered carefully, [Ne 5:7](https://ref.ly/Neh5:7).

> \[Str4434] \[מַלְכֹּ֫דֶת] v. לכד

> \[Str4404] מַלְכֵּן v. מַלְבֵּן sub לבן

* **Associated Passages:** Nehemiah 5:7
* **Associated Articles:** לָכַד (ID: `BDB04902@BDBHebrewLexicon`); לָבֵן (ID: `BDB04772@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## I מָלַל (id: BDB05365)

\[Str4448] †I. \[מָלַל] **vb. Pi.** speak, utter, say (NH Pi. *id.;* Aram. ܡܰܠܶܠ (and many deriv.), מַלֵּל; perh. cf. Ar. مَلَّ IV. *dictate* (a letter, etc.), (ܡܠܘ) مَلَا IV *id.* NöZMG xi. 1886, 725\);—**Pi.** *Pf.* מִלֵּל [Gn 21:7](https://ref.ly/Gen21:7); cf. 1 s. מַלּוֺתִי (Ew:§274 b. N) as **n.pr.** infr.; מִלֵָּלוּ [Jb 33:3](https://ref.ly/Job33:3); *Impf.* יְמַלֵּל [Psalm 106:2](https://ref.ly/Ps106:2); תְּמַלֶּ־ל [Jb 8:2](https://ref.ly/Job8:2); —say, sq. dir. quot. \+ לְ of ind. obj. [Gn 21:7](https://ref.ly/Gen21:7); utter, obj. דַּ֫עַת [Jb 33:3](https://ref.ly/Job33:3); cf. [8:2](https://ref.ly/Job8:2); obj. ´גְּבוּרוֺת י [Psalm 106:2](https://ref.ly/Ps106:2).

> \[Str4405] †מִלָּה **n.f.** word, speech, utterance (poet., esp. Jb; common in Aram.);—´מ [Psalm 139:4](https://ref.ly/Ps139:4), [Jb 30:9](https://ref.ly/Job30:9); sf. מִלָּתִי [Jb 13:17](https://ref.ly/Job13:17) \+ 3 t.; מִלָּתוֺ [2 S 23:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:2); pl. מִלִּים [Jb 6:26](https://ref.ly/Job6:26) \+ 9 t. (all Jb); מִלִּין [Jb 4:2](https://ref.ly/Job4:2) \+ 12 t. (all Jb); pl. sf. מִלָּי [Jb 19:23](https://ref.ly/Job19:23) \+ 4 t.; מִלֶּיְ [Jb 4:4](https://ref.ly/Job4:4), [Pr 23:9](https://ref.ly/Prov23:9); מִלֵּיהֶם [Psalm 19:5](https://ref.ly/Ps19:5); —*word* בִּלְשׁוֺנִי *in* (*on*) *my tongue* [Psalm 139:4](https://ref.ly/Ps139:4); *word* (of ´י), עַל־לְשׁוֺנִי *on my tongue* [2 S 23:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:2); ´שֵכֶל מ [Pr 23:9](https://ref.ly/Prov23:9); fig. [Psalm 19:5](https://ref.ly/Ps19:5) (‖קַוָּם; but rd. קוֺלָם v. Che crit. n.); elsewhere only Jb (34 t.); sg. *speech, utterance* [Jb 13:17](https://ref.ly/Job13:17), [21:2](https://ref.ly/Job21:2), [24:25](https://ref.ly/Job24:25), [29:22](https://ref.ly/Job29:22); also *word \= by\-word*, וָאֱהִי לָהֶם לְמִלָּה [Jb 30:9](https://ref.ly/Job30:9); pl. *words,* [Jb 4:4](https://ref.ly/Job4:4), [6:26](https://ref.ly/Job6:26), [8:10](https://ref.ly/Job8:10), [12:11](https://ref.ly/Job12:11), [15:3](https://ref.ly/Job15:3) (‖דָּבָר), [16:4](https://ref.ly/Job16:4), [19:2](https://ref.ly/Job19:2), [19:23](https://ref.ly/Job19:23), [32:11](https://ref.ly/Job32:11), [32:14](https://ref.ly/Job32:14), [33:1](https://ref.ly/Job33:1), [33:8](https://ref.ly/Job33:8) (´קוֺל מ), [34:2](https://ref.ly/Job34:2), [34:3](https://ref.ly/Job34:3), [34:16](https://ref.ly/Job34:16) (´קוֺל מ), [36:2](https://ref.ly/Job36:2), [36:4](https://ref.ly/Job36:4); words of God [Jb 23:5](https://ref.ly/Job23:5); *words \= speech, speaking*, וַעְצֹּר בְּמִלִּין מִי יוּכָל [Jb 4:2](https://ref.ly/Job4:2), cf. [29:9](https://ref.ly/Job29:9); *utter words*, וְהֹצֵאתָ מִפִּיְ מִלִּין [15:13](https://ref.ly/Job15:13); ´הִגַּדְתָּ מ [26:4](https://ref.ly/Job26:4); *lay snares for words* תְּשִימוּן קִנְצֵי לְמִלִּין [Jb 18:2](https://ref.ly/Job18:2), cf. [Jb 32:15](https://ref.ly/Job32:15) הֶעְתִּיקוּ מֵהֶם מִלִּים; מָלֵתִי מִלִּים [Jb 32:18](https://ref.ly/Job32:18) *I am full of words,* cf. אִם־יֵשׁ־מִלִּין הֲשִׁיבֵנִי [33:32](https://ref.ly/Job33:32); אֲשִׁיבְ מִלִּין [Jb 35:4](https://ref.ly/Job35:4); בִּבְלִי־דַ֫עַת מִלִּין יַכְבִּר [35:16](https://ref.ly/Job35:16), cf. [38:2](https://ref.ly/Job38:2).

> \[Str4413] †מַלּ֫וֺתי as **n.pr.m.** a son of Heman (*I have uttered* \= מִלַּלְתִּי Ew§ 274 b. N. v. מלל);—[1 Ch 25:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:4) (on this appar. list of names v. Ewl.c. RSOTJC 422; 2nd ed. 143\) v:[26](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:26).

> \[Str4450] †מִֽלֲלִי **n.pr.m.** a priest's son, [Ne 12:36](https://ref.ly/Neh12:36).

## II מָלַל (id: BDB05369)

\[Str4448] †II. \[מָלַל] **vb.** rub, scrape (NH *id., rub* ears of wheat, *scrape*);—only **Qal** *Pt.* מוֺלֵל in קֹרֵץ בְּעֵינָו֯ מוֺלֵל בְּרַגְלָו֯ מֹרֶה בְּאֶצְבְּעֹתָיו [Pr 6:13](https://ref.ly/Prov6:13) *he that winketh with his eyes, that scrapeth with his feet* (making signs), etc., so De Now Str \< Ew Wild fr. I. מלל *that speaketh with his feet*.

> \[Str4425] †\[מְלִילָה] **n.f.** ear of wheat, etc. (perh. as *rubbed* or *scraped*, cf. NH הַמּוֺלֵל מְלִילוֺת);—only pl. abs. מְלִילוֺת [Dt 23:26](https://ref.ly/Deut23:26) (cf. Di).

* **Associated Passages:** Deuteronomy 23:26; Proverbs 6:13


## III מָלַל (id: BDB05371)

\[Str5243] †III. \[מָלַל] **vb.** languish, wither, fade (apparently secondary √ of אמל q. v.);—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 ms. יִמַּל [Jb 18:16](https://ref.ly/Job18:16); וַיִּמָּל [Jb 14:2](https://ref.ly/Job14:2); 3 mpl. יִמָּלוּ [Jb 24:24](https://ref.ly/Job24:24), [Psalm 37:2](https://ref.ly/Ps37:2) (so Hup De and most; \> Niph. fr. IV. מלל Thes);—*hang down, wither,* fig. of man [Jb 14:2](https://ref.ly/Job14:2) (כְּצִיץ); of wicked יִבָשׁוּ וּמִמַּ֫עַל יִמַּל קְצִירוֺ מִתַּחַת שָֽׁרָשָׁיו [18:16](https://ref.ly/Job18:16); כְּראֹשׁ שִׁבֹּ֫לֶת יִמָּלוּ [24:24](https://ref.ly/Job24:24) (‖יִקָּפְצוּן, הֻמְּכוּ); כֶּחָצִיר מְהֵרָה יִמָּלוּ [Psalm 37:2](https://ref.ly/Ps37:2) (‖יִבּוֺלוּן). **Po’l.** *Impf.* יְמוֺלֵל [Psalm 90:6](https://ref.ly/Ps90:6) *it withereth* (of grass; \+ וְיָבֵשׁ).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 37:2; Job 18:16; Job 14:2; Job 24:24; Psalms 90:6

(מאן), Eth. *(manu),* ‘What ?’ in these dialects being مَا מָה ܡܳܐ מָא (Eth. *ment* ); מָן in the sense of ‘What ?’ must correspond to the later Syr. ܡܳܢ contr. from ܡܳܐ ܕܶܝܢ Targum מָדֵין Nö§ 68 WSG 125\).
