## מִ־ן (id: BDB05396)

\[Str4480] מִ־ן and •מִ, before יְ (exc. [Dn 12:2](https://ref.ly/Dan12:2), 2 Ch [20:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:11)) contr. to מִי (as מִידֵי from מִיְּדֵי), before gutt. and ר, mostly מֵ, occasionally מִ (Ges§ 102, l R.; more fully Köii. 291ff), מִן before art. in all books much commoner than \-מֵ; מִן; before other words most freq. in Ch \[51 t.: elsewhere 47 t. (Kö292)]; poet. also מִנִּי, with the old י of the gen. (Ges§ 90\.3a) Ju [5:14](https://ref.ly/Judg5:14), [5:14](https://ref.ly/Judg5:14), [Is 46:3](https://ref.ly/Isa46:3), [46:3](https://ref.ly/Isa46:3), [Psalm 44:11](https://ref.ly/Ps44:11), [44:19](https://ref.ly/Ps44:19), [68:32](https://ref.ly/Ps68:32), [74:22](https://ref.ly/Ps74:22), [78:2](https://ref.ly/Ps78:2), [78:42](https://ref.ly/Ps78:42), [88:10](https://ref.ly/Ps88:10) \+ 19 t. Jb, and in לְמִנִּי † [Mi 7:12](https://ref.ly/Mic7:12), [7:12](https://ref.ly/Mic7:12) and מִנֵּ־י (on anal. of עֲדֵי, עֲלֵי) †[Is 30:11](https://ref.ly/Isa30:11), [30:11](https://ref.ly/Isa30:11), prep. expressing the idea of *separation*, hence out of, from, on account of, off, on the side of, since, above, than, so that not (Ph. מ; Aram.ܡܶܢ; Ar.مِنْ; Eth.እምነ፡; Sab. בן (DHMZMG xxix.606 ff.; xxxvii.375\)); with suff, מִמֶּנִּי (the מִן reduplicated, O1:§ 223c, al., Köii. 289 f.), poet. מִנִּי † [Is 22:4](https://ref.ly/Isa22:4), [30:1](https://ref.ly/Isa30:1), [38:12](https://ref.ly/Isa38:12), [Jb 16:6](https://ref.ly/Job16:6), מֶ֫נִּי † [Psalm 18:23](https://ref.ly/Ps18:23), [65:4](https://ref.ly/Ps65:4), [139:19](https://ref.ly/Ps139:19), [Jb 21:16](https://ref.ly/Job21:16), [22:18](https://ref.ly/Job22:18), [30:10](https://ref.ly/Job30:10); מִמְּ, מִמֶָָּ֑, מִמֵּח; מִמֶּ֫נּוּ († [Jb 4:12](https://ref.ly/Job4:12) מֶ֫נְהוּ), מִמֶּ֫נָּה; 1 pl. מִמֶּ֫נּוּ (acc. to Orientals מִמֵּ֫נַוּ: v. BaerJob. p. 57 Kö290); מִכֶּם; מֵהֶם (מִנְּהֶם † [Jb 11:20](https://ref.ly/Job11:20)), מֵהֵ֫מָּה † [Je 10:2](https://ref.ly/Jer10:2), [Ec 12:12](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:12); מֵהֵ֫נָּה 7 t. (v. הֵ֫מָּה **8 c**), מֵהֶן (edd. מֵהֵן) † Ez [16:47](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:47), [16:52](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:52) (v. ZerweckHeb, Präp. Min, 1894\):—1\. with verbs expressing (or implying) *separation* or *removal*, whether from a person or place, or in any direction, also from guilt, calamity, etc.:—thus **a.** to descend *from* a mountain [Ex 19:14](https://ref.ly/Exod19:14), to fall *from* a roof [Dt 22:8](https://ref.ly/Deut22:8), *from* heaven [Is 14:12](https://ref.ly/Isa14:12), to go up *from* a valley [Jos 10:7](https://ref.ly/Josh10:7), to raise up *from* the dunghill [1 S 2:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:8), or the ground [2 S 12:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:17). So constantly with vbs. of *going*, as בא, הלך, שׁב, ברח (sq. a person, usu. מפני), of *calling*, as [Is 24:14](https://ref.ly/Isa24:14) צָֽהֲלוּ מִיָּם, [42:10](https://ref.ly/Isa42:10), [42:11](https://ref.ly/Isa42:11), [Psalm 148:1](https://ref.ly/Ps148:1) of *asking* or *exacting* as שׁאל, בקשׁ, דרשׁ, נָקַם, הִנָּקֵם, of *delivering*, as הושׁיע, הציל, etc., of *hiding* as נעלם, העלים, etc., of *taking* or *withholding*, as לקח, חשך, מנע, of *keeping* (*from*), as נצר, שׁמר, of *being far*, or *desisting*, as רחק [Ex 23:7](https://ref.ly/Exod23:7), חֲדַל מִמֶּנּוּ desist *from* us,[14:2](https://ref.ly/Exod14:2), so הֶרֶף [Dt 9:14](https://ref.ly/Deut9:14), הֵנִיחַ [12:10](https://ref.ly/Deut12:10), השׁקיט \> [Psalm 94:13](https://ref.ly/Ps94:13), שׁבת [Gn 2:2](https://ref.ly/Gen2:2), cf. [5:29](https://ref.ly/Gen5:29); note esp. the pregn. constructions, שׁפט to judge (and save) *from* [Psalm 43:1](https://ref.ly/Ps43:1), sq. מִיַּד [1 S 24:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:16) \+, ענה to answer (and save) *from* [Psalm 22:22](https://ref.ly/Ps22:22), חרשׁ and חשׁה to be silent (turning) *from* [Psalm 28:1](https://ref.ly/Ps28:1), [28:1](https://ref.ly/Ps28:1), החרישׁו ממני [Jb 13:13](https://ref.ly/Job13:13), [Je 38:27](https://ref.ly/Jer38:27), [Psalm 18:22](https://ref.ly/Ps18:22) ולא רשׁעתי מאלהי not did wickedly (turning) *from* my God, [30:4](https://ref.ly/Ps30:4) Kt., [73:27](https://ref.ly/Ps73:27), כל־זוֺנֶה ממך, [Je 3:20](https://ref.ly/Jer3:20) בָּגַד מִן (LXX εἰς \= ב), [31:13](https://ref.ly/Jer31:13) c. שִמַּח ([2 Ch 20:27](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:27)), Ez [27:34](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:34), [Pr 25:17](https://ref.ly/Prov25:17); הֹקַר רַגְלְךָ מִבֵּית רֵעְֶ֫, [Is 29:4](https://ref.ly/Isa29:4), [63:17](https://ref.ly/Isa63:17), [Jo 1:12](https://ref.ly/Joel1:12), [Je 51:5](https://ref.ly/Jer51:5) אַלְמָן מֵאֱלֽהָיו widowed (and severed) *from* etc., Ez [44:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:22); also Ju [7:17](https://ref.ly/Judg7:17) מִמֶּנִּי תִרְאוּ וְכֵן תַּעֲשוּ ye shall *see from* me, i.e. learn what to do by observing me. Some of the verbs mentioned above are construed more precisely with מֵאֵת, מֵעִם, מֵעַל, q.v.: and others, esp. לקח, דרשׁ, הושׁיע, הצּיל, פּדה, פִּלֵּט, idiom. with מִיַּד. With inff., v. infr. **7 b.** Often also with adverbs, as מִשָּׁם *thence*, מֵאַיִן *whence ?* מִוֶּה, מִפֹּה *hence*. Cf. also חָפְשִׁי מִן free (safe) *from* [Jb 3:19](https://ref.ly/Job3:19), עֵזֶר מִצָּרָיו a help *from* his foes [Dt 33:7](https://ref.ly/Deut33:7), [Na 3:11](https://ref.ly/Nah3:11) מָעוֺז מֵאוֺיֵב, [Psalm 32:7](https://ref.ly/Ps32:7) סֵתֶר מִצַּר, [60:13](https://ref.ly/Ps60:12) עֶזְרָת מִצָּר, [Is 4:6](https://ref.ly/Isa4:6) צֵל מֵחֹרֶב a shadow *from*, the heat, [25:4](https://ref.ly/Isa25:4). Here also belong [Is 40:15](https://ref.ly/Isa40:15) מַר מִדְּלִי a drop *from* (hanging from) a bucket; Ct [4:1](https://ref.ly/Song4:1) goats שֶׁגָּֽלְשׁוּ מהר גלעד that sit up (seeming to hang) *from* the hills of Gilead (cf. Od. 21\. 420 ἐκ διφροιο καθήμενος: also the Arabic idiom قَرِبَ مِنْ, lit. to be near *from* a thing): prob. also the difficult passage [Dn 8:9](https://ref.ly/Dan8:9), and from one of them there came forth מִצְּעִירָה קֶרֶן אַחַת a horn *from* (being) a little one, i.e. gradually increasing from small beginnings (but ? rd. אַחֶרֶת צְעִירָה Bev cf. [7:8](https://ref.ly/Dan7:8)). **b.** מן also, without a verb of similar significance, sometimes expresses the idea of separation, *away from, far from* (cf. II. 2\. 162 φίλης ἀπὸ πατρίδος αἴης): [Pr 20:3](https://ref.ly/Prov20:3) שֶׁכֶת מֵרִיב to sit *away from* strife (cf. قَعَدَ عَنْ *ap.* De), [Nu 15:24](https://ref.ly/Num15:24) if the thing be done מֵעֵינֵי הָעֵדָה *away from* the eyes of etc., Ju [5:11](https://ref.ly/Judg5:11) De RV (but Be RVm *because of*), [Is 14:19](https://ref.ly/Isa14:19) cast out מִקִּבְרְ *away from* thy sepulchre, [Jb 28:4](https://ref.ly/Job28:4) דַּלּוּ מֵאֱנוֺשׁ, [Gn 4:11](https://ref.ly/Gen4:11) cursed art thou האדמה מ־ן, De Di *away from* the (tilled) soil, cf. v:[14](https://ref.ly/Gen4:14) (but Kn Ke *from*, cf. v:[12](https://ref.ly/Gen4:12) a), [27:39](https://ref.ly/Gen27:39) (prob.) הָאָרֶץ מִשְׁמַנֵּי *away from* the fatness of the earth shall be thy dwelling; almost \= *without*, Jb [11:15](https://ref.ly/Job11:15) then shalt thou lift up thy face מִמּוּם *away from, without* spot, [19:26](https://ref.ly/Job19:26) (prob.) מִבְּשָרִי *without* my flesh shall I see God, [21:9](https://ref.ly/Job21:9) בָּֽתֵּיהֶם שָׁלוֺם מִפַּחַד are at peace *without* fear, [Pr 1:33](https://ref.ly/Prov1:33), [Je 48:45](https://ref.ly/Jer48:45) fleeing עָֽמְדוּ מִכֹּחַ they stand *without* strength, [Is 22:3](https://ref.ly/Isa22:3) מִקֶּשֶׁת אֻסָּ֫רוּ (Hi De Di Che) they were bound *without* the bow (being used) (but Ges as **2 e:** by the bowmen), [Zp 3:18](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:18). (On [2 S 13:16](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:16) v. We or Dr). See also **7 b. c.** of position, *off, on the side of, on*, esp. with ref. to quarters of the heavens (cf. πρὸς Νότου, ἐκ δεξιῶν, *a* fronte, *a* tergo, etc.): [Gn 2:8](https://ref.ly/Gen2:8) ´י planted a garden in Eden מִקֶּדֶם *off, on* the east, [12:8](https://ref.ly/Gen12:8) מִקֶּדֶם בֵּיתְאֵל מִיָּם וְהָעַי Bethel *off* (*on*) the West and ïAi *off* (*on*) the East; so מִמִּוְרָךְ, מִצָּפוֺן, מִנֶּגֶכ [Jos 11:3](https://ref.ly/Josh11:3), [15:8](https://ref.ly/Josh15:8), [18:5](https://ref.ly/Josh18:5), [1 S 14:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:5), etc.: similarly מִיָּמִין *on* the right, מִשְּמאֹול *on* the left, מִמּוּל and (more rarely) מִפָּנִים *on* the front, מֵאָחוֺר \= behind ([2 S 10:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:9)), מִסָּבִיב [Dt 12:10](https://ref.ly/Deut12:10) \+ *round about*, מִמַּעַל, מִלְמַעְלָה, and מֵעָל (rare) \= *above*, מִתַּחַת [Ex 20:4](https://ref.ly/Exod20:4) \+ *beneath*, מִבַּיִת *on* the inside, מִחוּץ *on* the outside [Gn 6:14](https://ref.ly/Gen6:14) \+, מִנֶּגֶד *in front, at* a distance, [Gn 21:16](https://ref.ly/Gen21:16) \+, מֵרָחוֺק [Ex 2:4](https://ref.ly/Exod2:4) \+ *afar off*, מִפְּנִ֫ימָה *within*, מִוֶּה…מִוֶּה and …מִפֹּה מִפֹּה *on* this side…*on* that side. And so even with verbs of motion, as [Gn 11:2](https://ref.ly/Gen11:2) מִקֶּדֶם ויהי בנסעם as they journeyed—not *from*, but—on, *the side of* the east, virtually \= *eastwards*, [13:11](https://ref.ly/Gen13:11) and Lot journeyed מקדם *east*, [Is 17:13](https://ref.ly/Isa17:13), [22:3](https://ref.ly/Isa22:3) ברחו מֵרָחוֺק they fled—not *from* afar but—*afar*, [23:7](https://ref.ly/Isa23:7) whose feet used to carry her מֵרָחוֺק *afar off* to sojourn, [57:9](https://ref.ly/Isa57:9) thou didst send thy messengers עד־מרחוק *even to afar*. With a foll. לְ (or sf.), most of these words are freq. in the sense of *on the east* (*west*, etc.) *of*…, as [Jos 8:13](https://ref.ly/Josh8:13) לָעִיר מִיָּם *on* the west of the city, מִקֶּדֶם לְ [Gn 3:24](https://ref.ly/Gen3:24), מִצְּפוֺן לְ (cstr. Ges§ 130 a, n.) [Jos 8:11](https://ref.ly/Josh8:11), [8:13](https://ref.ly/Josh8:13), [24:30](https://ref.ly/Josh24:30), מִימִיֶ\+נְ [Psalm 91:7](https://ref.ly/Ps91:7), מִתַּחַת לְ [Gn 1:7](https://ref.ly/Gen1:7), מִמַּעַל לְ [22:9](https://ref.ly/Gen22:9), מִבֵּית לְ [Lv 16:2](https://ref.ly/Lev16:2): מֵאַחֲרֵי *behind* [Ex 14:19](https://ref.ly/Exod14:19) מִצַּד בֵּיתְאֵל *beside* Bethel [Jos 12:9](https://ref.ly/Josh12:9), מִצִּדּוֺ *beside* it [1 S 6:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:8), מֵעֵבֶר לַיַּרְדֵּן *on* the other side of Jordan [Nu 21:1](https://ref.ly/Num21:1) \+; מֵהָֽלְאָה לְ *beyond* [Am 5:27](https://ref.ly/Amos5:27); tablets written מִשְּׁנֵי עֶבְרֵיהֶם *on* both their sides, מִכָּל־עֲבָרָיו [Je 49:32](https://ref.ly/Jer49:32) \+ (v. further the words cited). So מֵהַבַּיִת *on the side of the* house Ez [40:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:7), [40:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:8), [40:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:9), מֵאֵצֶל *beside* Ez [40:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:7); [1 S 20:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:21) the arrows are מִמְּ זָהֵנָּה *from thee* and hither \= on this side of thee, v:[22](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:22) מִמְּ וָהָֽלְהָא, cf. [Nu 32:19](https://ref.ly/Num32:19), [32:19](https://ref.ly/Num32:19).

 **2\.** Out of, Gk.ἐκ, Lat. *ex:* thus **a.** מִמִּצְרַיִם *out of* Egypt: so (*a*) with verbs of *proceeding, removing, expelling*, etc., as הוֺצִיא, יָצָא (v. יָצָא), שִׁלַּח [Gn 3:23](https://ref.ly/Gen3:23), לָקַח [Gn 34:26](https://ref.ly/Gen34:26), גָנַב [40:15](https://ref.ly/Gen40:15), הִכְרִית [Ex 8:5](https://ref.ly/Exod8:5), [Psalm 101:8](https://ref.ly/Ps101:8), גֵּרֵשׁ Ju [11:7](https://ref.ly/Judg11:7), etc.; to draw *out of* water, a pit, etc. [Ex 2:10](https://ref.ly/Exod2:10), [Psalm 40:3](https://ref.ly/Ps40:3); to cast *out of* the hand Ju [15:17](https://ref.ly/Judg15:17); to rescue *out of* the mouth [Am 3:12](https://ref.ly/Amos3:12); to drink מִכּוֺס [2 S 12:3](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:3); [Dt 30:3](https://ref.ly/Deut30:3) וְקִבֶּצְ מִן־הָעַמִּים, Ez [11:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:17), [36:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:24) \+: pregn. with חָרֵד [Ho 10:10](https://ref.ly/Hos10:10), [10:11](https://ref.ly/Hos10:11), רגז [Mi 7:17](https://ref.ly/Mic7:17) to tremble (and come) *out of*, חרג [Psalm 18:46](https://ref.ly/Ps18:46) (‖ [2 S 22:46](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:46) חגר), [74:11](https://ref.ly/Ps74:11), [Is 38:17](https://ref.ly/Isa38:17) חָשַׁק (ni חָשַח leg.), Ez [28:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:16) חִלֵּל, [Ezr 2:62](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:62); cf. נִשְׁכַּח מִפֶּה [Dt 31:21](https://ref.ly/Deut31:21), מִלֵּב [Psalm 31:13](https://ref.ly/Ps31:13). In this applic., often made more definite by the use of …מִתּוֺח, …מִקֶּרֶב *out of* the midst of. (*b*) of the place *out of* which one looks, speaks, exerts power, etc., [Gn 4:10](https://ref.ly/Gen4:10), [Nu 24:19](https://ref.ly/Num24:19) ([Psalm 110:1](https://ref.ly/Ps110:1)), [Dt 4:35](https://ref.ly/Deut4:35) קלו מן־השׁמים השׁמיעך א־ת, [Am 1:2](https://ref.ly/Amos1:2) מציון ישׁאג ´י, [Psalm 14:2](https://ref.ly/Ps14:2), [20:3](https://ref.ly/Ps20:3) send thee help מִקֹּדֶשׁ, v:[7](https://ref.ly/Ps20:7) יַעֲנֵהוּ מִשְּׁמֵי קָדְשׁוֺ, [42:7](https://ref.ly/Ps42:7), [68:36](https://ref.ly/Ps68:36), [85:12](https://ref.ly/Ps85:12), [109:10](https://ref.ly/Ps109:10) ודרשׁו מחרבזתיהם seek (their bread) *out of* their desolate homes (but Ew De as **1 b** *far from;* LXX Gr Che יְגֹֽרְשׁוּ let them *be driven from*), [128:5](https://ref.ly/Ps128:5), [130:1](https://ref.ly/Ps130:1), [Jb 27:23](https://ref.ly/Job27:23), [Is 29:18](https://ref.ly/Isa29:18) to see *out of* darkness, Ct [2:9](https://ref.ly/Song2:9) to look out מִן־הַחַלֹּנוֺת *from* the windows (usu. בעד), [5:4](https://ref.ly/Song5:4) he put forth his hand מִן־הַחוֺר *from* (i.e. *in through*) the hole (in the door or wall). (*c*) with בָּחַר to choose *out of*, [Ex 18:25](https://ref.ly/Exod18:25) \+ oft., cf. with הִבְדִּיל [Dt 29:20](https://ref.ly/Deut29:20), [1 K 8:53](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:53), with יָדַע [Am 3:2](https://ref.ly/Amos3:2), [Ex 19:5](https://ref.ly/Exod19:5) ye shall be to me סְגֻלָּה מכל־העמים a treasure (chosen) *out of* all peoples (cf. with בָּחַר [Dt 7:6](https://ref.ly/Deut7:6), [14:2](https://ref.ly/Deut14:2)). **b.** of the material *out of* which anything is formed, or from which it is derived: (*a*) [Gn 2:19](https://ref.ly/Gen2:19) to form מן־האדמה *out of the* soil, v:[23](https://ref.ly/Gen2:23) מִבְּשָרִי עֶצֶם מֵעֲצָמַי וּבָשָר, [Ex 39:1](https://ref.ly/Exod39:1), [Nu 6:4](https://ref.ly/Num6:4), [Ho 13:2](https://ref.ly/Hos13:2) to make images מִכַּסְפָּם, [Psalm 16:4](https://ref.ly/Ps16:4) נִסְכֵּיהֶם מִדָּם, [45:14](https://ref.ly/Ps45:14), Ct [3:9](https://ref.ly/Song3:9); [Is 40:17](https://ref.ly/Isa40:17) מאפס ותהו נחשׁבו־לו as *made of* nought and unreality are they reckoned by him (‖כְּאַיִן נֶגְדּוֺ), [41:24](https://ref.ly/Isa41:24) הֵן אַתֶּם מֵאַיִן (‖מֵאָפַע, rd. מֵאָפֶם), [Psalm 62:10](https://ref.ly/Ps62:10) מֵהֶבֶל (*al.* **6 c**), [Pr 13:11](https://ref.ly/Prov13:11); [Ex 25:19](https://ref.ly/Exod25:19), [25:31](https://ref.ly/Exod25:31) its cups, its knops, etc., ממנה יהיו shall be *from it* (i.e. of one piece with it), so v:[35](https://ref.ly/Exod25:35), v:[36](https://ref.ly/Exod25:36), [27:2](https://ref.ly/Exod27:2), [30:2](https://ref.ly/Exod30:2) *al.;* (*b*) with verbs of eating, filling, etc., as שׁתה [Gn 9:21](https://ref.ly/Gen9:21), [Jb 21:20](https://ref.ly/Job21:20) מֵחֲמַת שַׁדַּי יִשְׁתֶּה, Ct [8:2](https://ref.ly/Song8:2) הִשְׁקָה, רָוָה [Psalm 36:9](https://ref.ly/Ps36:9) \+, רִוָּה [Is 34:7](https://ref.ly/Isa34:7), שבע [Pr 18:20](https://ref.ly/Prov18:20) \+, השביע [Psalm 81:17](https://ref.ly/Ps81:17) \+, מִלֵּא (rare) [127:5](https://ref.ly/Ps127:5) : cf. [Dt. 32:42](https://ref.ly/Deut32:42) אַשְׁכִּיר חִצַּי מִדָּם, [Is 51:21](https://ref.ly/Isa51:21); [Psalm 28:7](https://ref.ly/Ps28:7) (peculiar) וּמִשִּׁירִי אֲהוֺדֶנּוּ *out of* song I will thank him ([69:31](https://ref.ly/Ps69:31) ´ב), also (unusual) [Is 2:3](https://ref.ly/Isa2:3) וְיוֺרֵנוּ מִדְּרָכָיו i.e. *out of* (the treasure of) his ways (*al.* part. \= *some of*), [Psalm 94:12b](https://ref.ly/Ps94:12) מִתּוֺרָֽתְ תְלַמְּדֶנּוּ; [Ec 7:10](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:10) to speak מֵחָכְמָה (cf. Rabb. מִתּוֺח). **c.** of the *source* or *origin:* viz. (*a*) of parents, [Gn 16:2](https://ref.ly/Gen16:2) אִבָּנֶה ממנה I shall be built up *out of* her (so [30:3](https://ref.ly/Gen30:3)), [17:16](https://ref.ly/Gen17:16) מלכי עמים ממנה יהיו, [35:11a](https://ref.ly/Gen35:11): often of coming forth מִבֶּטֶן [Jb 1:21](https://ref.ly/Job1:21), מֵחֲלָצֶיְ [Gn 35:11](https://ref.ly/Gen35:11) b, ממעיך [15:4](https://ref.ly/Gen15:4), [Is 2:20](https://ref.ly/Isa2:20) ´י give thee seed *from* this woman; cf. [Jb 16:4](https://ref.ly/Job16:4) מִי יִתֵּן טָהוֺר מִטָּמֵא (*b*) of the tribe or people, *from* which a person comes (cf. **1 a**), [Nu 3:12](https://ref.ly/Num3:12) ´מכני ישר …כל בכור, [Jos 12:4](https://ref.ly/Josh12:4), [2 S 4:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:2), [21:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:2), [Je 1:1](https://ref.ly/Jer1:1) \+; and so of a person's native place, as Ju [12:8](https://ref.ly/Judg12:8) אבצן מבית לחם Ibzan *from* B., [13:2](https://ref.ly/Judg13:2) אִישׁ מִצָּרְעָה [17:1](https://ref.ly/Judg17:1), [17:7](https://ref.ly/Judg17:7), [1 S 1:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:1), [9:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:1), [2 S 23:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:20), [23:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:30), [Am 1:1](https://ref.ly/Amos1:1) \+, cf. [Je 46:25](https://ref.ly/Jer46:25) אָמוֺן מִנּאֹ; [Is 48:1](https://ref.ly/Isa48:1) וּמִמֵּי יְהוּדָה יָצָֽאוּ, v:[2](https://ref.ly/Isa48:2) מֵעִיר הַקֹּדֶשׁ נִקְרֽאוּ, [58:12](https://ref.ly/Isa58:12) וּבָנוּ מִמֵח (those) *from* thee shall build, [Psalm 68:27](https://ref.ly/Ps68:27) מִמְּקוֺר ישראל ye (sprung) *from* the fountain of Israel, [Mi 5:1](https://ref.ly/Mic5:1), [Na 1:11](https://ref.ly/Nah1:11), v:[14](https://ref.ly/Nah1:14) fig. מִשִּׁמְ), [Zp 3:18](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:18); [Je 30:21](https://ref.ly/Jer30:21), [Zc 10:4](https://ref.ly/Zech10:4). (*c*) of beasts and things, [Gn 2:7](https://ref.ly/Gen2:7) עָפָר מִן־הָאֲדָמָה, [Je 5:6](https://ref.ly/Jer5:6) אַרְיֵה מִיַּעַר a lion *from* the forest, [Psalm 80:14](https://ref.ly/Ps80:14) חֲוִיר מִיַּעַרִ; cf. [10:18](https://ref.ly/Ps10:18) אֶנוֺשׁ מִן־הָאָרֶץ man *from* the earth (*terrenus*), [72:16](https://ref.ly/Ps72:16) יָצִיצוּ מֵעִיר; [Jb 28:12](https://ref.ly/Job28:12) וְהַחָכְמָה מֵאַיַן תִּמָּצֵא. (*d*) מִלֵּב [Is 59:13](https://ref.ly/Isa59:13), [Jb 8:10](https://ref.ly/Job8:10), esp. with reference to what is self\-devised, [Nu 16:28](https://ref.ly/Num16:28) כִּי לאֹ מִלִּבִּי, [24:13](https://ref.ly/Num24:13), [1 K 12:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:33), [Ne 6:8](https://ref.ly/Neh6:8); נְבִיאֵי מִלִּבָּם Ez [13:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek13:2). **d.** of the *source* or *author* of an action, counsel, or event: [1 S 24:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:14) מֵרְשָׁעִים יֵצֵא רֶשַׁע, [2 S 3:37](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:37) היתה מהמלך לא it was not *from* the king to, etc.; spec. of ´י, [Gn 24:50](https://ref.ly/Gen24:50) יצא הדבר ´כי מי, Ju [14:4](https://ref.ly/Judg14:4) knew הוא ´כי מי that it was *from* J., [1 K 2:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:15) ´היתה לו כי מי, [Pr 16:1](https://ref.ly/Prov16:1), [20:24](https://ref.ly/Prov20:24) מִצְעֲדֵי גֶבֶר ´מי, [Psalm 37:39](https://ref.ly/Ps37:39), [62:2](https://ref.ly/Ps62:2) מִמֶּנּוּ יְשׁוּעָתִי, [Ho 8:4](https://ref.ly/Hos8:4) they have made kings, מִמֶּנִּי וְלאֹ not *from me*, i.e. not at my prompting, [Is 30:1](https://ref.ly/Isa30:1), [50:11](https://ref.ly/Isa50:11) (מִיָּדִי) Ez [3:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:17) end \= [33:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:7) and warn them *from me*, [1 Ch 5:22](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:22), [13:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr13:2), [2 Ch 22:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr22:7), [25:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:20) (syn. מֵאֵת, מֵעִם, which are more frequent in this sense); poet. [Ho 7:5](https://ref.ly/Hos7:5) חֲמַת מִיַּיִן heat *from wine*, [Psalm 9:14](https://ref.ly/Ps9:14) מִשּֽנְאַי עָנְיִי *at the hands of* my haters, [74:22](https://ref.ly/Ps74:22), [Jb 4:13](https://ref.ly/Job4:13) שְעִפִּים מֵחֶזְיֹנוֺת לַיְלָה thoughts (arising) *out of*, etc., [6:25](https://ref.ly/Job6:25) הוֺכֵחַ מִכֶּם reproof *proceeding from* you, [20:29](https://ref.ly/Job20:29), [20:29](https://ref.ly/Job20:29), [Psalm 78:2](https://ref.ly/Ps78:2) חִידוֺת מִקֶּדֶם indirect lessons (springing) *out of* antiquity; [Dn 11:31](https://ref.ly/Dan11:31) זְרֹעִים מִמֶּנּוּ (Köiii. 241\). As marking the author of a judgment or estimate (rare), [Nu 32:22](https://ref.ly/Num32:22) וּמִיִּשְרָאֵל ´נְקִיִּם מי guiltless *at the hands of* (\= in the judgment of) J. and Israel (cf. מֵעִם [2 S 3:28](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:28), [Jb 34:33](https://ref.ly/Job34:33)), [Jb 4:17](https://ref.ly/Job4:17) shall man be just מֵאֱלֽהַּ *at God's hands ?* (‖מֵעשֵֹׁהוּ), [Je 51:5](https://ref.ly/Jer51:5) b (Ges Gf.); so in ´חָלִילָה לִי מי. **e.** of the immediate, or *efficient*, cause (chiefly poet.), *in consequence of, at by* (cf. Gk. ἀπό, as Hd. 2:54ζήησιν ἀπὸ σφέων γενέσθαι): (*a*) the cause, a *person* [Gn 19:36](https://ref.ly/Gen19:36) \+ הָֽרְתָה מן to be pregnant *by*, [49:25](https://ref.ly/Gen49:25), [1 S 31:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam31:3) וַיָּחֶל מֵהַמּוֺרִים was in anguish *from* the archers, [Ho 7:4](https://ref.ly/Hos7:4) בֹּעֵ֫רָה מֵאֹפֶה (ni בֹּעֵר הֵם leg.: v. We), [Psalm 18:9](https://ref.ly/Ps18:9), [Na 1:5](https://ref.ly/Nah1:5) הרים רעשׁו ממנו, [Dn 11:31](https://ref.ly/Dan11:31) (accents); with pass. vb., [Na 1:6](https://ref.ly/Nah1:6) הצורים נִתְּצוּ ממנו, [Psalm 37:23](https://ref.ly/Ps37:23) (cf. [Pr 20:24](https://ref.ly/Prov20:24) **d**), [Jb 24:1](https://ref.ly/Job24:1), Ec 12:1l, Dn 8:1l. (*b*) the cause, a *thing*, [Gn 49:24](https://ref.ly/Gen49:24) his arms were agile מידי אביר יעקב *from* the hands, etc., [Is 6:4](https://ref.ly/Isa6:4) the thresholds shook מִקּוֺל הַקּוֺרֵא (so מקול elsewhere, as [30:31](https://ref.ly/Isa30:31), [33:3](https://ref.ly/Isa33:3), [Je 8:16](https://ref.ly/Jer8:16)), [28:7](https://ref.ly/Isa28:7) תָעוּ מִן־הַשֵּׁכָר (‖ב), [31:4](https://ref.ly/Isa31:4), [31:9](https://ref.ly/Isa31:9), [Je 10:10](https://ref.ly/Jer10:10), Ez [19:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:10) became fruitful מִמַּיִם רַבִּים, [31:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:5), [Jb 4:9](https://ref.ly/Job4:9) מִנִּשְׁמַת אֱלוֺהַּ יאֹבֵדוּ *at* the breath of God, [14:9](https://ref.ly/Job14:9) מֵרֵיחַ מַיִם יַפְרִחַ, [31:23](https://ref.ly/Job31:23), [39:26](https://ref.ly/Job39:26) הֲמִבִּינָֽתְ יַאֲבֶר־נֵץ [Psalm 39:11](https://ref.ly/Ps39:11), [73:19](https://ref.ly/Ps73:19) סָפוּ תַּמּוּ מִן־בַּלָּהוֺת, [78:65](https://ref.ly/Ps78:65) מִתְרוֺנֵן מִיַּיִן, [104:15](https://ref.ly/Ps104:15) להצהיל פנים משׁמן *from* oil, [107:39](https://ref.ly/Ps107:39), cf. [Jb 7:14](https://ref.ly/Job7:14) (‖ב); so with ´מִגַּעֲרַת י *at* J.'s rebuke, [18:16](https://ref.ly/Ps18:16), [76:7](https://ref.ly/Ps76:7) \+; with a pass. vb., [Gn 9:11](https://ref.ly/Gen9:11) to be cut off מִמֵּי הַמַּבּוּל, [2 S 7:29](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:29) וּמִכִּכָֽתְ יְבֹרַח, [Is 28:7](https://ref.ly/Isa28:7) נבלעו מן־היין are undone *through* wine ([22:3](https://ref.ly/Isa22:3) is dub.: see **1 b**), Ob:[9](https://ref.ly/Obad1:9), [Jb 28:4](https://ref.ly/Job28:4) הַנִּשְׁכָּחִים מִנִּי רָגֶל; cf. [Is 34:3](https://ref.ly/Isa34:3), [Ho 6:8](https://ref.ly/Hos6:8). (*c*) with verbs of *fearing*, to express the source of the emotion, as ירא [Psalm 3:7](https://ref.ly/Ps3:7) \+ oft. (also c. מִפְּנֵי), פָּחַד [27:1](https://ref.ly/Ps27:1), נִבְהַל Ez [26:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:18), גּוּר [Dt 18:22](https://ref.ly/Deut18:22), [Jb 41:17](https://ref.ly/Job41:17), [Est 5:9](https://ref.ly/Esth5:9) ולא־קם ולא זָע ממנו; with בּוֺשׁ *to be disconcerted* or *disappointed*, to express the source of the disappointment, [Is 1:29](https://ref.ly/Isa1:29) \+ oft.; with שָמֵחַ † [Pr 5:18](https://ref.ly/Prov5:18), [Ec 2:10](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:10). **f.** of the remoter cause, the ultimate ground *on account of* which something happens or is done. (N.B. The line between **e** and **f** is not always clearly defined): [Ex 2:23](https://ref.ly/Exod2:23) they sighed מן־העבדה *on account of* the bondage, [6:9](https://ref.ly/Exod6:9), [15:23](https://ref.ly/Exod15:23), [Dt 7:7](https://ref.ly/Deut7:7) not מֵרֻבְּכֶם *through, by reason of*, your numbers did J. set his love upon you, [Jos 22:24](https://ref.ly/Josh22:24) to do a thing מִדְּאָנָה *out of* carefulness, [2 S 23:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:4) מִנֹּגַהּ, [1 K 14:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:4), [Is 53:5](https://ref.ly/Isa53:5) wounded מִפְּשָׁעֵינוּ *on account of* our transgressions, bruised מֵעֲוֺנוֺתֵינוּ, v:[8](https://ref.ly/Isa53:8), [Jb 4:19](https://ref.ly/Job4:19), Ob:[10](https://ref.ly/Obad1:10), Hb [2:17](https://ref.ly/Hab2:17), [Psalm 6:8](https://ref.ly/Ps6:8) עשׁשׁה עיני מִכַּעַס *from* vexation (cf. [Jb 17:7](https://ref.ly/Job17:7): [Psalm 31:10](https://ref.ly/Ps31:10) ´ב), [31:12](https://ref.ly/Ps31:12), [38:19](https://ref.ly/Ps38:19) I am concerned *on account of* my sin, [Pr 20:4](https://ref.ly/Prov20:4) מֵחֹרֶף עצל לא יחרשׁ *by reason of* the winter, [Jb 22:4](https://ref.ly/Job22:4) הֲמִיִּרְאָתְ יֹכִיחְֶָ *on account of* thy fear of him? Ct [3:8](https://ref.ly/Song3:8), [Ru 1:13](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:13) כִּי־מַר־לִי מְאֹד מִכֶּם *because of you:* see also [Dt 28:34](https://ref.ly/Deut28:34), [28:67](https://ref.ly/Deut28:67), [32:19](https://ref.ly/Deut32:19), Ju [2:18](https://ref.ly/Judg2:18), [Is 65:14](https://ref.ly/Isa65:14), [Je 12:4](https://ref.ly/Jer12:4), [24:2](https://ref.ly/Jer24:2) מֵרֹעַ, [50:13](https://ref.ly/Jer50:13), [51:5](https://ref.ly/Jer51:5) b (Ew Ke), Ez [7:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:27) (but read prob. with LXX SyrVer Vulgate Co כְּדרכם), [16:61](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:61), [35:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek35:11) (AV *out of*), [45:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:20), [Mi 2:12](https://ref.ly/Mic2:12), [7:13](https://ref.ly/Mic7:13), [Psalm 5:11](https://ref.ly/Ps5:11), [12:6](https://ref.ly/Ps12:6), [107:17](https://ref.ly/Ps107:17), [119:53](https://ref.ly/Ps119:53); [Gn 49:12](https://ref.ly/Gen49:12) red of eyes מִָ\+יּיִן, and white of teeth מֵחָלָב. Often also in מֵרֹב *from* the abundance of, abs. *for* abundance, with many different verbs, as [Gn 16:10](https://ref.ly/Gen16:10) לאֹ יִסָּפֵר מֵרֹב [Jos 9:13](https://ref.ly/Josh9:13), [1 S 1:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:16), etc. Similarly in מֵאֵין, מִבְּלִי, מִבִּלְתִּי (pp. 35, 115, 117\); and before an inf. (**7 a**), and in מֵאֲשֶׁר (p. 84\). **g.** the cause being conceived as regulative, מן occasionally approaches in sense to *according to* (cf. Lat. *ex* more, *ex* lege), [Jon 3:7](https://ref.ly/Jonah3:7) proclaimed in Nineveh מטעם המלך וגדליו *at, by, in accordance* with, the decree of the king (cf. Aram. [Ezr 6:14](https://ref.ly/Ezra6:14), [Dn 2:8](https://ref.ly/Dan2:8)): so [Jb 39:26](https://ref.ly/Job39:26) (**2 e** (*b*)) acc. to Ges Hi. **3\.** *Partitively* (a sense connecting with **2 a** (*c*), above):—**a.** [Gn 6:19](https://ref.ly/Gen6:19) שְׁנַיִם מִכֹּל *two out of, from,* all, [7:8](https://ref.ly/Gen7:8); [39:11](https://ref.ly/Gen39:11) …אישׁ מן a man (or one) *of*…(so [Nu 25:6](https://ref.ly/Num25:6), [1 S 24:28](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:28) \+, …אנשׁים מן [Nu 16:2](https://ref.ly/Num16:2), [Jos 2:2](https://ref.ly/Josh2:2) \+, [2 K 2:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:7), etc.); [Gn 42:16](https://ref.ly/Gen42:16) send מִכֶּם אֶחָד [Lv 1:2a](https://ref.ly/Lev1:2) [17:12](https://ref.ly/Lev17:12) כל־נפשׁ מִכֶּם, [19:34](https://ref.ly/Lev19:34), [26:8](https://ref.ly/Lev26:8), Ju [8:14](https://ref.ly/Judg8:14) נַעַר מֵאַנְשֵׁי סֻכּוֺת, [1 S 22:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:8), etc.; Ju [14:19](https://ref.ly/Judg14:19), [2 K 2:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs2:24), [Is 66:19](https://ref.ly/Isa66:19); …אֶחָד מִן [1 S 16:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:18) \+, …אַחַד מִן (Ges§ 130, 1\) [Dt 25:5](https://ref.ly/Deut25:5) \+; אַחַת מֵהֵ֫נָּה [Psalm 34:21](https://ref.ly/Ps34:21), [Is 34:16](https://ref.ly/Isa34:16); …מִי מִן [Jb 5:1](https://ref.ly/Job5:1), [Is 50:1](https://ref.ly/Isa50:1), …אֵין מִן [1 S 14:39](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:39), [Is 51:18](https://ref.ly/Isa51:18); [Is 21:11](https://ref.ly/Isa21:11) מ־ה מלילה how much *of* the night? [2 Ch 31:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:3) וּמְנָת הַמֶּלָח מִן־רְכוּשׁוֺ. **b.** the number being omitted, מִן is used indefinitely:—(*a*) \= *some of*,—as subj. [Ex 16:27](https://ref.ly/Exod16:27) יצאו מן־העם there went out (some) *of* the people, [Lv 25:49](https://ref.ly/Lev25:49), [2 S 11:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:17), [11:24](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:24), esp. in late Hebrew [Ezr 2:68](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:68), [2:70](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:70), [7:7](https://ref.ly/Ezra7:7), [Ne 11:4](https://ref.ly/Neh11:4), [11:25](https://ref.ly/Neh11:25), [1 Ch 4:42](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:42), [9:30](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:30), [9:32](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:32), [2 Ch 17:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr17:11), [32:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:21), [Dn 11:35](https://ref.ly/Dan11:35); more freq. as obj., esp. with לקח, [Gn 4:3](https://ref.ly/Gen4:3) וַיָּבֵא מִפְּרִי האדמה brought *of* the fruit of the ground, [30:14](https://ref.ly/Gen30:14) give me מִדּוּדָאֵי בְנֵח (some) *of* thy son's love\-apples, [33:15](https://ref.ly/Gen33:15), [Ex 12:7](https://ref.ly/Exod12:7), [17:5](https://ref.ly/Exod17:5), [Lv 18:30](https://ref.ly/Lev18:30), [20:2](https://ref.ly/Lev20:2), [Nu 11:17](https://ref.ly/Num11:17); [13:23](https://ref.ly/Num13:23), [1 K 12:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:4), [Is 39:7](https://ref.ly/Isa39:7), [47:13](https://ref.ly/Isa47:13), [Am 2:11](https://ref.ly/Amos2:11), [Dn 8:10](https://ref.ly/Dan8:10) \+, Ct [1:2](https://ref.ly/Song1:2) ישׁקני מִנְּשִׁיקוֺת פִּיו let him kiss me (with) *some of* the kisses etc., [Psalm 72:15](https://ref.ly/Ps72:15), [137:3](https://ref.ly/Ps137:3) מִשִּׁיר צִיּוֺן (some) of the songs of Zion, [Jb 11:6](https://ref.ly/Job11:6) (Ges RVm) \+ oft.; in late Hebr. note also …יֵשׁ מִן there are *some of*…[Ne 5:5](https://ref.ly/Neh5:5); …מֵהֶם …מֵהֶם *some… others…* [1 Ch 9:28](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:28) f. (as in Aram. [Dn 2:33](https://ref.ly/Dan2:33), and PS2155); …מִקְצָת *some of…* (as in NH) † [Dn 1:2](https://ref.ly/Dan1:2), [Ne 7:69](https://ref.ly/Neh7:69). (*b*) to designate an individual (rare), [Gn 28:11](https://ref.ly/Gen28:11) and he took מֵאַבְנֵי הַמָּקוֺם (one) of the stones of the place (see v:[18](https://ref.ly/Gen28:18)), [Ex 6:25](https://ref.ly/Exod6:25) מבּנות פוטיאל (one) *of* the daughters of P., Ez [17:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:5), [17:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:13), [Ne 13:28](https://ref.ly/Neh13:28), [Psalm 132:11](https://ref.ly/Ps132:11); as subj. [Lv 25:33](https://ref.ly/Lev25:33), [Dn 11:5](https://ref.ly/Dan11:5) the king of the south שָרָיו וּמִ־ן and (one) of his captains, v:[7](https://ref.ly/Dan11:7), cf. [Je 41:1](https://ref.ly/Jer41:1), [Est 4:5](https://ref.ly/Esth4:5), [2 Ch 26:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:11); [Ru 2:20](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:20) מִגֹּאֲלֵנוּ הוא he is (one) of our kinsmen, [Ex 2:6](https://ref.ly/Exod2:6). (*c*) in a neg. or hypoth. sentence (whether subject or object) \= *any, aught*, [Dt 16:4](https://ref.ly/Deut16:4) ולא ילין מן־הבשר and there shall *not* remain *any* of the flesh, [1 K 18:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:5) ולא נכרית מן־הבהמה and we shall not destroy *any* of the beasts, [2 K 10:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:10) (cf. [Jos 21:43](https://ref.ly/Josh21:43), [23:14](https://ref.ly/Josh23:14), [1 S 3:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:19), [1 K 8:56](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:56), [Est 6:10](https://ref.ly/Esth6:10)), v:[23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:23) ´פֶּן־יֶשׁ־פֹּה מֵעַבְדֵי י [Jb 27:6](https://ref.ly/Job27:6) לאֹ יֶחֱרַף לְבָבִי מִיָּמָי my heart reproacheth not *one* of my days: with אִם, etc. [Ex 29:34](https://ref.ly/Exod29:34), [Lv 4:2](https://ref.ly/Lev4:2) a (cf. v:[22](https://ref.ly/Lev4:22), v:[27](https://ref.ly/Lev4:27)), [11:32](https://ref.ly/Lev11:32) (מהם), v:[33](https://ref.ly/Lev11:33), v:[35](https://ref.ly/Lev11:35), v:[37](https://ref.ly/Lev11:37)–[39](https://ref.ly/Lev11:39), [18:29](https://ref.ly/Lev18:29), [25:25](https://ref.ly/Lev25:25), [Nu 5:6](https://ref.ly/Num5:6); מִכֹּל *anything whatever*, [Lv 5:24](https://ref.ly/Lev5:24), [11:34](https://ref.ly/Lev11:34); cf. [1 S 23:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:23) (?). (*d*) peculiarly, with a noun of unity, esp. אֶחָד, to express forcibly the idea of a *single one:* [Dt 15:7](https://ref.ly/Deut15:7) if there be a poor man in thy midst, מֵאַחַר אַחֶיְ *any one* of thy brethren, [Lv 4:2b](https://ref.ly/Lev4:2) if he doeth מֵאַחַת מֵהֵ֫נָּה *any one* of these things, [5:13](https://ref.ly/Lev5:13), Ez [18:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:10) מֵאַחַד מֵאֵלֶּה וְעָשָה אָח (where אח is a *vox nihili*) and doeth *aught* of these things; [1 S 14:45](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:45) if there shall fall מִשַּעֲרַת ראֹשׁוֺ אַרְצָה *a single* hair of his head to the ground! (for שְעָרָה as a nom. unit. v. Ges§ 122 t Dr), cf. [2 S 14:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:11), [1 K 1:52](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:52). (Probably a rhetorical application of the partitive sense, though the explanation ‘*starting from* one ’ \= ‘*even* one ’ would also be possible. مِنْ is used similarly after neg. and interrog. particles, not merely with collective nouns, as مَاء لَهُمْ مِنْ nought have they of knowledge (Qor 18:4\), but also with nouns of unity, as Qor 3:55 Ye have not عِلْمٍ *aught of* (\= *any*) god, except Him; 19:19 dost thou perceive مِنْ إِلَهٍ *even one* of them ? 6:59 there falleth not مِنْهُمْ مِنْ أَحَدٍ *even a single* leaf (nom. unit.) without His knowing it. See further Thes, and WAG. ii. § 48 f. b), (*e*) (rare) specifying the objects, or elements, of which a genus consists (the Ar. مِنْ وَرَقَةٍ, *min* of *explication*, WAG. ii. § 48 g): [Gn 6:2](https://ref.ly/Gen6:2) אשׁר בחרו מכל *consisting of* all whom they chose, [7:22](https://ref.ly/Gen7:22), [9:10](https://ref.ly/Gen9:10), [Lv 11:32](https://ref.ly/Lev11:32) (מכל), [1 Ch 5:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:18), [Je 40:7](https://ref.ly/Jer40:7) (מאשׁר); [44:28](https://ref.ly/Jer44:28) and they shall know דְּבַר־מִי יָקוּם מִמֶּנִּי וּמֵהֶם whose word shall stand, *consisting of me and of them* (defining the genus embraced by מי: cf. مِنْ لِلْبَيَانِ both (of them), Abel as well as Cain, and Qor 3:193: W1. c.).—On מֵאַיִן [Je 10:6](https://ref.ly/Jer10:6), [10:7](https://ref.ly/Jer10:7), v. אַיִן *ad fin.*, p. 35\. **4\.** Of *time*—viz. **a.** as marking the *terminus a quo*, the anterior limit of a continuous period, *from, since* [Dt 9:24](https://ref.ly/Deut9:24) מִיּוֺם דַּעְתִּי אֶתְכֶם *from* the day of my (first) knowing you (so [1 S 7:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam7:2), [8:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:8) \+ ); [1 S 18:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:9) מהיום ההוא וָהָֽלְאָה *from* that day and onwards, simil. † [Lv 22:27](https://ref.ly/Lev22:27), [Nu 15:23](https://ref.ly/Num15:23), Ez [39:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:22); [Is 18:2](https://ref.ly/Isa18:2), [18:7](https://ref.ly/Isa18:7) (where מִן־הוּא \= מֵאֲשֶׁר הוּא; cf. [Na 2:9](https://ref.ly/Nah2:9) מִימֵי הִיא \= מִימֵי אֲשֶׁר הִיא); מהיום ההוא וָמָֽעְלָה † [1 S 16:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:13), [30:25](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:25) ([Hg 2:15](https://ref.ly/Hag2:15), [2:18](https://ref.ly/Hag2:18) appar. of time *backwards*); מֵאָז *from* the time of .…(v. p. 23\); מִן־הַבֶּטֶן *from* the womb Ju [13:5](https://ref.ly/Judg13:5), [13:7](https://ref.ly/Judg13:7), מִבֶּטֶן אִמִּי [16:17](https://ref.ly/Judg16:17) \+; …מִימֵי [Ho 10:9](https://ref.ly/Hos10:9), , [Is 23:7](https://ref.ly/Isa23:7) [Je 36:2](https://ref.ly/Jer36:2) \+; מִיָּמֶיְ idiom. \= *since* thou wast born † [1 S 25:28](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:28), [1 K 1:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:6) (מִיָּמָיו), [Jb 38:12](https://ref.ly/Job38:12); מִקֶּדֶם *from* antiquity Hb [1:12](https://ref.ly/Hab1:12), [Psalms 74:12](https://ref.ly/Ps74:12), [Is 42:14](https://ref.ly/Isa42:14) הֶחֱשֵׁיתִי מֵעוֺלָם I have been silent *since* old time; מֵעַתָּה *from* now, *hence\-forth* [Je 3:4](https://ref.ly/Jer3:4) \+, מִיּוֺם *from* to\-day † [43:13](https://ref.ly/Jer43:13), Ez [48:35](https://ref.ly/Ezek48:35), מן־היום הזה [Hg 2:19](https://ref.ly/Hag2:19). See also **5 c, 7 c.** Cf. [Ex 33:6](https://ref.ly/Exod33:6) מֵהַר חוֺרֵב *from* Horeb, [Ho 13:4](https://ref.ly/Hos13:4) I am ´י thy God מֵאֶרֶץ מִצְרַיִם. **b.** as marking the period immediately succeeding the limit, *after:* [Gn 38:24](https://ref.ly/Gen38:24) כְּמִשְׁלשׁ חֳדָשִׁים *after* about three months it was told Judah, מִיָּמִים רַבִּים † [Jos 23:1](https://ref.ly/Josh23:1), Ez [38:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:8), מִיָּמִים *after* (some) days, † Ju [11:4](https://ref.ly/Judg11:4), [14:8](https://ref.ly/Judg14:8), [15:1](https://ref.ly/Judg15:1), מִיֹּמַיִם *after* two days † [Ho 6:2](https://ref.ly/Hos6:2); מֵרֹב יָמִים [Is 24:22](https://ref.ly/Isa24:22): more freq. with קֵץ or קָצֶה, [Gn 4:3](https://ref.ly/Gen4:3) מִקֵּץ יָמִים, [8:3](https://ref.ly/Gen8:3), [Jos 3:2](https://ref.ly/Josh3:2) \+ oft.; [Dt 14:28](https://ref.ly/Deut14:28) מִקְצֵה שָׁלשׁ שָׁנִים, [15:1](https://ref.ly/Deut15:1) שָׁנִים מִקְצֵה שֶׁבַע (by which is meant not the actual *end* of the three or seven years, but the period when the third or seventh year has arrived); מִמָּחֳרָת *after* the morrow (had arrived) \= *on* the morrow, [Gn 19:34](https://ref.ly/Gen19:34) \+ oft.; מֵהַבֹּקֶר [2 S 2:27](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:27).—[2 S 23:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:4) מִמָּטָר; [Is 53:11](https://ref.ly/Isa53:11) מֵעֲמַל נַפְשׁוֺ; perhaps [29:18](https://ref.ly/Isa29:18) (Kö581). Cf. [2 S 20:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:5) and he delayed מן־המועד *beyond* the appointed time. **c.** in such phrases, however, מִן sometimes (cf. **1 c**) loses its signif.: thus מֵאָז [2 S 15:34](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:34), [Is 16:13](https://ref.ly/Isa16:13) not *from* former time, but *in* former time, formerly, [Gn 6:4](https://ref.ly/Gen6:4) הַגִּבּוֺרִים אֲשֶׁר מֵעוֺלָם which were *of old*, [Jos 24:2](https://ref.ly/Josh24:2) dwelt *of old*, [Psalm 77:6](https://ref.ly/Ps77:6) days מִקֶּדֶם *aforetime*, מֵאַחֲרֵי [Dt 29:21](https://ref.ly/Deut29:21) (p. 30\).—†מִקָּרוֺב lit. *from near*, i.e. recently [Dt 32:17](https://ref.ly/Deut32:17), of short duration [Jb 20:5](https://ref.ly/Job20:5), shortly Ez [7:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:8); at hand (in a *local* sense, **1 c**) [Je 23:23](https://ref.ly/Jer23:23). **5\.** (וְעַד] עַד…מִן *from…even, to :* very oft .:—**a.** in geogr. or local sense, [Gn 10:19](https://ref.ly/Gen10:19), [15:18](https://ref.ly/Gen15:18) מנהר מצרים עד הנהר הגדול, [25:18](https://ref.ly/Gen25:18), [Ex 23:31](https://ref.ly/Exod23:31), [Dt 2:36](https://ref.ly/Deut2:36), [1 S 3:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:20), [2 S 5:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:25), [Je 31:38](https://ref.ly/Jer31:38), [Psalm 72:8](https://ref.ly/Ps72:8) \+ oft.; so with מִלְּבוֺא † [1 K 8:65](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:65) ([2 Ch 7:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:8)), [2 K 14:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:25), [Am 6:14](https://ref.ly/Amos6:14); [Dt 13:8](https://ref.ly/Deut13:8) מִקְצֵה הָאָרֶץ וְעַד קְצֵה הָאָרֶץ, [28:64](https://ref.ly/Deut28:64), [Je 12:12](https://ref.ly/Jer12:12); [Gn 47:21](https://ref.ly/Gen47:21) מִקְצֵה גְּבוּל־מִצְרַיִם וְעַד־קָצֵהוּ, [1 K 6:24](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:24), [7:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:23) שְפָתוֺ מִשְּפָתוֺ וְעַד; [Lv 13:12](https://ref.ly/Lev13:12) מראשׁו ועד רגליו, [Dt 28:35](https://ref.ly/Deut28:35) ועד קדקדך מכף רגלך (similarly [2 S 14:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:25), [Jb 2:7](https://ref.ly/Job2:7), [Is 1:6](https://ref.ly/Isa1:6)). Cf. [1 S 9:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:2) מִשִּׁכְמוֺ וָמַ֫עְלָה, [2 S 5:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam5:9), [Jos 15:46](https://ref.ly/Josh15:46). **b.** metaph. not of actual space, but of *classes of objects,* to express idiomatically the idea of*comprehension*, so that the two preps. may be often represented by *both…and:* [Gn 19:4](https://ref.ly/Gen19:4) מִנַּעַר וְעַד־זָקֵן *from* young *to* old (i.e. both included) \= *both* young *and* old, v:[11](https://ref.ly/Gen19:11), [1 S 5:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:9) \+ מִקָּטֹן וְעַד־גָּדוֺל \= *both* great *and* small, [Je 6:13](https://ref.ly/Jer6:13) מִקְּטַנָּם וְעַד־גְּדוֺלָם *from* the least of them *even to* the greatest of them, ib. וְעַד־כֹּהֵן מִנָּבִיא *from* the proph. *even to* the priest every one dealeth falsely, [Ex 9:25](https://ref.ly/Exod9:25) \+ מאדם ועד בהמה *both* man *and* beast, [11:5](https://ref.ly/Exod11:5) עד…מבכור פרעה ´בכור השׁפחה וגו, [13:15](https://ref.ly/Exod13:15), [Nu 6:4](https://ref.ly/Num6:4), [Dt 29:10](https://ref.ly/Deut29:10) עד שֹׁאב מימיך מחֹטב עציך, [Jos 8:25](https://ref.ly/Josh8:25) \+ מאישׁ ועד אשׁה, [1 S 15:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:3) (4 pairs), [22:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:19), [1 K 5:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:13), [2 K 17:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:9) \= [18:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:8), [Is 10:18](https://ref.ly/Isa10:18) מנפשׁ ועד בשר \= *both* soul *and* body, [Je 9:9](https://ref.ly/Jer9:9); after a word implying a negative \= *either…or* [Gn 14:23](https://ref.ly/Gen14:23), [31:24](https://ref.ly/Gen31:24), [31:29](https://ref.ly/Gen31:29) מטוב עד רע *either* good *or* evil. The עַד may be repeated, if required: [Gn 6:7](https://ref.ly/Gen6:7) עד־בהמה עד רמש ועד־עוף השׁמים מאדם (so [7:23](https://ref.ly/Gen7:23)), [Ex 22:3](https://ref.ly/Exod22:3), [Jos 6:21](https://ref.ly/Josh6:21), Ju [15:5](https://ref.ly/Judg15:5) מִגָּדִישׁ וְעַד־קָמָה וְעַד־כֶּרֶם ָ\+זיִת, [20:48](https://ref.ly/Judg20:48), [1 S 30:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:19): without מן, [Lv 11:42](https://ref.ly/Lev11:42), [Nu 8:4](https://ref.ly/Num8:4). Similar is the use of מן in the idiom (וָמָֽטָּה) שָׁנָה וָמָֽעְלָה…מִבֶּן (also שָׁנָה …וְעַד־בֶּן): v. p. 122:a; and cf. [2 K 3:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:21) **c.** of *time,* [Gn 46:34](https://ref.ly/Gen46:34) מִנְּעֻרֵינוּ וְעַד עַתָּה *from* our youth even until now, [1 S 12:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:2), [Je 3:25](https://ref.ly/Jer3:25); [Nu 14:19](https://ref.ly/Num14:19); [Ex 10:6](https://ref.ly/Exod10:6); [18:13](https://ref.ly/Exod18:13) מן־הבֹקר עד־הערב, [1 S 30:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:17), [1 K 18:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:26); [Is 9:6](https://ref.ly/Isa9:6), [Psalm 90:2](https://ref.ly/Ps90:2); \+ often: with two inff. [Hos 7:4](https://ref.ly/Hos7:4): and implying *intervals* (not duration) Ez [4:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:10), [4:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:11) (cf. with לְ [Est 3:7](https://ref.ly/Esth3:7), [3:7](https://ref.ly/Esth3:7)). **d.** there occur further (1\) אֶ־ל …מִן, [Jos 15:21](https://ref.ly/Josh15:21), [Ex 26:28](https://ref.ly/Exod26:28) \= [36:33](https://ref.ly/Exod36:33) מִן־הַקָּצֶה אֶל־הַקָּצֶה *from* end *to* end, Ez [40:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:23), [40:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:27), [Ezr 9:11](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:11) מִפֶּה־אֶל־פֶּה; [Psalm 144:13](https://ref.ly/Ps144:13) מִזַּן אֶל זַן *from* kind *to* kind \= kinds of every sort; with a verb of motion, [Je 48:11](https://ref.ly/Jer48:11), [Psalm 105:13](https://ref.ly/Ps105:13), [1 Ch 17:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:5), and expressing a climax, [Psalm 84:8](https://ref.ly/Ps84:8) יֵֽלְכוּ מֵחַיִל אֶל־חַיִל, [Je 9:2](https://ref.ly/Jer9:2) (cf. [Rom 1:17](https://ref.ly/Rom1:17)): of time, מיום־אל־יום † [Nu 30:15](https://ref.ly/Num30:15), cf. [1 Ch 16:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:23) (‖ [Psalm 96:2](https://ref.ly/Ps96:2) לְ); implying *intervals,* מֵעֵת אֶל־עֵת † [1 Ch 9:25](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:25). (2\) לְ …מִן [Ex 32:27](https://ref.ly/Exod32:27), Ez [40:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:13), [Zc 14:10](https://ref.ly/Zech14:10): of time [Psalm 96:2](https://ref.ly/Ps96:2); [Jb 4:20](https://ref.ly/Job4:20): with *intervals,* [2 S 14:26](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:26). (3\) ה—ָ …מִן [Nu 34:10](https://ref.ly/Num34:10), Ez [6:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:14); מִמִּדְבָּר רִבְלָ֫תָה (so Ges Ew Hi etc.) *from* the wilderness *to* Riblah, [21:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:3), [25:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek25:13) מִתֵּימָן וּדְדָ֫נֶה (Co Kö Berthol); without —ָ [1 K 5:1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:1). And of time in the phrase (implying *intervals*) מִיָּמִים יָמִ֫ימָה [Ex 13:10](https://ref.ly/Exod13:10) \+ 4 t. **6\.** In *comparisons, beyond, above,* hence in Engl. *than:* so constantly: viz. **a.** when an object is compared with another distinct from itself, Ju [14:18](https://ref.ly/Judg14:18) מַה־מָּתוֺק מִדְּבַשׁ what is sweet *away from, beyond in excess of,* honey ? i.e. in our idiom, what is *sweeter than* honey ? [Lv 21:10](https://ref.ly/Lev21:10) הַכֹּהֵן הַגָּדוֺל מֵאֶחָיו the priest that is great *above* his brethren, [Nu 14:12](https://ref.ly/Num14:12), Ez [28:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:3) חָכָם מִדָּנִאֵל wiser *than* Daniel, [Psalm 119:72](https://ref.ly/Ps119:72) \+ very oft.; [Ho 2:9](https://ref.ly/Hos2:9) כִּי טוֺב לִי אָז מֵעָתָּה; with an inf. (sts. c. לְ) as subj., [Gn 29:10](https://ref.ly/Gen29:10) ´טוֺב תִּתִּי אֹתָהּ לְ מִתִּתִּי אֹתָהּ וגו better is my giving her to thee *than* my giving her to another, [Ex 14:12](https://ref.ly/Exod14:12), [1 S 15:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:22), [Pr 21:9](https://ref.ly/Prov21:9), [21:19](https://ref.ly/Prov21:19), [25:7](https://ref.ly/Prov25:7), [Psalm 118:8](https://ref.ly/Ps118:8), [118:9](https://ref.ly/Ps118:9) \+; with verbs, [Gn 19:9](https://ref.ly/Gen19:9) עתה נָרַע לְ מֵהֶם now we will harm thee *beyond* them, *more than* them, [25:23](https://ref.ly/Gen25:23) לְאֹם מִלְאֹם יֶאֱמַץ, [26:16](https://ref.ly/Gen26:16), [29:30](https://ref.ly/Gen29:30) את־רחל מִלֵּאָה ויאהב ג־ם, [38:26](https://ref.ly/Gen38:26) צָֽדְקָה מִמֶּנִּי, [48:19](https://ref.ly/Gen48:19) יִגְדַּל מִמֶּנּוּ, Ju [2:19](https://ref.ly/Judg2:19) השׁחיתו מאבותם they have done corruptly *more than* their fathers, [1 S 2:29](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:29), [2 S 20:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:6), [1 K 1:37](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:37), [14:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:9), [Je 5:3](https://ref.ly/Jer5:3), Ez [5:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:6) \+ oft.; \= *in, preference to, above,* [Psalm 45:8](https://ref.ly/Ps45:8), [52:5](https://ref.ly/Ps52:5), [52:5](https://ref.ly/Ps52:5), [Ho 6:6](https://ref.ly/Hos6:6), Hb [2:16](https://ref.ly/Hab2:16), after בחר [Je 8:3](https://ref.ly/Jer8:3), [Jb 7:15](https://ref.ly/Job7:15), [36:21](https://ref.ly/Job36:21), [Psalm 84:11](https://ref.ly/Ps84:11) (מִדּוּר) \+: note also נֹפֵל מִן [Jb 12:3](https://ref.ly/Job12:3), [13:2](https://ref.ly/Job13:2) \= inferior *to* (Ew Ges: al. Hi; al. De); שֹׁנֶה מִן different *from* [Est 1:7](https://ref.ly/Esth1:7), [3:8](https://ref.ly/Esth3:8). **b.** when an obj. is compared with a group or multitude of which it forms one, esp. with מִכֹּל, [Gn 3:1](https://ref.ly/Gen3:1) מִכֹּל חַיַּת הַשָּדֶה עָרוּם subtil *out of* all beasts of the field, or *beyond, above* all (other) beasts, v:[14](https://ref.ly/Gen3:14) cursed *above* all cattle (but without implying any judgment whether other cattle are cursed likewise), [37:3](https://ref.ly/Gen37:3) Israel loved Joseph מִכָּל־בָּנָיו *out of* all his sons, or *above* all his (other) sons, [Dt 7:7](https://ref.ly/Deut7:7), [33:24](https://ref.ly/Deut33:24) בָּרוּח מִבָּנִים אָשֵׁר blessed above sons be Asher, Ju [5:24](https://ref.ly/Judg5:24) תְּבֹרַח מִנָּשִׁים blessed *above* women be Jael, [1 S 9:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:2) b, [15:33](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:33) כֵּן תִּשְׁכַּל מִנָּשִׁים אִמְֶָּ, [18:30](https://ref.ly/1Sam18:30), [Je 17:9](https://ref.ly/Jer17:9) עָקֹב מִכֹּל deceitful *above* all things, [Psalm 45:3](https://ref.ly/Ps45:3), Ct [5:10](https://ref.ly/Song5:10) דָּגוּל מֵרְבָבָה (cf. בָּחַר מִן supr. **2 a** c: ex in eximius, egregius; II. 18:431 ὄσς̓, ἐμοὶ ἐκ πάσεων Κρονίδης Ζεὺς̀ ἄλγἐ ἔδωκεν, 3:227 ἔξοχος Ἀργείων…κεφαλήν). **c.** sometimes in poetry the idea on which מן is logically dependent, is unexpressed, and must be understood by the reader, [Is 10:10](https://ref.ly/Isa10:10) ופסיליהם מירושׁלם and their idols are (more) *than* (those of) Jerusalem, [Mi 7:4](https://ref.ly/Mic7:4) יָשָׁר מִמְּסוּכָה the uprightest is (sharper) *than a* thorn\-hedge (but We *from*), [Jb 11:7](https://ref.ly/Job11:7), [28:18](https://ref.ly/Job28:18); [Psalm 4:8](https://ref.ly/Ps4:8) מֵעֵת *beyond* (that of) the time when etc. [Ec 4:17](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:17), [9:17](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:17); cf. Ez [15:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek15:2). **d.** מן not unfrequently expresses the idea of a thing being *too much for a* person, or surpassing his powers: [Gn 18:14](https://ref.ly/Gen18:14) דָּבָר הֲיִפָּלֵא ´מי can a thing be *too hard for* J.? (so [Dt 17:8](https://ref.ly/Deut17:8) \+; cf. [Psalm 131:1](https://ref.ly/Ps131:1), [139:6](https://ref.ly/Ps139:6) \+ ), [32:11](https://ref.ly/Gen32:11) קָטֹנְתִּי מִן, [Dt 14:24](https://ref.ly/Deut14:24) כִּי יִרְבֶּה מִמְּ הַדֶּרֶח \= is *too great for* thee ([1 K 19:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:7)), [32:47](https://ref.ly/Deut32:47) רֵק מִכֶּם: so c. עָצַם [Gn 26:16](https://ref.ly/Gen26:16), כָּבֵד be *too heavy for* [Ex 18:18](https://ref.ly/Exod18:18), [Psalm 38:5](https://ref.ly/Ps38:5), מָח [Lv 27:8](https://ref.ly/Lev27:8) (too poor to pay), קָשָׁה [Dt 1:7](https://ref.ly/Deut1:7), צַר be *too narrow for,* [2 K 6:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:1), [Is 49:19](https://ref.ly/Isa49:19), קצר [Is 50:2](https://ref.ly/Isa50:2), אָמַץ [Psalm 18:18](https://ref.ly/Ps18:18), ירום נמני is *too high for* me [61:3](https://ref.ly/Ps61:3), גָּבַר [65:4](https://ref.ly/Ps65:4); הַֽמְעַט מִמְּ † [Jb 15:11](https://ref.ly/Job15:11) *too little for* thee ? הַֽמְעַט מִכֶּם † [Nu 16:9](https://ref.ly/Num16:9), [Is 7:13](https://ref.ly/Isa7:13), Ez [34:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:18) (v. מְעַט; also on [16:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:20)); oft. with an inf., as [Gn 4:13](https://ref.ly/Gen4:13) גָּדוֺל עֲוֺנִי מִנְּשוֺא is *greater than* I can bear, [30:7](https://ref.ly/Gen30:7) their substance was רַב מִשֶּׁבֶת יַחְדָּו lit. *great beyond* dwelling together \= *too great for* (their) dwelling together, [Ex 12:4](https://ref.ly/Exod12:4), Ju [7:2](https://ref.ly/Judg7:2) מִתִּתִּי רַב \= *too great for* me to give, [1 S 25:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:17) b, [1 K 8:64](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:64) רַב מֵהָכִיל, [Is 28:20](https://ref.ly/Isa28:20), [33:19](https://ref.ly/Isa33:19), [59:1](https://ref.ly/Isa59:1), Hb [1:13](https://ref.ly/Hab1:13), [Psalm 40:6](https://ref.ly/Ps40:6), [Ru 1:12](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:12): note esp. רַב מִן it is *more than that…* (\= there is enough of), [Ex 9:28](https://ref.ly/Exod9:28), [1 K 12:28](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:28), sq. subst. Ez [44:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:6) (v. רַב); and [Is 49:6](https://ref.ly/Isa49:6) נָקֵל מִהְיוֺתְ לִי ´עֶבֶד לְהָקִים וגו *too light for thy being* my servant was it to raise up, etc., Ez [8:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:17) ´הֲנָקֵל לְבֵית יִשְר ´מֵעֲשוֺת וגו was it *too trifling* to the house of Isr. *for* the doing etc. (less than the doing etc. deserved) ? (but ? rd. עֲשוֺת on anal. of [1 K 16:31](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:31)). **7\.** מן is prefixed to an *infin.:* **a.** with a *causal* force (rare), *from, on account of, through* (cf. **2 f** ): [Dt 7:7](https://ref.ly/Deut7:7), [7:8](https://ref.ly/Deut7:8) אֶתְכֶם ´מֵאַהֲבַת י *through* J.'s loving you \= *because* J. *loved* you, [9:28](https://ref.ly/Deut9:28) (cf. [1:27](https://ref.ly/Deut1:27) בְּ) [2 S 3:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:11) מִיִּרְאָתוֺ אֹתוֺ \= *because* he feared him, [Is 48:4](https://ref.ly/Isa48:4) …מִדַּעְתִּי \= *because* I knew… So in …מִבְּלִי, מִבִּלְתִּי. **b.** after verbs implying *restraint, prevention, cessation*, etc., as מִלֶּדֶת עֲצָרַנִי [Gn 16:2](https://ref.ly/Gen16:2) hath restrained me *from* bearing, וַתַּעֲמֹד מִלֶּדֶת [29:35](https://ref.ly/Gen29:35), [30:9](https://ref.ly/Gen30:9): so with חשך [20:6](https://ref.ly/Gen20:6) \+, חדל \+ [Ex 23:5](https://ref.ly/Exod23:5) \+, כִּלָּה \+ [34:33](https://ref.ly/Exod34:33) \+, נִכְלָא [36:6](https://ref.ly/Exod36:6), מָנַע [1 S 25:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:26) \+, שׁבת [Je 31:36](https://ref.ly/Jer31:36) \+, יָרֵא מֵהַבִּיט [Ex 3:6](https://ref.ly/Exod3:6) \+, שִׁמְרוּ מִן־הַחֵרֶם † [Jos 6:18](https://ref.ly/Josh6:18), [2 Ch 25:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:13) הֵשִׁיב מִלֶּכֶת. Esp. (*a*) after similar verbs, expressing concisely a *negative consequence*, lit. *away from…*, i.e. *so as not to, so that not:* [Gn 23:6](https://ref.ly/Gen23:6) b, [27:1](https://ref.ly/Gen27:1) Jacob's eyes were dim, מֵֽרְאֹת *away from* seeing \= *so that* he could not see, [31:29](https://ref.ly/Gen31:29) נִשְׁמַר מִן (‖ v:[24](https://ref.ly/Gen31:24) פֶּן), [Ex 14:5](https://ref.ly/Exod14:5), [Lv 26:13](https://ref.ly/Lev26:13), [Nu 32:7](https://ref.ly/Num32:7), [Dt 28:55](https://ref.ly/Deut28:55) (his eye shall be envious etc.) מִתֵּת *from* giving \= *so that he will not* give, Ju [6:27](https://ref.ly/Judg6:27), [1 S 21:31](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:31), [7:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam7:8) do not be silent from us מִזְּעֹק *so as not to* call (cf. [1 K 22:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:3)), [8:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:7) me have they rejected מִמְּלֽח עליהם, [16:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:1) ([Ho 4:6](https://ref.ly/Hos4:6)), [2 S 14:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:11) (rd. מֵהַרְבּוֺת), [Is 5:6](https://ref.ly/Isa5:6), [8:11](https://ref.ly/Isa8:11), [21:3](https://ref.ly/Isa21:3), [23:1](https://ref.ly/Isa23:1) is destroyed מִבּוֺא *so that none* can enter in, [24:10](https://ref.ly/Isa24:10), [33:15](https://ref.ly/Isa33:15), [44:18](https://ref.ly/Isa44:18), [49:15](https://ref.ly/Isa49:15), [54:9](https://ref.ly/Isa54:9), [56:6](https://ref.ly/Isa56:6), [58:13](https://ref.ly/Isa58:13), [Je 10:14](https://ref.ly/Jer10:14) מִדַּעַת …נִבְעַר is stupefied *so that he has no knowledge*, [13:14](https://ref.ly/Jer13:14), [33:21](https://ref.ly/Jer33:21), [33:24](https://ref.ly/Jer33:24), [33:26](https://ref.ly/Jer33:26), Ez [20:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:17), [Mi 3:6](https://ref.ly/Mic3:6), [Zc 7:11](https://ref.ly/Zech7:11), [7:12](https://ref.ly/Zech7:12), [Psalm 30:4](https://ref.ly/Ps30:4), Qr, [39:2](https://ref.ly/Ps39:2), [69:24](https://ref.ly/Ps69:24), [102:5](https://ref.ly/Ps102:5) (after שָׁכַח, peculiarly), [106:23](https://ref.ly/Ps106:23), [La 3:44](https://ref.ly/Lam3:44), [4:18](https://ref.ly/Lam4:18), [Jb 34:30](https://ref.ly/Job34:30) a, [2 Ch 36:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr36:13). So in חָלִילָה־לִי מֵעֲשוֺת *that I should not* do [Gn 44:17](https://ref.ly/Gen44:17) \+. (*b*) still more concisely, esp. in poetry, with a noun alone: [1 S 15:23](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:23) has rejected thee מִמֶּלֶח *from* (being) king (v:[26](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:26) מֶלֶח מִהְיוֺת), [Lv 26:43](https://ref.ly/Lev26:43) מֵהֶם, [Is 7:8](https://ref.ly/Isa7:8) יֵחַת מֵעָם shall be shattered *from* (being) a people, *that it be no more* a people, [17:1](https://ref.ly/Isa17:1) מוּסָר מֵעִיר, [25:2](https://ref.ly/Isa25:2) b, [52:14](https://ref.ly/Isa52:14) מֵאִישׁ מַרְאֵהוּ כֵּן מִשְׁחַת marred *away from* (being) a man('s), [Je 2:25](https://ref.ly/Jer2:25) מִיָּחֵף, [17:16](https://ref.ly/Jer17:16) מֵרֹעֶה, [48:2](https://ref.ly/Jer48:2) וְנַכְרִיתֶנָּה מִגּוֺי, v:[42](https://ref.ly/Jer48:42) מֵעָם, [Psalm 8:6](https://ref.ly/Ps8:6) hast made him lack but little מאלהים *ut non esset* Deus, [83:5](https://ref.ly/Ps83:5) מִגּוֺי, [Pr 30:2](https://ref.ly/Prov30:2) אָנֹכִי מֵאִישׁ בַּעַר *too* boorish *to be* a man: cf. [Dn 4:13](https://ref.ly/Dan4:13); [1 K 15:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs15:13) וַיְסִרֶהָ מִגְּבִירָה removed her *from* (being) queen\-mother, Ez [16:41](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:41) וִהִשְׁבַּתִּיח מִזּוֺנָה; and even to express absol. the non\-existence of a thing not named in the principal clause, as [Is 23:1](https://ref.ly/Isa23:1) שֻׁדַּד מִבַּיִת כִּי *so that there is no* house, Ez [12:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:19) ([32:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:15)), [25:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek25:9) מֵהֶעָרִים, [Ho 9:11](https://ref.ly/Hos9:11), [9:11](https://ref.ly/Hos9:11), [9:11](https://ref.ly/Hos9:11), [9:12](https://ref.ly/Hos9:12), [Mi 3:6](https://ref.ly/Mic3:6), [Hg 1:10](https://ref.ly/Hag1:10) מִטָּל, [Zc 7:14](https://ref.ly/Zech7:14), [9:8](https://ref.ly/Zech9:8), [Psalm 39:3](https://ref.ly/Ps39:3), [49:15](https://ref.ly/Ps49:15) מִזְּבֻל־לוֺ *so that it has no* dwelling, [52:7](https://ref.ly/Ps52:7) יִסָּֽחְֲ מֵאֹהֶל pluck thee up *tentless*, [109:24](https://ref.ly/Ps109:24) is lean מִשָּׁמֶן *so that there is no* fatness, [Jb 28:11](https://ref.ly/Job28:11), [33:21](https://ref.ly/Job33:21), [34:30](https://ref.ly/Job34:30) b. Oft. strengthened by pleon. אֵין or בְּלִי, as מֵאֵין יוֺשֵׁב [Is 5:9](https://ref.ly/Isa5:9), מִבְּלִי יוֺשֵׁב v. pp. 35a, 115:b. Cf. Köiii. 592 ff. **c.** with a *temporal* force, *since, after* (cf. **4**), chiefly late: [Nu 24:23](https://ref.ly/Num24:23) מִשֻּמוֺ אֵל *after* God's establishing him, [Is 44:7](https://ref.ly/Isa44:7) מִשּׂוּמִי עַם־עוֺלָם *since* I appointed the ancient people, [Ho 7:4](https://ref.ly/Hos7:4), [Hg 2:16](https://ref.ly/Hag2:16) (?) [Ru 2:18](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:18), [Ne 5:14](https://ref.ly/Neh5:14), [1 Ch 8:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:8) מִן־שִׁלְחוֺ אוֺתָם (cf. [6:16](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:16)) [2 Ch 31:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:10), [Dn 11:23](https://ref.ly/Dan11:23), [12:1](https://ref.ly/Dan12:1) (cf. [9:25](https://ref.ly/Dan9:25)), [Psalm 73:20](https://ref.ly/Ps73:20) כַּחֲלוֺם מֵהָקִיץ *after* (one's) awaking, [Jb 20:4](https://ref.ly/Job20:4). **8\.** Once [Dt 33:11](https://ref.ly/Deut33:11) as a **conj.** before a finite verb. Smite…his haters מִן־יְקוּמוּן *that* they rise not again, poet. for מִקּוּם (**7 b**): cf. Dr§ 41\.— On מֵאֲשֵׁר **adv.** and **conj.,** v. p. 84\. **9\.** In compounds:—**a.** מֵאַחֲרֵי, מֵאֵת, מִבֵּין, מִבַּלְעֲדֵי, מִבַּ֫עַד, מִלְּ (common in NH, esp. before inf.; in O.T. only in מִלְּבַד, מִלְּבוֺא (supr. **5 a**), מִלְמַ֫עְלָה, מִלְמַ֫טָּה, מִלְּעֻמַּת, מִלְּפָנִים, מִלִּפְנֵי, and, si vera l., מִלְמַעֲלֵה הֶחָרֶס [Jd 8:13](https://ref.ly/Judg8:13), v. מַעֲלֶה), מִנֶּגֶד, מֵעַל, מֵעִם, מִתַּ֫חַת; see אַחֲרֵי, אֵת, etc. **b.** לְמִן (לְמִנִּי † [Mi 7:12](https://ref.ly/Mic7:12), [7:12](https://ref.ly/Mic7:12)), with לְ pleon., *from* \= מִן, but used almost exclusively of the *terminus a quo*, whether of space or time (cf. מִן **5\.**);—(I) of *space*, [Dt 4:32](https://ref.ly/Deut4:32), Ju [20:1](https://ref.ly/Judg20:1) לְמִדַּן וְעַד־בְּאֵר שֶׁבַע, [Mi 7:12](https://ref.ly/Mic7:12), .[7:12](https://ref.ly/Mic7:12) (both לְמִנִּי), [Zc 14:10](https://ref.ly/Zech14:10), [1 Ch 5:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:9); לְמֵרָחוֺק *from* afar [Jb 36:3](https://ref.ly/Job36:3), [39:29](https://ref.ly/Job39:29) (on [Ezra 3:13](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:13), [2 Ch 26:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:15), v. sub עַד לְ); לְמִתַּחַת לְ \= *underneath* [1 K 7:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:32). Idiom., esp. sq. וְעַד, to denote comprehensively an entire class, לְמֵאִישׁ וְעַד בְּהֵמָה † [Ex 11:7](https://ref.ly/Exod11:7), [Je 51:62](https://ref.ly/Jer51:62) (לְמֵאָדָם), לְמֵאִישׁ וְעַד אִשָּׁה † [2 S 6:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:19), [2 Ch 15:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr15:13), לְמִקָּטֹן וְעַד גָּדוֺל † [2 K 23:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:2), [Je 42:8](https://ref.ly/Jer42:8), simil. [2 Ch 15:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr15:13), [Je 31:34](https://ref.ly/Jer31:34), [Est 1:5](https://ref.ly/Esth1:5), [1:20](https://ref.ly/Esth1:20), לְמֵרָע וְעַד־טוֺב † [2 S 13:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:22), וּלְמָטָּה לְמִבֶּן עֶשְרִים שָׁנָה † [1 Ch 27:23](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:23), cf. [2 Ch 25:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr25:5).—On לְמִבֵּית [Nu 18:7](https://ref.ly/Num18:7), see p. 110b. (2\) of *time* (oft. foll. by עַד), …לְמִן הַיּוֺם אֲשֶׁר *from* the day when…, † [Dt 4:32](https://ref.ly/Deut4:32), [9:7](https://ref.ly/Deut9:7), [2 S 7:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:11) (‖ [1 Ch 17:10](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:10) וּלְמִיָּמִים אֲשֶׁר) [Je 7:25](https://ref.ly/Jer7:25), [32:31](https://ref.ly/Jer32:31), [Hg 2:18](https://ref.ly/Hag2:18); …לְמִקְצָת הַיָּמִים אֲשֶׁר † [Dn 1:18](https://ref.ly/Dan1:18); לְמִן הַיּוֺם sq. inf. (in appos.) † [Ex 9:18](https://ref.ly/Exod9:18), [2 S 19:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:25); לְמִיּוֺםsq. inf. (genit.) † Ju [19:30](https://ref.ly/Judg19:30), [2 S 7:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:6), [Is 7:17](https://ref.ly/Isa7:17); …לְמִימֵי † [2 K 19:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:25) (‖ [Is 37:26](https://ref.ly/Isa37:26) מימי) [Mal 3:7](https://ref.ly/Mal3:7); עולם ועד עולם למ־ן † [Je 7:7](https://ref.ly/Jer7:7), [25:5](https://ref.ly/Jer25:5); לְמֵרָחוֺק *from* afar \= long before, [2 S 7:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:19) \= [1 Ch 17:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:17), [2 K 19:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:25) \= [Is 37:26](https://ref.ly/Isa37:26).

\[Str4487] †מָנָה **vb.** count, number, reckon, assign (NH *id.;* As. *manû* DlHWB 416; Ar. مَنَى *assign, apportion;* Aram. ܡܢܳܐ, מְנָא);—**Qal** *Pf.* מָנָה [Nu 23:10](https://ref.ly/Num23:10); וּמָנִ֫יתִי [Is 65:12](https://ref.ly/Isa65:12); *Impf.* 2 ms. תִּמְנֶ־ה [1 K 20:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:25); וַיִּמְנוּ [2 K 12:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:11); *Imv.* מְנֵה [2 S 24:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:1); *Inf. cstr.* לִמְנוֺת [Gn 13:16](https://ref.ly/Gen13:16) \+ 4 t.; *Pt.* מוֺנֶה [Je 33:13](https://ref.ly/Jer33:13), [Psalm 147:4](https://ref.ly/Ps147:4); —**1\.** *count, number* (cf. סָפַר) dust (grains) [Gn 13:16](https://ref.ly/Gen13:16) (J), cf. עֲפַר יַעֲקֹב [Nu 23:10](https://ref.ly/Num23:10) (JE); money [2 K 12:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:11); stars (מִסְפָּר לַכּוֺכָבִים ´מ) [Psalm 147:4](https://ref.ly/Ps147:4); flock [Je 33:13](https://ref.ly/Jer33:13) (symb. of peace); days [Psalm 90:12](https://ref.ly/Ps90:12); people, army [2 S 24:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:1), [1 Ch 21:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:1), [21:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:17), cf. [27:24](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:24) (object om.); [1 K 20:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:25). **2\.** *reckon, assign, appoint* לַחֶרֶב [Is 65:12](https://ref.ly/Isa65:12) (with play on מְנִי v:[11](https://ref.ly/Isa65:11)). **Niph.** *Pf.* נִמְנָה [Is 53:12](https://ref.ly/Isa53:12); *Impf.* יִמָּנֶה [Gn 13:16](https://ref.ly/Gen13:16), [1 K 3:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:8); יִמָּנוּ [1 K 8:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:5) \= [2 Ch 5:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:6); *Inf. cstr.* הִמָנוֺת [Ec 1:15](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:15); —**1\.** *be counted, numbered*, of descendants [Gn 13:16](https://ref.ly/Gen13:16) (J), of people [1 K 3:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:8) (‖נִסְפַּר); of sacrificial animals [1 K 8:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:5) \= [2 Ch 5:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:6) (both ‖ *id.*); indef. [Ec 1:15](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:15). **2\.** *be reckoned, assigned* (a place), of servant of ´י [Is 53:12](https://ref.ly/Isa53:12) (c. אֶת פּשְׁעִים). **Pi.** (late and poet.) (B Aram. and Targum. מַנִּי);—*Pf.* מִנָּה [Dn. 1:10](https://ref.ly/Dan1:10), [1:11](https://ref.ly/Dan1:11); מִנּוּ [Jb 7:3](https://ref.ly/Job7:3); *Impf.* וַיְמַן [Jon 2:1](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:1) \+ 4 t.; *Imv.* מַן [Psalm 61:8](https://ref.ly/Ps61:8); *appoint, ordain*, food [Dn 1:10](https://ref.ly/Dan1:10), v:[5](https://ref.ly/Dan1:5) (sq. לְ), officer v:[11](https://ref.ly/Dan1:11) (sq. על); nights of weariness (לֵילוֺת עָמָל) [Jb 7:3](https://ref.ly/Job7:3) (subj. indef.; sq. לְ); in story of Jonah, object. fish [Jon 2:1](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:1), ricinus [4:6](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:6), worm v:[7](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:7), wind v:[8](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:8) (all subj. ´י); obj. חֶסֶד וֶאֱמֶת [Psalm 61:8](https://ref.ly/Ps61:8) (subj. ´י). **Pu.** *Pt.* pl. מְמֻנִּים [1 Ch 9:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:29) *appointed*, of porters (sq. עַל).

> \[Str4490] †מָנָה **n.f.** part, portion;—´מ [Ex 29:26](https://ref.ly/Exod29:26) \+ 4 t.; (on מְנָֽ־ת [Je 13:25](https://ref.ly/Jer13:25), cf. [Psalm 16:5](https://ref.ly/Ps16:5), v. מְנָת); pl. מָנוֺת [1 S 1:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:4) \+ 5 t.; sf. מָֽנוֺתֶ֫הָ [Est 2:9](https://ref.ly/Esth2:9); —*part, portion,* esp. choice portion, [Ex 29:26](https://ref.ly/Exod29:26), [Lv 7:33](https://ref.ly/Lev7:33), [8:29](https://ref.ly/Lev8:29) (all of sacrificial animal; all P); cf. [2 Ch 31:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:19); also [1 S 1:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:4), [1:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:5); of distrib. of choice bits [Ne 8:10](https://ref.ly/Neh8:10), [8:12](https://ref.ly/Neh8:12), [Est 9:19](https://ref.ly/Esth9:19), [9:22](https://ref.ly/Esth9:22); cf. also [Est 2:9](https://ref.ly/Esth2:9) (v. > \[Str4488] †מָנֶה **n.m.** maneh, mina, a weight (perh. orig. *a specific part;* NH *id.;* As. *manû* DlHWB 417; Aram. ܡܰܢܝܳܐ, מַנְיָא);—´מ Ez [45:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:12); מָנִים [1 K 10:17](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:17) \+ 3 t.; \= 50 shekels Ez [45:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:12) (on חֲמִשִּׁים for חֲמִשָּׁה v. Hi Co); of gold [1 K 10:17](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:17); of silver [Ezr 2:69](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:69), [Ne 7:71](https://ref.ly/Neh7:71), [7:72](https://ref.ly/Neh7:72).—The weight of the mina was 1/60 of talent; i.e. acc. to older (Bab.) standard, 982\.2 grammes (\= 60 shekels at c. 16\.37 g.) \= c. 2 lbs.; in Ezekiel's time the mina \= 50 shekels \= 818\.6 g. \= c. 1 2/3 lb. (cf. כִּכָּר, שֶׁקֶל). Vid. J. BenzArch. l87 ff. 194 NowArch. i. 208 f.

## מנח (id: BDB05415)

מנח (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. مَنَحَ *lend, give a gift*, مِنْحَةٌ *loan, gift* esp. of she camel, sheep, or goat for milking, Ph. מנחת, *gift, offering*).

> \[Str4503] מִנְחָה:153 **n.f.** gift, tribute, offering (NH *id.*; Ph. מנחת; Targum מִנְחָתָא; on Ar.v. supr.);—abs. ´מ [Gn 4:3](https://ref.ly/Gen4:3) \+ 81 t.; cstr. מִנְחַת [Lv 2:7](https://ref.ly/Lev2:7) \+ 22 t.; sf. מִנְחָתִי [Gn 33:10](https://ref.ly/Gen33:10) \+ 3 t.; \+ 41 t. sfs. sg.; pl. sf. מִנְחֹתֶיְ [Psalm 20:4](https://ref.ly/Ps20:4); מִנְחֹתֵיבֶם [Nu 29:39](https://ref.ly/Num29:39), [Am 5:22](https://ref.ly/Amos5:22); —† **1\.** *gift, present* [Gn 32:14](https://ref.ly/Gen32:14), [32:19](https://ref.ly/Gen32:19), [32:21](https://ref.ly/Gen32:21), [32:22](https://ref.ly/Gen32:22) (E), [33:10](https://ref.ly/Gen33:10), [43:11](https://ref.ly/Gen43:11), [43:15](https://ref.ly/Gen43:15), [43:25](https://ref.ly/Gen43:25), [43:26](https://ref.ly/Gen43:26) (J), Ju [6:18](https://ref.ly/Judg6:18), [1 S 10:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:27), [1 K 10:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:25) \= [2 Ch 9:24](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:24), [2 K 8:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:8), [8:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:9), [20:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs20:12) \= [Is 39:1](https://ref.ly/Isa39:1), [Psalm 45:13](https://ref.ly/Ps45:13), [2 Ch 32:23](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:23). †**2\.** *tribute* Ju [3:15](https://ref.ly/Judg3:15), [3:17](https://ref.ly/Judg3:17), [3:18](https://ref.ly/Judg3:18), [3:18](https://ref.ly/Judg3:18), [2 S 8:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:2), [8:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:6) \= [1 Ch 18:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr18:2), [18:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr18:6), [1 K 5:1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:1), [2 K 17:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:3), [17:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:4), [2 Ch 17:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr17:5), [17:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr17:11), [26:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:8), [Ho 10:6](https://ref.ly/Hos10:6), [Psalm 72:10](https://ref.ly/Ps72:10). †**3\.** *offering* made to God, of any kind, whether grain or animals [Gn 4:3](https://ref.ly/Gen4:3), [4:4](https://ref.ly/Gen4:4), [4:5](https://ref.ly/Gen4:5), [Nu 16:15](https://ref.ly/Num16:15) (J), [1 S 2:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:17), [2:29](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:29), [26:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:19), [Is 1:13](https://ref.ly/Isa1:13), [Psalm 96:8](https://ref.ly/Ps96:8), [Zp 3:10](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:10); \+ (prob.) עֲלוֺת הַ֫מִּנְחָה(לַ) [1 K 18:29](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:29), [18:36](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:36), [2 K 3:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:20) (Ephr.; usually regarded as \= מִנְחַת עֶרֶב, but that much later usage). This gen. meaning poss. an also in foll.; זבח ומנחה [1 S 2:29](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:29) (see v:[29](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:29) b), [3:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:14), [Is 19:21](https://ref.ly/Isa19:21); עלה ומנחה [Je 14:12](https://ref.ly/Jer14:12); ‖עולה [Psalm 20:4](https://ref.ly/Ps20:4); ‖שֶׁלֶם [Am 5:22](https://ref.ly/Amos5:22) (עֹלוֺת gloss, to explain אָרִיחַ); or these may shew early stage of discrimination. †**4\.** *grain offering* (whether raw, roasted, ground to flour, or prepared as bread or cakes, see below. ## מנן (id: BDB05425)

מנן (of foll.; Ar. مَنَّ is  *praecidit* funem, *diminuit* numerum,*debilitavit;* also *be bounteous*, Qor 3:158 al., مَنٌّ *a gift;* whence ZerweckPräp. Min 4 *assumes* a primary mng. *to separate*—either *from* or *for* (\&ld;zutheilen\&rd;), as the case might be).

> \[Str4482] † II. \[מֵן] **n.\[m.]** portion (Sim Hup al.);—מִנֶּהָוּ [Psalm 68:24](https://ref.ly/Ps68:24), but rd. (הּ—ָ) מְנָתוֺ Ol Dy BI Che We (v. מְנָת supr.).—[Psalm 45:9](https://ref.ly/Ps45:9), 154:4, v. 1\. מְן supr.

> \[Str4511] † מִנִּית **n.pr.loc.** in Ammonite territory, site unknown (4 Rom. m. fr. Heshbon Euseb. :Onom. 253, 33; cf. BuhlGeogr. 266\);— Ju [11:33](https://ref.ly/Judg11:33) (LXX ἄχρις Ἀρνών, A εἰς Σεμωειθ, LXXL Σεμενειθ); ´חִטֵּי מ Ez [27:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:17) *wheat of Minnîth* (cf. wheat fr. Ammon [2 Ch 27:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr27:5)) so Berthol, but dub.; Co conj. חִטִּים וּנְכאֹת *wheat and storax*.

> \[Str4521] מְנָת v. מנה

> \[Str4522] †I. מַס **n. m.**:[1 K 5:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:27) usu. **coll.** body of forced labourers, task\-workers, labour\-band or gang, also (late) forced service, task\-work, serfdom ( Targum מִסָּא, esp. in phr. מַסְּקֵי מִיסִּין (implying late and \[for BH] false sense 'tribute,' so NH מַעֲלֵי מָסִּים; √ unknown, prob. loan\-wd.; follows anal. of ע“ע);—abs. ´מ [1 K 5:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:27) \+ ; לָמַָס [Jos 17:13](https://ref.ly/Josh17:13) \+; הַמַָּס [2 S 20:24](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:24) \+; pl. מִסִּים [Ex 1:11](https://ref.ly/Exod1:11); —**1\.** *labour\-band, labour\-gang,* body of task\-workers for public service, formed by levy upon the people by Sol. וַיַּ֫עַל הַמֶּלֶךְ שׁלֽמֹה מַס מִכָּל־יִשְרָאֵל [1 K 5:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:13), amounting to 30,000 men v:[27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:13); אֲשֶׁר הֶעֱלָה ´הַמּ [9:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:15); ´וַיַּעֲלֵם שׁ לְמַס עֹבֵד v:[21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:21) *and Sol. levied them for a toiling labour\-band* (cf. [Gn 49:15](https://ref.ly/Gen49:15), [Jos 16:10](https://ref.ly/Josh16:10)) \= ´לְמַס וַיַּעֲלֵם שׁ [2 Ch 8:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:8); appar. instituted, earlier, by David, cf. וַאֲדֹרָם עַל־הַמַָּס [2 S 20:24](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:24), i.e. had charge of the body of labourers, so [1 K 4:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:6), [5:28](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:28), [12:18](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:18) \= [2 Ch 10:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr10:18). **2\. a.** *of labour\-bands, slave\-gangs* of Isr. in Egypt, only שָרֵי מִסִּים [Ex 1:11](https://ref.ly/Exod1:11) (J) *gang\-overseers*. **b.** of conquered populations, subject to forced labour: יִהְיוּ לְ לָמַס וַעֲבָדוְּ [Dt. 20:11](https://ref.ly/Deut20:11) cf. Ju [1:30](https://ref.ly/Judg1:30), [1:33](https://ref.ly/Judg1:33), [1:35](https://ref.ly/Judg1:35) (v. GFM), [Is 31:8](https://ref.ly/Isa31:8), [La 1:1](https://ref.ly/Lam1:1); so ויהי לְמַס עֹבֵד [Jos 16:10](https://ref.ly/Josh16:10) (cf. [Gn 49:15](https://ref.ly/Gen49:15), [1 K 9:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:21)); וַיִתְּנוּ אֶת־הַכְּנַעֲנִי לָמַָס [Jos 17:13](https://ref.ly/Josh17:13), cf. (´וַיָּ֫שֶם וגו) Ju [1:28](https://ref.ly/Judg1:28). **3\.** in gen.: וַיְהִי לְמַס־עֹבֵד [Gn 49:15](https://ref.ly/Gen49:15) *and he* (Issachar) became *a slaving labour\-band* (poem in J; cf. [Jos 16:10](https://ref.ly/Josh16:10), [1 K 9:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:21)); cf. רְמִיָּה תִּהְיֶה לָמַָס [Pr 12:24](https://ref.ly/Prov12:24) (here appar. of individ.) **4\.** forced service, *serfdom* (or poss. (in late passage) *tribute \= enforced payment*, cf. NH, Targum, v. supr.), only of Ahasuerus: ´וַיָּשֶם הַמֶּלֶךְ מַס עַל־הָאָרֶץ וגו [Est 10:1](https://ref.ly/Esth10:1).

## מָסָה (id: BDB05444)

\[Str4529] †\[מָסָה] **vb.** melt, dissolve, be liquefied (Eth. መሰወ፡ *liquefy;* Aram. מְסָא *melt away,* ܡܣܳܐ *dissolve, decay, coagulate;* As. *masû* is *wash;* v. also מסס);—only **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 pl. הִמְסִיו [Jos 14:8](https://ref.ly/Josh14:8) (on form v. Ges§ 75 R.17\). *Impf.* sf . יַּעמסֵם [Psalm 147:18](https://ref.ly/Ps147:18); 2 ms. וַתֶּ֫מֶס [Psalm 39:12](https://ref.ly/Ps39:12); אַמְסֶה [Psalm 6:7](https://ref.ly/Ps6:7); —*cause to dissolve, melt*, ice [Psalm 147:18](https://ref.ly/Ps147:18); hyperb. בְּדִמְעָתִי עַרְשִׁי אַמְּסֶה [Psalm 6:7](https://ref.ly/Ps6:7) *with my tears I cause my bed to dissolve;* וַתֶּמֶס כָּעָשׁ חֲמוּדוֺ [Psalm 39:12](https://ref.ly/Ps39:12) \= *consume, cause to vanish;* fig. sq. לֵב \= *intimidate* [Jos 14:8](https://ref.ly/Josh14:8).

## מָסַךְ (id: BDB05450)

\[Str4537] †מָסַךְ vb. mix, produce by mixing (cf. perh. מזג, מֶזֶג Baes 33, 51\);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. ´מ [Is 19:14](https://ref.ly/Isa19:14); מָֽסְכָה [Pr 9:2](https://ref.ly/Prov9:2); מָסָכְתִּי [Pr 9:5](https://ref.ly/Prov9:5), [Psalm 102:10](https://ref.ly/Ps102:10); *Inf. cstr.* מְסֹךְ [Is 5:22](https://ref.ly/Isa5:22); —**1\.** *pour*, only fig., בְּקִרְבָּהּ ´מ רוּחַ עִוְעִים [Is 19:14](https://ref.ly/Isa19:14) (subj. ´מ); *mix,* c. acc. שֵׁכָר [Is 5:22](https://ref.ly/Isa5:22); i.e. make a choice drink by mixing with spices, etc. (mixing with water came later, cf. 2 Macc 15:39) Löwp. 419 Frä162 f, 171 f. NowArch. 1, 120 BenzArch. 95; obj. יַיִן fig., subj. wisdom [Pr 9:2](https://ref.ly/Prov9:2), [9:5](https://ref.ly/Prov9:5); שִׁקֻּוַי בִּבְכִי מָסָכְתִּי [Psalm 102:10](https://ref.ly/Ps102:10) *my drink with weeping have I mixed* (v. As. \[*akâl*] *ul âkul, bikîtum kurmati, mê ul aštî, dîmtu maštîtî* ZimBP 34\).

> \[Str4538] †מֶ֫סֶךְ **n.\[m.]** mixture, i.e. wine mixed with spices; ´וְיַיִן חָמַר מָלֵא מ ´כּוֺס בְּיַד י [Psalm 75:9](https://ref.ly/Ps75:9) (fig. of ´י's judgment).

\[Str4549] †\[מָסַס] **vb.** dissolve, melt, intr. (Targum מְסַס; cf. מסה, and II. מאס);—**Qal.** *Inf. cstr.* מְסֹס נֹסֵס [Is 10:18](https://ref.ly/Isa10:18); *like the melting* (wasting away) of a נֹסֵס (q.v.) **Niph.** *Pf.* וְנָמֵס consec. Ez [21:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:12); וְנָמָס [Ex 16:21](https://ref.ly/Exod16:21), [Psalm 112:10](https://ref.ly/Ps112:10); נָמַ֫סּוּ [Psalm 97:5](https://ref.ly/Ps97:5) \+ 2 t; *Impf.* יִמַּס [Dt 20:8](https://ref.ly/Deut20:8) \+; וַיִּמַּסּוּ Ju [15:14](https://ref.ly/Judg15:14); *Inf. abs.* הִמֵּס [2 S 17:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:10); *cstr. id.* [Psalm 68:3](https://ref.ly/Ps68:3); *Pt.* נָמֵס [Psalm 22:15](https://ref.ly/Ps22:15) \+ 2 t.;—**1\.** *melt* (intr.) of manna [Ex 16:21](https://ref.ly/Exod16:21); wax [Psalm 68:3](https://ref.ly/Ps68:3) (sim. of wicked); manacles Ju [15:14](https://ref.ly/Judg15:14) (\= *vanished, dropped off*); wicked [Psalm 112:10](https://ref.ly/Ps112:10) (*melt away*); especially of hills and mts. [Mi 1:4](https://ref.ly/Mic1:4), [Psalm 97:5](https://ref.ly/Ps97:5), [Is 34:3](https://ref.ly/Isa34:3). **2\.** most oft. fig. of heart \= *faint, grow fearful,* [Na 2:11](https://ref.ly/Nah2:11), [2 S 17:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:10), [17:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:10) (cf. Dr), [Dt 20:8](https://ref.ly/Deut20:8), [Jos 2:11](https://ref.ly/Josh2:11), [5:1](https://ref.ly/Josh5:1), [7:5](https://ref.ly/Josh7:5), [Is 13:7](https://ref.ly/Isa13:7), [19:1](https://ref.ly/Isa19:1), Ez [21:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:12), [Psalm 22:15](https://ref.ly/Ps22:15). **3\.** *Pt.* \= *wasted, consumptive* (?), [1 S 15:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:9) (‖נְמִבְזָה, rd. נִבְזָה v. בזה **Niph.**) \< rd. נִמְאֶסֶת *worthless*, lit. *rejected* (v. מאס **Niph.**) **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 pl. הֵמַסּוּ אֶת־לְבָבֵנוּ [Dt 1:28](https://ref.ly/Deut1:28) *cause to melt* ( \= intimidate).
