## מעז (id: BDB05491)

מעז (√ of following). p. 734–35

> \[Str4590] †מַֽעַזְיָהוּ, מַֽעַזְיָה **n.pr.m.** of priests. **1\.** [1 Ch 24:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:18) ascribed to David's time, A Μοοζαλ, LXXL Μοοζια. **2\.** [Neh 10:9](https://ref.ly/Neh10:9), Nehemiah's time, A Μααζεια, LXXL Μααζίας.

* **Associated Passages:** Nehemiah 10:9; 1 Chronicles 24:18


## מָעַט (id: BDB05493)

\[Str4591] †\[מָעַט] **vb.** be or become small, diminished, few (NH, Talm., *id.;* Ar. مَعِطَ is *to be without hair on the body*)—**Qal** *Impf.* יִמְעַט [Ex 12:4](https://ref.ly/Exod12:4), 2 mpl. תִּמְעָטוּ [Je 29:6](https://ref.ly/Jer29:6), 3 mpl. יִמְעָטוּ [Is 21:17](https://ref.ly/Isa21:17) \+, וַיִּמְעֲטוּ [Psalm 107:39](https://ref.ly/Ps107:39); *Inf.* מְעֹט [Lv 25:16](https://ref.ly/Lev25:16); —[Ex 12:4](https://ref.ly/Exod12:4) אִס־יִמְעַט הַבַּיִת מִהְיוֺת מִשֶּה if the house be *too small* for a lamb; [Ne 9:32](https://ref.ly/Neh9:32) אַל־יִמְעַט לְפָנֶיְ אֵת כָּל־הַתְּלָאָה (cf. Ges§ 117 l); of a people, [Je 29:6](https://ref.ly/Jer29:6) וּרְבוּ שָׁם וְאַל־תִּמְעָטוּ and *become* not *few*, [30:19](https://ref.ly/Jer30:19) וְהִרְבִּתִים וְלאֹ יִמְעָ֔טוּ, [Is 21:17](https://ref.ly/Isa21:17), [Psalm 107:39](https://ref.ly/Ps107:39); [Pr 13:11](https://ref.ly/Prov13:11) הוֺן מֵהֶבֶל יִמְעָט *diminishes* (opp. יַרְבֶּה); [Lv 25:16](https://ref.ly/Lev25:16) וּלְפִי מְעֹט הַשָּׁנִים acc. to the *fewness* of the years. **Pi.** *become few* (Ges§ 52 k), *Pf.* 3 pl. [Ec 12:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:3) וּבָֽטְלוּ הַטֹּחֲנוֺת כִּי מִעֵ֔טוּ. **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 fs. consec. וְהִמְעִ֫יטָה (Ges§ 49 k), [Lv 26:22](https://ref.ly/Lev26:22), 1 S. with sf. וְהִמְעַטְתִּים Ez [29:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:15); *Impf.* 2 ms. תַּמְעִיט [Lv 25:16](https://ref.ly/Lev25:16), תַּמְעִטֵ֫נִי [Je 10:34](https://ref.ly/Jer10:34), 2 fs. תַּמְעִ֫יטִי [2 K 4:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:3), etc.;—*make small* or *few, diminish:* **a.** [Lv 26:22](https://ref.ly/Lev26:22) (of the sword) וְהִמְעִ֫יטָה אֶתְכֶם, Ez [29:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:15) וְהִמְעַטְתִּים, [Je 10:24](https://ref.ly/Jer10:24) אַל־בְּאַפְּ פֶּ־ן תַּמְעִטֵנִי lest thou *make me* (the people) *small*, [Psalm 107:38](https://ref.ly/Ps107:38) וּבְהֶמְתָּם לאֹ יַמְעִיט; [Lv 25:16](https://ref.ly/Lev25:16) וּלְפִי מְעֹט הַשָּׁנִים תַּמְעִיט מִקְנָתוֺ acc. to the smallness (fewness) of the years, thou shalt *make small* its price, [Nu 26:54](https://ref.ly/Num26:54) לָרַב תַּרְבֶּה נַחֲלָתוֺ וְלַמְעַט תַּמְעִיט נַחֲלָתוֺ and to the small (few) thou shalt *make small* his inheritance (so [33:54](https://ref.ly/Num33:54)). **b.** qualifying an *action* (sts. to be understood from the context), [Ex 16:17](https://ref.ly/Exod16:17) וַיִּלְקְטוּ הַמַּרְבֶּה וְהַמַּמְעִיט and they gathered, he that made much, and he that *made little* (sc. לִלְקֹט), v:[18](https://ref.ly/Exod16:18), [Nu 11:32](https://ref.ly/Num11:32) עשרה חמרים הממעיט אסף, [2 K 4:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:3) כֵּלִים רֵקִים אַל־תַּמְעִיטִי empty vessels *make not few* (sc. in borrowing); [Ex 30:15](https://ref.ly/Exod30:15) הֶעָשִׁיר לאֹ יַרְבֶּה וְהַדַּל לאֹ יַמְעִיט מִמַּחֲצִית הַשָּׁקֶל ´לָתֵת וגו *shall not diminish* from the half shekel to give, i.e. shall not give less, [Nu 35:8](https://ref.ly/Num35:8) מֵאֵת הָרַב תַּרְבּוּ וּמֵאֵת הַמְעַט תַּמְעִיטוּ and from the few ye shall *make few* (sc. לָתֵת), i.e. ye shall give few (cities).

> \[Str4592] מְעַט:101 **subst.** a little, fewness, a few;—´מ abs. and cstr., מְעָט [Is 10:7](https://ref.ly/Isa10:7) \+, pl. מְעַטִּים †[Psalm 109:8](https://ref.ly/Ps109:8), [Ec 5:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:1); —**1\. a.** [Gn 30:30](https://ref.ly/Gen30:30) מעט אשׁר היה לך לְפָנַי *the little* that thou hadst, [47:9](https://ref.ly/Gen47:9) מעט ורעים היו ימי שׁני חיי *few* and evil, [Lv 25:52](https://ref.ly/Lev25:52), [Nu 26:54](https://ref.ly/Num26:54) (cf. [33:54](https://ref.ly/Num33:54)) וְלַמְעַט תמעיט נחלתו and *to the small* (few) thou shalt make small his inheritance, v:[56](https://ref.ly/Num26:56), [35:8](https://ref.ly/Num35:8) וּמֵאֵת הַֽמְעַט תמעיטו, [Dt 7:7](https://ref.ly/Deut7:7) וכי אתם הַֽמְעַט מכ־ל העמים ye are *the fewest* out of all peoples, [1 S 14:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:6) לְהוֺשִׁיעַ בְּרַב אוֺ בִמְעָט, [Je 42:2](https://ref.ly/Jer42:2) כִּי נִשְׁאַרְנוּ מְעַט מֵהַרְבֵּה we are left *as few* out of many, Ez [5:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:3) וְלָקַחְתָּ מִשָּׁם מְעַט בְּמִסְפָּר, [Hg 1:6](https://ref.ly/Hag1:6) זְרַעְתֶּם הַרְבֵּה וְהָבֵא מְעָט (cf. v:[9](https://ref.ly/Hag1:9)), [Psalm 8:6](https://ref.ly/Ps8:6) ותחסּרהו מעט מאלהים, [37:16](https://ref.ly/Ps37:16) טוֺב מְעַט לַצַּדִּיק (cf. [Pr 15:16](https://ref.ly/Prov15:16), [16:8](https://ref.ly/Prov16:8)), [2 Ch 29:34](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:34) רַק הַכֹּהֲנִים הָיוּ לִמְעָט; as pred. [Nu 13:18](https://ref.ly/Num13:18) הַמְעַט הוּא אִם־רָב, [Jos 7:3](https://ref.ly/Josh7:3) כִּי מְעַט הֵמָּה, [Jb 10:20](https://ref.ly/Job10:20), [Ru 2:7](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:7), [Ne 7:4](https://ref.ly/Neh7:4); [Is 16:14](https://ref.ly/Isa16:14) וּשְׁאָר מְעַט מִזְעָר.—[Ho 8:10](https://ref.ly/Hos8:10) וַָיָּחֵלּוּ מְעַט and they begin *as littleness* ( \= *to be minished*) because of, \&c.; but rd. prob. (LXX) וְיֶחְדְּלוּ מְעַט מִמְּשֹׁחַ and they shall cease *for a little* (**d**) from anointing, etc. **b.** as subst. with foll. gen., [Gn 18:4](https://ref.ly/Gen18:4) מְעַט מַיִם *a little of water,* [24:17](https://ref.ly/Gen24:17), [24:43](https://ref.ly/Gen24:43), מְעַט אֹכֶל [43:2](https://ref.ly/Gen43:2), [44:25](https://ref.ly/Gen44:25), [1 S 14:43](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:43), [17:28](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:28) מְעַט הַצּאֹן הָהֵ֫נָּה lit. *the fewness of* those sheep, [Pr 6:10](https://ref.ly/Prov6:10) \+; as gen. to a previous subst., מְתֵי מְעָט men *of fewness* †[Dt 26:5](https://ref.ly/Deut26:5), [28:62](https://ref.ly/Deut28:62); in appos., † [Is 10:7](https://ref.ly/Isa10:7) לְהַכְרִית גּוֺיִם לאֹ מְעָט, [Ezr 9:8](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:8) מִחְיָה מְעַט, [Ne 2:12](https://ref.ly/Neh2:12) וַאֲנָשִׁים מְעַט עִמִּי, [Ec 9:4](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:4); either gen. or appos., [10:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:1), [Dn 11:34](https://ref.ly/Dan11:34) יֵעָֽזְרוּ עֵזֶר מְעָט with a help *of smallness.* **c.** twice declined as an adj., [Psalm 109:8](https://ref.ly/Ps109:8) יִהְיוּ יָמָיו מְעַטִּים, [Ec 5:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:1) על־כן יהיו דבריך מְעַטִּים. **d.** as adverb. acc., of place, [2 S 16:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:1) וְדָוִד עֹבֵר מְעַט; of time, [Jb 10:20](https://ref.ly/Job10:20) b; of degree, [2 K 10:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:18) אַחְאָב עָבַד אֶת־הַבַּעַל מְעָט, Ez [11:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:16) and have been to them for a sanctuary *but little*, [Zc 1:15](https://ref.ly/Zech1:15); repeated מְעַט מְעַט *by little, by little* \= gradually, [Ex 23:30](https://ref.ly/Exod23:30), [Dt 7:22](https://ref.ly/Deut7:22). **e.** phrases:—*a.* with the interr. הֲ, † [Gn 30:15](https://ref.ly/Gen30:15) הַֽמְעַט קַחְתֵּח אֶ־ת אִישִׁי וְלָקַחַת was thy taking my husband (too) little? and (wilt thou be) for taking also my son's love\-apples? [Nu 16:9](https://ref.ly/Num16:9)–[10](https://ref.ly/Num16:10) הַֽמְעַט מִכֶּם כִּי וּבִקַּשְׁתֶּם גַּם כְּהֻנָּה …הִבְדִּיל is it *too little for you* that J. hath separated, etc., ... and do ye seek, etc.? v:[13](https://ref.ly/Num16:13) כִּי תִשְתָּרֵר גַּם הִשְתָּרֵר …הַֽמְעַט כִּי הֶעֱלִיתָנוּ, [Jos 22:17](https://ref.ly/Josh22:17) f. ´וְאַתֶּם תָּשׁוּבוּ וגו …הַֽמְעַט לָנוּ, [Is 7:13](https://ref.ly/Isa7:13) ´הַֽמְעַט מִכֶּם הַלְאוֺת אֲנָשִׁים כִּי תַּלְאוּ וגו is wearying men *too little* for you, that you will weary also my God? Ez [34:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:18) (as Ew Co AVRV), [Jb 15:11](https://ref.ly/Job15:11) הַֽמְעַט מִמְּ תַּנְחוּמוֺת אֵל. But Ez [16:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:20) (si vera l.) was it (too) little *because of* thy whoredom? (was that insufficient for thee? Kö: iii. 406 m). *b.* ´עוֺד מְעַט וְ, †[Ex 17:4](https://ref.ly/Exod17:4) עוֺד מְעַט וּסְקָלֻנִי *yet a little, and* they will stone me, [Je 51:33](https://ref.ly/Jer51:33), [Ho 1:4](https://ref.ly/Hos1:4), [Psalm 37:10](https://ref.ly/Ps37:10); ´עוֺד מְעַט מִזְעָר וְ †[Is 10:25](https://ref.ly/Isa10:25), [29:17](https://ref.ly/Isa29:17); cf. [Hg 2:6](https://ref.ly/Hag2:6), [Jb 24:24](https://ref.ly/Job24:24).
> 
> **2\.** † כִּמְעַט *like a little:* hence **a.** *within a little, almost* (cf. ὀλίγου), [Gn 26:10](https://ref.ly/Gen26:10) …כִּמְעַט שָׁכַב *almost* had lain ..., [Psalm 73:2](https://ref.ly/Ps73:2) Qr, [94:17](https://ref.ly/Ps94:17), [119:87](https://ref.ly/Ps119:87), [Pr 5:14](https://ref.ly/Prov5:14) (all sq. pf.); Ez [16:47](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:47) כִּמְעַט קָט וַתַּשְׁחִתִי מֵהֶן *like a little*, and thou hadst done more corruptly than they; Ct [3:4](https://ref.ly/Song3:4) כִּמְעַט שֶׁעָבַרְתִּי מֵהֶם \= *hardly* had I passed. **b.** with impf., *just,* [2 S 19:37](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:37) כמעט יעבר עבדך *just* would thy servant pass over; *lightly, easily, quickly* (cf. δἰ ὀλίγου), [Psalm 2:12](https://ref.ly/Ps2:12) כִּי יִבְעַר כִּמְעַט אַפּוֺ, [81:15](https://ref.ly/Ps81:15), [Jb 32:22](https://ref.ly/Job32:22). **c.** \= *shortly,* [2 Ch 12:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr12:7). **d.** \= *little worth*, [Pr 10:20](https://ref.ly/Prov10:20) כמעט ולב רשׁעים. *e.* pleon. for מְעַט, [Is 1:9](https://ref.ly/Isa1:9) (accents) כִּמְעָט שָרִיר a *little* remnant, [Psalm 105:12](https://ref.ly/Ps105:12) (\= [1 Ch 16:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:19)) כִּמְעַט וְגָרִים בָּהּ; כִּמְעַט רֶגַע *like the littleness of* a moment \= *for a little* moment, [Is 26:20](https://ref.ly/Isa26:20), [Ezra 9:8](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:8).

## מָעַךְ (id: BDB05501)

\[Str4600] † \[מָעַךְ] **vb.** press, squeeze (NH *id.;* Targum מְעַח; Ar. مَعَكَ *rub*);—**Qal** *Pt. pass.* וּמָעוּח וְכָתוּת וְנָתוּק וְכָרוּת [Lv 22:24](https://ref.ly/Lev22:24) (H), not to be offered to ´י, reference to emasculation (cf. Di); וַחֲנִיתוֺ מְעוּכָה־בָאָרֶץ [1 S 26:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:7) *and his spear pressed* (thrust) *into the ground.* **Pu.** *Pf.* 3 mpl. שָׁמָּה מֹעֲכוּ שְׁדֵיהֶן Ez [23:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:3) *there were their breasts squeezed* (‖עִשּוּ; unchastely; fig. of intercourse of Samaria and Jerus. with Egypt, involving idolatry).

## מָעַל (id: BDB05506)

\[Str4603] † מָעַל **vb.** act unfaithfully, treacherously, a priestly word (P Ez. Ch.), chiefly late (NH *id., act unfaithfully;* Ar. مَغَلَ *whisper, backbite*, مَغَالَةٌ *perfidy, fraud*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. מָעַל [Jos 22:20](https://ref.ly/Josh22:20) \+ 4 t.; 3 fs. מָֽעֲלָה [Lv 5:21](https://ref.ly/Lev5:21), [Nu 5:12](https://ref.ly/Num5:12) \+ 13 t. Pf.; *Impf.* יִמְעַל [Pr 16:10](https://ref.ly/Prov16:10), [2 Ch 26:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:16); 3 fs תִּמְעֹל [Lv 5:15](https://ref.ly/Lev5:15), [Nu 5:27](https://ref.ly/Num5:27); 3 mpl. יִמְעֲלוּ [Jos 7:1](https://ref.ly/Josh7:1), וַיִּמְעֲלוּ [1 Ch 5:25](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:25); 2 mpl. תִּמְעָלוּ [Ne 1:8](https://ref.ly/Neh1:8); *Inf. cstr.* לִמְעֹל, so rd. for HCT למסר [Nu 31:16](https://ref.ly/Num31:16) Thes Di;—*act unfaithfully, treacherously*, usu. **1\.** מָעַל מַ֫עַל: **a.** abs. בַּחֵרֶם *in the matter of the devoted thing* (the sin of Achan) [Jos 7:1](https://ref.ly/Josh7:1) (JE), [22:20](https://ref.ly/Josh22:20) (P), cf. [Lv 5:15](https://ref.ly/Lev5:15) (P), [2 Ch 36:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr36:14), Ez [14:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:13), [15:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek15:8), [18:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:24). **b.** wife against (בְּ) husband [Nu 5:12](https://ref.ly/Num5:12), [5:27](https://ref.ly/Num5:27) (P; marital infidelity). **c.** elsewhere ag. (בְּ) God, [Lv 26:40](https://ref.ly/Lev26:40) (H), [5:21](https://ref.ly/Lev6:2), [Nu 5:6](https://ref.ly/Num5:6), [31:16](https://ref.ly/Num31:16) (?) [Jos 22:16](https://ref.ly/Josh22:16), [22:31](https://ref.ly/Josh22:31) (all P), [1 Ch 10:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr10:13), [28:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:19), Ez [17:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:20), [20:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:27), [39:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:26), [Dn 9:7](https://ref.ly/Dan9:7). **2\.** usage without מַעַל (only late): **a.** במשׁפט לא ימעל פיו [Pr 16:10](https://ref.ly/Prov16:10) *let not his* (the king's) *mouth act treacherously against justice* (RV *in judgment*). **b.** elsewhere against God, c. בְּ, [Dt 32:51](https://ref.ly/Deut32:51) (P), [1 Ch 5:25](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:25), [12:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:2), [26:22](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:22), 30:7, [Ezr 10:2](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:2), [Ne 13:27](https://ref.ly/Neh13:27), Ez [39:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:23); without בְּ, [2 Ch 26:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:18), [29:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:6), [Ezr 10:10](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:10), [Ne 1:8](https://ref.ly/Neh1:8); בַּחֵרֶם [1 Ch 2:7](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:7).

[Is 41:12](https://ref.ly/Isa41:12); [Ec 9:15](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:15) *they found in it* \= there happened to be in it; אֶת־לִבּוֺ לְהִתְפַּלֵּל ´מ [2 S 7:27](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:27) *he found the heart* (took courage) *to pray* \= [1 Ch 17:25](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:25) (om. את־לבו, perh. ancient text. err.); לאֹ מָֽצְאוּ יְדֵיהֶם [Psalm 76:6](https://ref.ly/Ps76:6) *they did not find their hands,* were not able to use them, were paralyzed; חַיַּת יָדֵךְ מָצָאתְ [Is 57:10](https://ref.ly/Isa57:10) \= (perh.) *experience renewal of thy strength* (cf. [Jos 17:16](https://ref.ly/Josh17:16), [Zc 10:10](https://ref.ly/Zech10:10)).

## מָצָה (id: BDB05554)

\[Str4680] †\[מָצָה] **vb.** drain, drain out (NH *id. press,* or *drain out;* Aram. ܡܨܳܐ, מְצָא *id.;* Ar. مصو II. *drain to the last drop* (Dozy); cf. מָצַץ);—**Qal** *Pf.* 2 fs. מָצִית [Is 51:17](https://ref.ly/Isa51:17), וּמָצִית consec. Ez [23:34](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:34) *Impf.* 3 ms. apoc. וַיִמֶץ Ju [6:38](https://ref.ly/Judg6:38), 3 mpl יִמְצוּ [Psalm 75:9](https://ref.ly/Ps75:9); —*drain, drain out,* וַיִמֶץ טַל מִן־הַנִּזָּה Ju [6:38](https://ref.ly/Judg6:38) *and drained dew out of the fleece;* so fig., obj. קֻבַּעת *bowl, goblet,* of bewilderment fr. ´י, [Is 51:17](https://ref.ly/Isa51:17) ( \+ שׁתה; of personif. Jerusalem), cf., obj. בּוֺפ, Ez [23:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:24) (\+ שׁתה); obj. שְׁמָרֶיהָ *the dregs of it* (viz. בּוֺמ) [Psalm 75:9](https://ref.ly/Ps75:9) (subj. the wicked). **Niph.** *Pf.* וְנִמְצָה consec. [Lv 1:15](https://ref.ly/Lev1:15) *and* the blood (of the bird) *shall be drained out;* so *Impf.* יִמָּצֵה [5:9](https://ref.ly/Lev5:9) (both **P**); מֵי מָלֵא יִמָּצוּ לָמוֺ [Psalm 73:10](https://ref.ly/Ps73:10) *water of abundance is drained* (i.e. drunk) *by them* (Gr Che Buhl rd. יִמְצוּ or יָמֹצוּ (מצץ) *they drain*).

## מָקַק (id: BDB05603)

\[Str4743] † \[מָקַק] **vb.** decay, rot, fester, fig. pine away (NH *id.;* Aram. (Targum) מְקַק);—**Niph.** *Pf.* 3 pl. נָמַ֫קּוּ [Psalm 38:6](https://ref.ly/Ps38:6), ´וְנ consec. [Is 34:4](https://ref.ly/Isa34:4), Ez [4:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:17); 2 mpl. וּנֶמַקֹּתֶם consec. Ez [24:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:23); *Impf.* 3 fs. תִּמַּק [Zc 14:12](https://ref.ly/Zech14:12); 3 mpl. יִמַּ֫קּוּ [Lv 26:39](https://ref.ly/Lev26:39), יִמָּקּוּ v:[39](https://ref.ly/Lev26:39); 3 fpl. תִּמַּקְנָה [Zc 14:12](https://ref.ly/Zech14:12); *Pt.* pl. נְמַקִּים Ez [33:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:10); —**1\.** *fester*, of wounds [Psalm 38:6](https://ref.ly/Ps38:6) ( \+ הבאישׁ, v. באשׁ). **2\.** *rot, rot away,* :עֵתנָיו תִּמַּקְנָה בְחֹרֵיהֶן וּלְשׁוֺנוֺ תִּמִק בְּפִיהֶ֫ם [Zc 14:12](https://ref.ly/Zech14:12) (plague upon foes of Jerusalem). **3**. *moulder away*, of צְבָא הַשָּׁמַיִם [Is 34:4](https://ref.ly/Isa34:4) (‖וְנָנֹלוּ כַּסֵּפֶר הַשָּׁמַיִם. **4**. of *pining away* by reason of (בְּ), as punishment for, iniquity Ez [4:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:17), [24:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:23), [33:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:10), [Lv 26:39](https://ref.ly/Lev26:39), [26:39](https://ref.ly/Lev26:39). **Hiph.** *Inf. abs.* הָמֵק, trans. בְּשָרוֺ ´ה [Zc 14:12](https://ref.ly/Zech14:12) *a causing his flesh to rot*.

> \[Str4716] †מַק (c. art. מָק) **n.m.** decay, rottenness;—תַּחַת בּשֹׁם מַק יִהְיֶה [Is 3:24](https://ref.ly/Isa3:24) *instead of perfume rottenness shall there be;* fig. שָׁרְשָׁם כַּמָּק יִהְיֶה [5:24](https://ref.ly/Isa5:24) (‖וּפִרְחָם כָּאָבָק יַעַלֶה).

## I מָרָא (id: BDB05610)

\[Str4754] †I. \[מָרָא] **vb.** only **Hiph.** *Impf.* 3 fs.: mng dub., perh. *beat* the air, or *flap* the wings, cf. Di De (and Wetzst in De), Bu Du (cf. Ar. مَرَى *whip, urge on* a horse ?);—תַּמְרִָיא תִּשְחָק :לַסּוּם וּלְדֹבְבֽוֺ [Jb 39:18](https://ref.ly/Job39:18) *she* (the ostrich) *flaps away*, she laughs at the horse and his rider.

* **Associated Passages:** Job 39:18


## II מרא (id: BDB05611)

II. מרא (√ of foll., *be fat;* cf. As. *marû* III. causat., adj. *marû*, *well\-fed, fat;* Ar. مَرِيءَ *be digestible, agree with* (of food)).

> \[Str4806] †מְרִיא **n.\[m.]** fatling, fatlings;—abs. ´מ[2 S 6:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:13) \+ 3 t.; pl. abs. מְרִיאִים [Is 1:11](https://ref.ly/Isa1:11), cstr. מְרִיאֵי Ez [39:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:18); sf. מְרִיאֵיכֶם [Am 5:22](https://ref.ly/Amos5:22); —*fatling:* עֵגֶל וּכְפִיר וּמְרִיא [Is 11:6](https://ref.ly/Isa11:6); elsewh. only of sacrificial animals, שֶׁלָם מְרִיאֵיכֶם [Am 5:22](https://ref.ly/Amos5:22) (‖עֹלוֺת, מִנְחֹתֵיכֶם); חֵלֶב מְרִיאִים [Is 1:11](https://ref.ly/Isa1:11) (‖עֹלוֺת אֵלִים, דַּם פָּרִים, etc); מְרִיאֵי בָשָׁןEz [39:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:18) (\+ פָרִים, אֵילִים, etc.); also sg. coll., וַיִּזְבָּח שׁוֺר וּמְדִיא [2 S 6:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:13), so (\+ צאֹן וּבָקָר) [1 K 1:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:9), and (\+ שׁוֺר, צאֹן) v:[19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:19), v:[25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:25).

## מָרַד (id: BDB05625)

\[Str4775] †\[מָרַד] **vb. rebel** (NH *id,;* Ar. مَرَدَ *be bold and audacious in acts of rebellion or disobedience;* Eth. መረደ፡ *run strenuously, attack;* መራድ፡ *rebellion*, PräBeiträge iii. 44; Sab. מרד *rebellion* Ha1535:16 Sab. Denkm.:38 HomChrestom. 127; Aram. ܡܪܰܕ, מְרַד; *rebel*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. מָרָד [2 Ch 36:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr36:13), 2 ms מָרַדְתָּ [2 K 18:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:20) \= [Is 36:5](https://ref.ly/Isa36:5), etc.; *Impf.* וַיִּמְרֹד [2 K 18:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:7) \+ 3 t., וַיִּמְרָ־ד [2 K 24:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs24:1), Ez [17:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:15), etc.; *Inf. cstr.* לִמְרֹד [Jos 22:29](https://ref.ly/Josh22:29), לִמְרוֺד [Ne 6:6](https://ref.ly/Neh6:6); *Pt.* pl. מֹרְדִים [Ne 2:19](https://ref.ly/Neh2:19), Ez [20:38](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:38), ´מוֺ Ez [2:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek2:3); cstr. מֹרדי [Jb 24:13](https://ref.ly/Job24:13); —*rebel, revolt:* **1\.** against human king, c. בְּ Pers. [2 K 18:7](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:7), [18:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:20) \= [Is 36:5](https://ref.ly/Isa36:5), [2 K 24:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs24:1), [24:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs24:20) \= [Je 52:3](https://ref.ly/Jer52:3), cf. Ez [17:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:15), [2 Ch 36:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr36:13), [Ne 9:26](https://ref.ly/Neh9:26); less oft. (late) c. עַל Pers. [2 Ch 13:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr13:6), [Ne 2:19](https://ref.ly/Neh2:19); abs. [Gn 14:4](https://ref.ly/Gen14:4), [Ne 6:6](https://ref.ly/Neh6:6). **2\.** against God, c. בְּ [Nu 14:9](https://ref.ly/Num14:9) (JE), [Jos 22:16](https://ref.ly/Josh22:16), [22:18](https://ref.ly/Josh22:18), [22:19](https://ref.ly/Josh22:19), [22:29](https://ref.ly/Josh22:29) (all P), Ez [2:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek2:3) (Co מָרוּ), [Dn 9:9](https://ref.ly/Dan9:9); abs. Ez [2:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek2:3) (pt., Co מורים), [20:38](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:38), [Dn 9:5](https://ref.ly/Dan9:5). **3\.** poet., against light only מֹרְדֵי אוֺר [Job 24:13](https://ref.ly/Job24:13).

> \[Str4777] †I. מֶ֫רֶד **n.\[m.]** rebellion, revolt, against ´י, וְאִם־בְּמַעַל ביהוה ´אִם בְּמ [Jos 22:24](https://ref.ly/Josh22:24) (P; cf. מָרַד 2\).

> \[Str4778] † II. מֶ֫רֶד **n. pr.m.** name in Judah acc. to [1 Ch 4:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:17), מָרֶד v:[18](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:18). LXX Πωραδ, Νωρωηλ, A Μωραδ, Μωρηδ, LXXL Βαραδ, Μαρω.

> \[Str4780] †מַרְדּוּת **n.f.** rebellion, rebelliousness, only in ´בֶּן־נַעֲוַת המ [1 S 20:30](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:30), where read prob. בֶּן־נַעֲרַת הַמַרְדוּת *son of a girl of rebelliousness* \= *rebellious girl,* cf. LXX Vulgate Th We Dr HPS; \> LagM i. 236 f. Bu (cf. also Dr Kit) der. ´מ fr. רדת, and, retaining MT, rd. *a woman gone astray* (v. II. עָוָה) *from discipline* (Aram. sense: √ רְדָא).

> \[Str4781] †מְרֹדַךְ **n.pr.div.** chief god of Babylon in Nebuchadrezzar's time (As *Mar(u)duk(u)* SchrCOT Gloss TieleBab.\-Ass. Gesch. 530 ff. SayRel. Bab. 96 ff.; cf. Muss\-ArnoltJBL xi. 1892,164 f.);—נִלְכְּדָת בָּבֶל הֹבִישׁ בֵּל חַת מְרֹדָךְ [Je 50:2](https://ref.ly/Jer50:2) (LXX Μαιωδακ).

Baer's n.) \= [2 K 20:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs20:12) (where read מרדך for בראדך); LXX (in both) Μαρωδακ(χ) Βαλ(α)δαν; cf. also בַּלְאֲדָן, p. 114

> \[Str4782] †מָרְּדֳּכַי (van d. H. מָרְדְּכַי) **n.pr.m.**Mordecai (perh. fr. n. div. *Marduk* (מְרֹרַךְ), ZimZAW xi.161 ff.; cf. further Wild (citing Jensen):Comm. Est. 173 f.);—´מ [Ezr 2:2](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:2), [Est 2:5](https://ref.ly/Esth2:5) \+; מָרְדֳּכָי [Est 2:22](https://ref.ly/Esth2:22) \+;—**1\.** companion of Zerub., acc. to [Ezr 2:2](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:2) \= [Ne 7:7](https://ref.ly/Neh7:7). LXX Μαραθχαιος, Μαρδοχαιος, etc: **2\.** cousin and adoptive father of Esther [Est 2:5](https://ref.ly/Esth2:5), [2:7](https://ref.ly/Esth2:7), [2:10](https://ref.ly/Esth2:10) \+ 55 t. Est. LXX Μαρδοχαιος.

## מָרָה (id: BDB05633)

\[Str4784,4754,4754] † מָרָה **vb.** be contentious, refractory, rebellious (NH *id.,* Hiph.; Ar. مَرَىَ III. *dispute with;* Syr. ܡܰܪܺܝ Pa. *contend with*);—**Qal.** *Pf.* 3 ms. מָרָה [1 K 13:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:26); f. מָֽרְתָה [Ho 14:1](https://ref.ly/Hos14:1); מָרָתָה [Je 4:17](https://ref.ly/Jer4:17); 2 ms. מָרִיתָ [1 K 13:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:21) \+ 11 t. pf.; *Inf. abs.* מָרוֺ [La 1:20](https://ref.ly/Lam1:20); *Pt.* מֹרֶה [2 K 14:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:26) (but rd. מר, √ מרר, KmpKau כִּי מַר הוּא); מוֺרֶה [Dt 21:18](https://ref.ly/Deut21:18) \+ 3 t.; f. מֹרְאָה [Zp 3:1](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:1) (rd. מֹרָה; metapl. form acc. to Ges§ 75 R. 22\); pl. מֹרִים [Nu 20:10](https://ref.ly/Num20:10); —*be disobedient, rebellious:* **1\.** בֵּן סוֺרֵר וּמֹרֶה [Dt 21:18](https://ref.ly/Deut21:18), [21:20](https://ref.ly/Deut21:20) *stubborn and rebellious son* (towards father). **2\.** elsewhere towards God: דֹּר סוֺרֵר וּמֹרֶה [Psalm 78:8](https://ref.ly/Ps78:8); ´לֵב סורר ומ [Je 5:23](https://ref.ly/Jer5:23); מוֺרְאָה ונגאלה הָעִיר [Zp 3:1](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:1); הַמֹּרִים [Nu 20:10](https://ref.ly/Num20:10) *ye rebels* (P); abs. [Is 50:5](https://ref.ly/Isa50:5), [63:10](https://ref.ly/Isa63:10), [La 1:20](https://ref.ly/Lam1:20), [1:20](https://ref.ly/Lam1:20); ‖מאן [Is 1:20](https://ref.ly/Isa1:20); פשׁע [La 3:42](https://ref.ly/Lam3:42); c. בְּ Pers. [Ho 14:1](https://ref.ly/Hos14:1), [Psalm 5:11](https://ref.ly/Ps5:11), elsewh. acc. Pers. [Je 4:17](https://ref.ly/Jer4:17); ´י's words [Psalm 105:28](https://ref.ly/Ps105:28); פי (את)מרה [Nu 20:24](https://ref.ly/Num20:24), [27:14](https://ref.ly/Num27:14) (P), [1 S 12:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:15), [1 K 13:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:21), [13:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:26) (D2) [La 1:18](https://ref.ly/Lam1:18).—In [Ho 4:4](https://ref.ly/Hos4:4) rd. perh. מָרוּ בִי *have rebelled against me* (for MT כִּמְרִיבֵי), so RSProph. iv. n. 8 Che (for other views v. ריב). **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 mpl. הִמְרוּ [Psalm 106:33](https://ref.ly/Ps106:33), [107:11](https://ref.ly/Ps107:11); *Impf.* יַמְרֶה [Jos 1:18](https://ref.ly/Josh1:18); f. וַתֶּ֫מֶר Ez [5:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:6); 2 ms. תַּמֵּר (as if √ מרר) [Ex 23:21](https://ref.ly/Exod23:21), rd. תֶּ֫מֶר Di al.; 3 mpl. יַמְרוּ [Psalm 106:43](https://ref.ly/Ps106:43) \+ 6 t; sf. יַמְרוּהוּ [Psalm 78:40](https://ref.ly/Ps78:40); 2 mpl. תַּמְרוּ [1 S 12:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:14) \+ 3 t.; *Inf. cstr.* לַמְרוֺת [Is 3:8](https://ref.ly/Isa3:8), [Psalm 78:17](https://ref.ly/Ps78:17); sf. הַמְּרוֺתָם (Ges§ 20\. 2b) [Jb 17:2](https://ref.ly/Job17:2); *Pt.* pl. מַמְרִים [Dt 9:7](https://ref.ly/Deut9:7) \+ 2 t.;—*shew disobedience, rebelliousness*, always toward God: abs. [Ne 9:26](https://ref.ly/Neh9:26). (‖מרד), [Psalm 106:7](https://ref.ly/Ps106:7), [106:43](https://ref.ly/Ps106:43), [Job 17:2](https://ref.ly/Job17:2); c. בְּ Pers. [Ex 23:21](https://ref.ly/Exod23:21), Ez [20:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:8), [20:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:13), [20:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:21); עִם [Dt 9:7](https://ref.ly/Deut9:7), [9:24](https://ref.ly/Deut9:24), [31:27](https://ref.ly/Deut31:27); elsewhere acc. of God or his commands: [Is 3:8](https://ref.ly/Isa3:8), Ez [5:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:6), [Psalm 78:17](https://ref.ly/Ps78:17), [78:40](https://ref.ly/Ps78:40), [78:56](https://ref.ly/Ps78:56), [107:11](https://ref.ly/Ps107:11); המרה את פי [Dt 1:26](https://ref.ly/Deut1:26), [1:43](https://ref.ly/Deut1:43), [9:23](https://ref.ly/Deut9:23), [Jos 1:18](https://ref.ly/Josh1:18), [1 S 12:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:14) (D2), את־רוחוֺ [Psalm 106:33](https://ref.ly/Ps106:33) (cf. [Eph 4:30](https://ref.ly/Eph4:30)); perh. [139:20](https://ref.ly/Ps139:20) (v. אָמַר **1\.**).

## מָרַט (id: BDB05649)

\[Str4803,4593,4178] †\[מָרַט] **vb.** make smooth, bare, bald; scour, polish (NH *id.; pull out, off* (feathers, hair); Aram. ܡܪܰܛ. מְרַט *id.;* Ar. مَرِطَ *have little hair,* مَرَطَ *pluck out hair*);—**Qal** *Impf.* 1 S. וָאֶמְרְטָה [Ezr 9:3](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:3), sf. 3 mpl. וָאֶמְרְטֵם [Ne 13:25](https://ref.ly/Neh13:25); *Inf. cstr.* מָרְטָה Ez [21:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:16); *Pt.* pl. מֹרְטִים [Is 50:6](https://ref.ly/Isa50:6); *pass.* f. מְרוּטָה Ez [21:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:14) \+ 2 t.;—**1\.** *make bare* כָּל־כָּתֵף מְרוּטָה Ez [29:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:18) *every shoulder is laid bare* (fr. chafing of burden ; ‖כָּל־ראֹשׁ מֻקְרָח); the cheek (by plucking out beard) גֵּוִי נָתַתִּי לְמַכִּים וּלְחָיַי לְמֹרְטִָים [Is 50:6](https://ref.ly/Isa50:6); c. acc. Pers. וָאֶמְרְטֵָם [Ne 13:25](https://ref.ly/Neh13:25) (both acts of violence); of hair and beard וָאֶמְרְטָה מִשְּעַר ראֹשִׁי וּזֶקָנִי [Ezr 9:3](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:3) (sign of grief). **2\.** *scour, polish*, a sword, only Ez [21:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:14) (\+ הוּחַדָּת; Co rds. ´מ as Pu), v:[16](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:16) (del. Co), v:[33](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:28), \+ v:[20](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:20) מְרֻטָה for MT מְעֻטָּה (Ew Sm Co Berthol). **Niph.** *Impf.* יִמָּרֵט ראֹשׁוֺ [Lv 13:40](https://ref.ly/Lev13:40), [13:41](https://ref.ly/Lev13:41) *his head is made bald* (by leprosy). **Pu.** *Pf.* 3 fs. מֹרָטָה Ez [21:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:15), [21:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:16); *Pt.* מְמֹרָט [1 K 7:45](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:45), מוֺרָט [Is 18:27](https://ref.ly/Isa18:27) (Ges§ 52 R.6\):—*scoured, polished,* of bronze utensils of temple [1 K 7:45](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:45); of human skin [Is 18:27](https://ref.ly/Isa18:27); of sword Ez [21:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:15), [21:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:16) (both \+ הוּחַדָּה, v. **Qal** supr.)

LXX Μαριαμ. **2\. m.** (prob.) a Judahite [1 Ch 4:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:17). LXX τὸν Μαιων, LXXL Μωεωρ.

## מָרַץ (id: BDB05671)

\[Str4834] †\[מָרַץ] **vb.** be sick, only in der. species (As. II. *marâṣu* DlHWB 426; Ar. مَرِضَ; Sab. מרץַ *sick person, sickness*, MordtZMG 1876, 32; Aram. vb. ܡܪܰܥ, מְרַע);—**Niph.** *Pf.* 3 pl. נִמְרְצוּ [Jb 6:25](https://ref.ly/Job6:25) (but v. infr.); *Pt.* נִמְרָץ [Mi 2:10](https://ref.ly/Mic2:10); f. נִמְרֶ֫צֶת [1 K 2:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:8); —חֶבֶל נִמְרָץ [Mi 2:10](https://ref.ly/Mic2:10) *a sore, grievous* destruction (lit. *made sick*, cf. מַכָּה נַחְלָה [Je 14:17](https://ref.ly/Jer14:17)); נִמְרֶצֶת קְלָלָה [1 K 2:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:8) *a grievous curse*.—In [Jb 6:25](https://ref.ly/Job6:25) this mng. unsuit., Bu *are strong, effective* \[cf. As. I. *marâṣu, be difficult, inaccessible* DlHWB 425 f.; always of something repelling]. More prob. נמרצו \= נמלצו *be sweet* (cf. [Psalm 119:103](https://ref.ly/Ps119:103)) or rd. נמלצו (q. v.), CheJQ July, 1897 Du. **Hiph.** *Impf.* sf. מַה־יַּמְרִיצְ [Jb 16:3](https://ref.ly/Job16:3) *what sickens thee* (what disturbs, vexes thee) that thou answerest ?

> \[Str4794,4835] \[מְרֻחָה] v. מְרוּצָה sub רוץ, 954

> \[Str4836–37] מַרְצֵעַ v. רצע. מַרְצֶ֫פֶת v. רצף. p. 954

## I מָרַק (id: BDB05674)

\[Str4838] †I. \[מָרַק] **vb.** scour, polish (NH *id.;* Aram. ܡܪܰܩ מְרַק; cf. Ar. مَرَقَ *scrape off wool* from a skin);—**Qal** *Imv.* מִרְקוּ הָֽרְמָחִים [Je 46:4](https://ref.ly/Jer46:4) *polish the lances! Pt. pass.* מָרוּק [2 Ch 4:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:16) *polished*, of bronze utensils of temple (\= מְמוֺרָט [1 K 7:45](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:45)). **Hiph.** *Impf.* 3 fs. חַמְרִיק only [Pr 20:30](https://ref.ly/Prov20:30) Kt, fig., in cl. בְּרָע ´חַבֻּרוֺת פֶּצַע ת *blows that cut in, cleanse away evil* (cf. De Now Wild VB); Qr תַּמְרוּק i.e. (are) *a cleaning*. (LXX συναντᾷ κακοῖς, whence Gr תַּקְדִּים, Frankenb. תִּקְרֶינָה, but improb.). **Pu.** *Pf.* וּמֹרַק consec. [Lv 6:21](https://ref.ly/Lev6:21) *be well scoured*, of bronze vessel (‖וְשֻׁטַּף בַּמָּיִם).

> \[Str4795] †\[מָרוּק] (Köii. 138\) **n.\[m.]** a scraping, rubbing;—only pl. sf. יְמֵי מְרוּקֵיהֶן [Est 2:12](https://ref.ly/Esth2:12) lit. *the days of their* (bodily) *rubbings*, i.e. the year's preparation of girls for the harem.

> \[Str8562] † תַּמְרוּק **n.\[m.]** id.;—abs. sg. only [Pr 20:30](https://ref.ly/Prov20:30) Qr, v. מרק **Hiph.**; elsewhere pl. cstr. תַּמְרוּקֵי הַנָּשִׁים [Est 2:12](https://ref.ly/Esth2:12); sf. תַּמְרוּקֶיהָ v:[9](https://ref.ly/Esth2:9), תַּמְרוּקֵיהֶן v:[3](https://ref.ly/Esth2:3).

* **Associated Passages:** Esther 2:12; Esther 2:3; Proverbs 20:30; Esther 2:9; 2 Chronicles 4:16; 1 Kings 7:45; Jeremiah 46:4; Leviticus 6:21

## I מָרַר (id: BDB05680)

\[Str4843] † I. מָרַר **vb.** be bitter (NH *id.;* As. *marâru* and deriv. DlHWB 427; Ar. مُرَّ *become roused* (of bile), II. *make bitter*, IV. *become bitter;* مُرٌّ *bitter;* Eth. መረረ፡; Aram. ܡܰܪ, מְרַר);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. מַר Is 38:l7 \+ 2 t.; 3 fs. מָ֫רָה [1 S 30:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:6), [2 K 4:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:27); *Impf.* 3 ms. יֵמַר [Is 24:9](https://ref.ly/Isa24:9); —*be bitter:* **1\.** lit. יֵמַר שֵׁכָר לְשֹׁתָיו [Is 24:9](https://ref.ly/Isa24:9). **2\.** fig. מָ֫רָה נֶפֶשׁ [1 S 30:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:6) *the soul* of all the people *was bitter* against (עַ־ל); נַפְשָׁהּ מָ֫רָה־לָהּ [2 K 4:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:27) *her soul, it is bitter to her* (i.e. she is in bitter distress); impers. מַר־לִי מְאֹד מִכֶּם [Ru 1:13](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:13) *it is very bitter to me on your account* (I am much distressed), cf. [La 1:4](https://ref.ly/Lam1:4).—לְשָׁלוֺם מַר־לִי מָר [Is 38:17](https://ref.ly/Isa38:17) is dub.; CheComm. *for* (*my*) *welfare was it* (*so*) *bitter to me*, (*so*) *bitter*, cf. Brd; so De Kau (treating מר appar. as adj.); \< Drechsl. Di *for my welfare did the bitter become bitter to me* (מָר adj.); Lo Gr, cf. Buhl (sub מַר adj.), rd. מָר for מַר (√ מור), the bitter *is changed* for me into welfare; CheHpt del. מָר; Du (after LXX) del. cl. as gloss. **Pi.** *Impf.* 1 S. אֲמָרֵר [Is 22:4](https://ref.ly/Isa22:4); 3 mpl. וַיְמָֽרֲרוּ [Ex 1:14](https://ref.ly/Exod1:14), sf. וַיְמָֽרֲרֻהוּ [Gn 49:23](https://ref.ly/Gen49:23); —*make bitter, shew bitterness:*—**1\.** ´וַיְמ [Gn 49:23](https://ref.ly/Gen49:23) (poem) and the archers *shewed bitterness* (i.e. bitter hostility) *toward him* (sf. of indir. obj.); אֲמָרֵר בַּבֶּכִָי [Is 22:4](https://ref.ly/Isa22:4) *I will shew bitterness in weeping* (\= weep bitterly). **2\.** אֶת־חַיֵּיהֶם ´וַיְמ [Ex 1:14](https://ref.ly/Exod1:14) (P) *and they made their lives bitter* by slavery. **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. הֵמַר [Ru 1:20](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:20), [Jb 27:2](https://ref.ly/Job27:2); *Impf.* 2 ms. תַּמֵּר [Ex 23:21](https://ref.ly/Exod23:21) (but rd. תֶּ֫מֶר √ מרה **Hiph.** q.v.); *Inf. abs.* הָמֵר [Zc 12:10](https://ref.ly/Zech12:10), [12:10](https://ref.ly/Zech12:10); —*make bitter, shew bitterness:* **1\.** שַׁדַּי הֵמַר נַפְשִׁי [Jb 27:2](https://ref.ly/Job27:2) *Shadday, who hath embittered my soul;* הֵמַר שַׁדַּי לִי מְאֹד [Ru 1:20](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:20). **2\.** וְהָמֵר עָלָיו כְּהָמֵר עַל־הַבְּכוֺר [Zc 12:10](https://ref.ly/Zech12:10) *and a bitter outcry* (\= lit. shewing bitterness; ‖מִסְפֵּד, סָפַד) over him, like a *bitter outcry* over the first\-born. **Hithpalp.** *Impf.* וְיִתְמַרְמַר [Dn 11:11](https://ref.ly/Dan11:11) *and* the king of the south *shall embitter himself* (be enraged); אֶ־ל ´וַיִּתְמ [8:7](https://ref.ly/Dan8:7) *and he was enraged against*.

Co); דָּבָר מָר [Psalm 64:4](https://ref.ly/Ps64:4); as adv. (cry, weep) *bitterly* Ez [27:30](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:30) (מָרָה), [Zp 1:14](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:14), [Is 33:7](https://ref.ly/Isa33:7) (both מַר). **b.** of feeling, temper, esp. c. נֶפֶשׁ: אֲנָשִׁים מָרֵי נֶפֶשׁ Ju [18:25](https://ref.ly/Judg18:25) *men fierce* (GFM *acrid*) *of temper*, so [2 S 17:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:8) (like a bear robbed of whelps); הַגּוֺי הַמַּר Hb [1:6](https://ref.ly/Hab1:6) *the fierce nation;* מַר־נֶפֶשׁ \= *discontented* [1 S 22:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam22:2); מַרֵי נֶפֶשׁ \= *the bitterly wretched* [Jb 3:20](https://ref.ly/Job3:20), [Pr 31:6](https://ref.ly/Prov31:6), so ´מָרַת נ [1 S 1:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:10); cf. יוֺם מָר [Am 8:10](https://ref.ly/Amos8:10); as subst. abstr. מַר־נֶפֶשׁ *bitterness of soul* [Is 38:15](https://ref.ly/Isa38:15), Ez [27:31](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:31) (del. Co), [Jb 7:11](https://ref.ly/Job7:11), [10:1](https://ref.ly/Job10:1) \= נפשׁ מָרָה [21:25](https://ref.ly/Job21:25); cf. מָרָא [Ru 1:20](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:20) (name given to Naomi by herself); מַר־הַמָּוֶת [1 S 15:32](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:32) *the bitterness of death ;* מַר־מִמָּוֶת [Ec 7:26](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:26) (metaph. of harlot); in Ez [3:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:14) *I went bitterly*, del. מַר Co.—[Is 38:17](https://ref.ly/Isa38:17) v. מרר.

## II מרר (id: BDB05697)

II. מרר (√ of foll.; perhaps (so Thes) cf. Ar. مَرٌّ *pass by, go*, sts. *flow*, مَرْمَرَ *cause to flow*, مَمَرٌّ *water\-course*).

## מָשַׁח (id: BDB05735)

\[Str4886] †מָשַׁח **vb.** smear, anoint (NH *id.;* orig. prob. as Ar. مَسَحَ *wipe* or*stroke with the hand* (cf. RS Sem. i. 215; 2nd ed., 283\), anoint, Aram.ܡܫܰܚ מְשַׁח *anoint*; on משת, *id.* (?) in Aram. inscr. v. CISii.1\. No.145, C, 1; Palm. משחא *oil* VogNo.16; Eth. ܡܣܚ: *anoint, feast, dine* DILex 176; (As.*mašâḫu* is measure; Aram. מְשַׁת *id.;* Ar. مساحة *measurement*, cf. DlProl. 178 Frä282));—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. ´מ [Nu 3:25](https://ref.ly/Num3:25) \+ 2 t.; sf. מְשָֽׁחְֲ [Psalm 45:8](https://ref.ly/Ps45:8), [1 S 10:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:1); מְשָׁחוֺ [2 Ch 22:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr22:7); 2 ms. מָשַׁחְתָּ [Gn 31:13](https://ref.ly/Gen31:13) \+ 12 t., \+ 9 t. Pf.; *Impf.* 3 ms. יִמְשַׁח [Lv 16:32](https://ref.ly/Lev16:32) \+ 6 t.; 3 mpl. יִמְשָׁחוּ [Am 6:6](https://ref.ly/Amos6:6), \+ 13 t. Impf.; *Imv.* sf. מְשָׁחֵהוּ [1 S 16:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:12); pl. מִשְׁחוּ [Is 21:5](https://ref.ly/Isa21:5); *Inf. abs.* מָשׁוֺחַ [Je 22:14](https://ref.ly/Jer22:14); *cstr.* מְשֹׁחַ Ju [9:8](https://ref.ly/Judg9:8), [Dn 9:24](https://ref.ly/Dan9:24) \+ [Ho 8:3](https://ref.ly/Hos8:3) LXX We GASm Now; מָשְׁחָה [Ex 29:29](https://ref.ly/Exod29:29); sf. מְשֽׁחְֳ[1 S 15:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:1) (Baer); מָשְׁחוֺ [Lv 7:36](https://ref.ly/Lev7:36); מָשְׁחָתָם [Ex 40:15](https://ref.ly/Exod40:15); *Pt.* pl. משְׁחִים Ju [9:15](https://ref.ly/Judg9:15); *pass.* מָשׁוּחַ [2 S 3:39](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:39) (We suggests deriv. from שׁח, defect. משׁח (מֻשַׁח), wrongly read as מָשֻׁחַ; or by transposition משׁוח may have been mistaken for an unexampled מוּשַׁח Hoph. Pt. of שׁוח); מָשִׁיחַ [2 S 1:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:21) (but rd. מָשֻׁח (or נמשׁח) Codd. We Dr Kit Bu HPS); pl. מְשֻׁחִים [Ex 29:2](https://ref.ly/Exod29:2) \+ 4 t;—**1\.** *smear*, house with colour (paint) [Je 22:14](https://ref.ly/Jer22:14); shield with oil (anoint) [Is 21:5](https://ref.ly/Isa21:5), [2 S 1:21](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:21) (cf. Dr; Gr כְּלִי מָשׁוּחַ *weapon anointed;* PetersJBL 893, 56 כְּלֵי מָשׁוּחַ *weapons of one* (the king) *anointed*); the person, with ointments [Am 6:6](https://ref.ly/Amos6:6); unleavened cakes, with oil [Ex 29:2](https://ref.ly/Exod29:2), [Lv 2:4](https://ref.ly/Lev2:4), [Nu 6:15](https://ref.ly/Num6:15) (P). **2\.** *anoint*, as consecration, solemn setting apart to an office, always by the use of oil poured on the head: לנביא as a prophet [1 K 19:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:16) (Elisha by Elijah) [Is 61:1](https://ref.ly/Isa61:1); elsewh. of king, c. acc., מֶלֶח Ju [9:8](https://ref.ly/Judg9:8) (E), so [Ho 8:3](https://ref.ly/Hos8:3) reading מִמְּשֹׁחַ מֶלֶח (for MT ´מִמַּשָּא מ) We GASm Now; also [Ho 7:3](https://ref.ly/Hos7:3) reading יִמְשְׁחוּ (for MT יְשַמְּחוּ) We Now; elsewhere acc. As. *mašâhu, measure*, Aram. מְשַׁח *id.*, etc.) *that covereth*, after Vulgate *cherub extentus* (far\-reaching wings), \&ld; *far\-covering cherub*, \&rd; Ew (Or “ *umspannender* ”); *the anointed cherub that covereth*, AV RV, so Hä. Co thinks gloss, and reads אֶת־כְּרוּב נְתַתִּיְ; text dub. acc. to SiegfKau SS Buhl Berthol.

> \[Str4898] †מְשֵׁיזַבְאֵל **n.pr.m.** Jewish name in Persian period (*God delivers;* from Aram. שֵׁיזֵיב, ܫܰܘܙܶܒ *deliver*, As. *šûzubu*, Shaph. from *ezêbu, deliver*, DlHWB 35; cf. the Bab. names *Mušizib\-Marduk,* ‘Marduk delivers,’ *Mušizib\-í lu*, KBii. 28l, iv. 129\):—grandfather of one of the wall\-builders [Ne 3:4](https://ref.ly/Neh3:4); one of those sealed [10:22](https://ref.ly/Neh10:22); father of royal officer (a Judaean) [11:24](https://ref.ly/Neh11:24); the relation of these to each other is unknown; LXX Μασεζεβηα, Μεσωζεβηλ, LXXL Μασσιζαβελ, etc.

## מָשַׁךְ (id: BDB05747)

\[Str4900] †מָשַׁךְ **vb.** draw, drag (NH *id.;* Aram. מְשַׁח (rare) *be extended*, also *conduct, take*, Syr. ܡܫܰܟ is *be dry, shrivelled* (of fruit., etc.); Eth. መሰከ፡ *bend* (the bow); Ar. مَسَكَ *grasp and hold* I. Frey; IV. Frey Lane; cf. also As. *masûku, skin* (perh. also *march* DlHWB 431\), Ar. مَسْكٌ *id.*, Aram. ܡܶܫܟܳܐ, מַשְׁכָּא *id.*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. ´מ [1 K 22:34](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:34) \+ 2 t., וּמָשַׁח consec. [Jb 24:22](https://ref.ly/Job24:22); 3 fs. מָֽשְׁכָה [Dt 21:3](https://ref.ly/Deut21:3), etc.; *Impf.* יִמְשׁוֺח [Jb 21:33](https://ref.ly/Job21:33); וַיִּמְשֹׁח Ju [20:37](https://ref.ly/Judg20:37); 2 ms. sf. תִּמְשְׁכֵנִי [Psalm 28:3](https://ref.ly/Ps28:3); 1 S. sf. אֶמְשְׁכֵם [Ho 11:4](https://ref.ly/Hos11:4), etc.; *Imv.* מְשֹׁח [Psalm 36:11](https://ref.ly/Ps36:11), sf. מָשְׁכֵנִי Ct [1:4](https://ref.ly/Song1:4); mpl. מִשְׁכוּ [Ex 12:21](https://ref.ly/Exod12:21), מָשְׁכוּ Ez [32:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:20); *Inf. cstr.* מְשֹׁח [Ex 19:13](https://ref.ly/Exod19:13), [Jos 6:5](https://ref.ly/Josh6:5), לִמְשׁוֺח [Ec 2:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:3); *Pt.* משֵׁח [Am 9:13](https://ref.ly/Amos9:13), [Psalm 109:12](https://ref.ly/Ps109:12); pl. משְׁכִים Ju [5:14](https://ref.ly/Judg5:14); cstr. משְׁכֵי [Is 5:18](https://ref.ly/Isa5:18), [66:19](https://ref.ly/Isa66:19); —**1\.** *draw* and lift out of (מִן), c. acc. Pers., וַיִּמְשְׁכוּ וַיַּעֲלוּ אֶת־יוֺסֵף מִן הַבּוֺר [Gn 37:28](https://ref.ly/Gen37:28) (E), so Jeremiah (\+ בַּחֲבָלִים, ב instr.) Pers. Ju [4:7](https://ref.ly/Judg4:7) (subj. ´י; \+ אֶ־ל Pers. et rei); *lead* or *drag off* (to doom, subj. ´י) [Psalm 28:3](https://ref.ly/Ps28:3) (cf. [26:9](https://ref.ly/Ps26:9)); similarly, or \= *draw down* to death, ´מָשְׁכוּ אוֺתָהּ וגו Ez [32:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:20) (si vera l.; ref. to fall of Egypt), but rd. perh. a form of שׁכב for משׁכו, LXX Co Berthol, cf. Sm.; abs., subj. heifer, מָֽשְׁכָה בְּעוֺל [Dt 21:3](https://ref.ly/Deut21:3) (ב instr.); *draw, lead* (in love), (fig., subj. ´י) בְּחַבְלֵי אָדָם אֶמְשְׁכֵם [Ho 11:4](https://ref.ly/Hos11:4), cf. Germ. *ziehen*) וּמָשַׁכְתַָּ֫ בְּהַר תָּבוֺר Ju [4:6](https://ref.ly/Judg4:6) cf. [20:37](https://ref.ly/Judg20:37) (abs.), [Jb 21:33](https://ref.ly/Job21:33) (אַחֲרָיו); so also (perh.) [Ex 12:21](https://ref.ly/Exod12:21) *proceed* (i.e. forthwith), and take you a sheep; and prob. משְׁכִים בְּשֵׁבֶט Ju [5:14](https://ref.ly/Judg5:14) *those marching with the staff*, etc. (most under **2\.** *drawing with*, i.e. wielding). **4\.** *draw out* a sound, *give a sound*, בִּמְשֹׁח הַיֹּבֵל [Ex 19:13](https://ref.ly/Exod19:13) (E) *when the ram* ('s horn) *sounds*, בְּקֶרֶן ´הַיֹּבֵל בִּמ [Jos 6:5](https://ref.ly/Josh6:5) (JE). **5\.** *draw out, prolong, continue*, מְשֹׁח חַסְדְּ לְיֹדְעֶיְ [Psalm 36:10](https://ref.ly/Ps36:10) *prolong, continue thy kindness to them that know thee;* אַל־יְהִי לוֺ משֵׁח חֶסֶד [109:12](https://ref.ly/Ps109:12); (so poss. מְשַׁכְתִּיְ חָסֶד [Je 31:3](https://ref.ly/Jer31:3) *I have prolonged kindness to thee*, cf. Ryle [Ne 9:30](https://ref.ly/Neh9:30); al. as **1** supr.); cf. עֲלֶיהֶם שָׁנִים ´וַתִּמ רַבּוֺת [Ne 9:30](https://ref.ly/Neh9:30) (חֶסֶד omitted); אַפְּ לְדֹר וָדֹר ´תִּמ [85:6](https://ref.ly/Ps85:6) (‖הַלְעוֺלָם תֶּאֱנַף בָּנוּ); אַבִּירִים בְּכֹחוֺ ´וּמ [Jb 24:22](https://ref.ly/Job24:22) i.e. *he* (God) *prolongeth* the life of *the mighty* (Du *draggeth* them *off*, as [Psalm 28:3](https://ref.ly/Ps28:3) **1** supr.,—reading also אֹבְּדִים for ´אַבּ with LXX BI); cf. **Niph. 6\.** *trail* seed (draw along in sowing) [Am 9:13](https://ref.ly/Amos9:13) (opp. דָֹּרֵח עֲנָבִים; cf. מֶשֶׁח infr.) **7\.** (late) *cheer* (draw, attract, gratify) בַּיַּיִן ´מ אֶת־בְּשָרִי [Ec 2:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:3), —so De (who cites NH, Chag14 a) Now Wild.—מָשַׁךְ יָדוֺ אֶת־לּֽצֲצִים [Ho 7:5](https://ref.ly/Hos7:5) is difficult, LXX AV RV *he stretcheth out his hand with*, maketh common cause with, is hardly poss., text prob. corrupt We Now. **Niph.** *Impf.* 3 mpl. לאֹ יִמָּשֵָׁכוּ [Is 13:22](https://ref.ly/Isa13:22) *they shall not be prolonged* (days of Babylon); 3 fs. לאֹ תִּמָּשֵׁח Ez [12:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:25), [12:28](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:28) *it shall not be postponed*. **Pu.** *Pt. long drawn out:* מְמֻשָּׁח [Is 18:2](https://ref.ly/Isa18:2), [18:7](https://ref.ly/Isa18:7) of persons, \= *tall;* תֹּחֶלֶת מְמֻשָּׁכָה [Pr 13:12](https://ref.ly/Prov13:12) *hope postponed, deferred*.

> \[Str4901] †I. \[מֶ֫שֶׁךְ] **n.** \[**m.**] a drawing, drawing up, a trail;—**1\.** cstr. מֶשֶׁךְ חָכְמָה מִפְּנִינִים [Jb 28:18](https://ref.ly/Job28:18) *the drawing up*(fishing up, i.e. securing after effort) of wisdom is beyond corals. **2\.** cstr. *a trail* (of seed), הַָ\+זּרַע ´מֶ [Psalm 126:6](https://ref.ly/Ps126:6) bearing the *trail* of seed ; cf. מָשַׁח **6\.**

> \[Str4902] †II. מֶ֫שֶׁךְ **n.pr.gent.** the Moschi (Gk.Μοσχοι, v. infr.; As. *Mušku, Muški*, DlPar 250 E. SchrCOT [Gn 10:2](https://ref.ly/Gen10:2); KGF 155 ff.; cf. Sab. משך Hal243);—‘son’ of Japheth [Gn 10:2](https://ref.ly/Gen10:2) (P), between תֻּבָל and תִּירָס, \= [1 Ch 1:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:5); also, מֶָשֶׁךְ, prob. err., v:[17](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:17) (v. מַשׁ); מֶָשֶׁךְ [Psalm 120:5](https://ref.ly/Ps120:5) (‖קֵדָר; here without תֻּבָל); ´תּוּבַל וּמ Ez [27:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:13) cf. [32:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek32:26), [38:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:2) (both \+ תֻּבָל); so also LXX [Is 66:19](https://ref.ly/Isa66:19) (Lo StaJavan 8 Du CheHpt; v. supr. מָשַׁח **2\.**); וְתֻבָל ´גּוֺג נְשִיא ראֹשׁ מ Ez [38:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:3), [39:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:1). LXX Μοσοχ (Μεσοχ), Sam. ך(י)מושׁ.—On identif. cf. Boch.; in Assyr. times they dwelt in W. (or NW.) Armenia (cf. Schrl. c.); in Pers. times appar. farther NE. (SE. of Euxine Sea), cf. Μοσχοι καὶ Τιβαρηνοι Herodiii. 94; vii. 78; also Di:[Gn 10:2](https://ref.ly/Gen10:2) and CheEncycl. Blb. Art. Geogr. (Biblical).

> \[Str4189] †\[משֶׁ֫כֶת] **n.f.** cord;—only pl. cstr. משְׁכוֺת כְּסִיל [Jb 38:31](https://ref.ly/Job38:31) *the cords of Orion*, i.e. prob. those by which (acc. to some legend) he is dragged along in the sky (cf. Di).

## I מָשַׁל (id: BDB05753)

\[Str4911] † I. \[מָשַׁל] **vb.** represent, be like (Ar. مَثَلَ *stand erect* (cf. FlDe [Pr 1:1](https://ref.ly/Prov1:1)), II. *effigiavit, representavit* (rem alicui), v. *imitate, use a verse as a poverb;* مَثَلٌ *description by way of comparison;* As. *mašâlu*, DlHWB 451 f.; Eth. መሰለ፡ *become like;* Aram. מְתַל *be like,* ܡܬܰܠ *compare*). **Niph.** *Pf.*3 ms. נִמְשַׁל [Psalm 49:13](https://ref.ly/Ps49:13), [49:21](https://ref.ly/Ps49:21); 2 ms. נִמְֹשָׁלְתָּ [Is 14:10](https://ref.ly/Isa14:10); 1 S. נִמְשַׁלְתִּי [Psalm 28:1](https://ref.ly/Ps28:1), [143:7](https://ref.ly/Ps143:7):—*be like, similar*, c. אֶל [Is 14:10](https://ref.ly/Isa14:10); עִם [Psalm 28:1](https://ref.ly/Ps28:1) \= [143:7](https://ref.ly/Ps143:7); כְּ [Psalm 49:13](https://ref.ly/Ps49:13), [49:21](https://ref.ly/Ps49:20). **Hiph.** *Impf.* 2 mpl. sf. וְתָמְשִׁלוּנִי *compare* [Is 46:5](https://ref.ly/Isa46:5). **Hithp.** *Impf.* 1 s. וָאֶתְמַשֵּׁל [Jb 30:19](https://ref.ly/Job30:19) (c. כְּ) *and I have become like dust.*.

> \[Str4912] † II. מָשָׁל **n.m.**:[Is 14:4](https://ref.ly/Isa14:4) proverb, parable (of sentences constructed in *parallelism*, usu. of Hebrew Wisdom, but occas. of other types);—abs. ´מ Ez [17:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:2) \+ 20 t.; cstr. מְשַׁל [1 S 24:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:14); sf. מְשָׁלוֺ [Nu 23:7](https://ref.ly/Num23:7) \+ 8 t.; pl. מְשָׁלִים [Ec 12:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:9) \+ 2 t.; cstr. מִשְׁלֵי [Pr 1:1](https://ref.ly/Prov1:1) \+ 3 t.;—**1\.** *proverbial saying,* brief terse sentence of popular sagacity [1 S 10:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:12), Ez [12:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:22), [12:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:23), [18:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:2), [18:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:3); הַקַדְמֹנִי ´מ [1 S 24:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:14) *proverb of the ancients.* **2\.** *by\-word* [Psalm 44:15](https://ref.ly/Ps44:15), [69:12](https://ref.ly/Ps69:12); לְמָשָׁל וְלִשְׁנִינָה [Dt 28:37](https://ref.ly/Deut28:37), [1 K 9:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:7), [2 Ch 7:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:20), [Je 24:9](https://ref.ly/Jer24:9); לְאוֺת וּלְמָשָׁל Ez [14:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:8) **3\.** prophetic figurative discourse: נָשָא מָשָׁל *lift up, utter a* ´מ [Nu 23:7](https://ref.ly/Num23:7), [23:18](https://ref.ly/Num23:18), [24:3](https://ref.ly/Num24:3), [24:15](https://ref.ly/Num24:15), [24:20](https://ref.ly/Num24:20), [24:21](https://ref.ly/Num24:21), [24:23](https://ref.ly/Num24:23) (all of Balaam); [Is 14:4](https://ref.ly/Isa14:4), [Mi 2:4](https://ref.ly/Mic2:4) (‖נהי) Hb [2:6](https://ref.ly/Hab2:6) (‖חידה), (cf. [Jb 27:1](https://ref.ly/Job27:1), [29:1](https://ref.ly/Job29:1) for same phrase under **6**). **4\.** *similitude, parable,* Ez [17:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:2), [21:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:5), [24:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:3).**5\.** *poem*, of various kinds: the ode ([Nu 21:27](https://ref.ly/Num21:27)–[30](https://ref.ly/Num21:30)), the 3,000 pieces traditionally ascribed to Solomon [1 K 5:12](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:12), didactic psalms [Psalm 49:5](https://ref.ly/Ps49:5), [78:2](https://ref.ly/Ps78:2). **6\.** *sentences of ethical wisdom* דִּבְרֵי חֲכָמִים collected in the מִשְׁלֵי שְׁלֽמֹה [Pr 10:1](https://ref.ly/Prov10:1), [25:1](https://ref.ly/Prov25:1) ([10:1](https://ref.ly/Prov10:1) – [22:16](https://ref.ly/Prov22:16) consisting of 376 couplets chiefly antithetical; 25–29 chiefly couplets of an emblematic type, but also occasional tristichs, tetrastichs, pentastichs, and decastichs. To these are appended דְּבָרִים of a more mixed character as to size and content [22:7](https://ref.ly/Prov22:7)–[24:22](https://ref.ly/Prov24:22); [24:23–34](https://ref.ly/Prov24:23-Prov24:34); [30:1–14](https://ref.ly/Prov30:1-Prov30:14); v:[15–33](https://ref.ly/Prov30:15-Prov30:33); [31:1–9](https://ref.ly/Prov31:1-Prov31:9); v:[10–31](https://ref.ly/Prov31:10-Prov31:31); the Praise of [Wisdom 1:8–9](https://ref.ly/Wis1:8-Wis1:9) is prefixed, and an introduction to the whole [1:1–7](https://ref.ly/Wis1:1-Wis1:7), in which the whole contents are represented as) משׁלים [Pr 1:1](https://ref.ly/Prov1:1), cf. v:[6](https://ref.ly/Prov1:6). The references in [Jb 13:12](https://ref.ly/Job13:12), [27:1](https://ref.ly/Job27:1), [29:1](https://ref.ly/Job29:1), [Pr 26:7](https://ref.ly/Prov26:7), [26:9](https://ref.ly/Prov26:9), [Ec 12:9](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:9) are to the same type of wisdom.

> \[Str4911] † II. מָשַׁל **vb. denom.** use of proverb, speak in parables or sentences of poetry, esp. Ezek.;—**Qal** *Impf.* יִמְשֹׁל Ez [16:44](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:44); 3 mpl. יִמְשְׁלוּ [12:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:23); *Imv.* מְשֹׁל [17:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:2), [24:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:3); *Inf. cstr.* מְשֹׁל [18:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:3) *Pt.* משֵׁל [16:44](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:44), pl. משְׁלִים [18:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:2), [Nu 21:27](https://ref.ly/Num21:27); —*use a proverb:* מָשָׁל´מ Ez [12:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:23), [18:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:3); a parablec. אֶ־ל [17:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:2) (‖חוּד חִידָה), [24:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:3); c. עַ־ל [18:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek18:2), and (מָשָׁל appar. om.) הִנֵּה כָּ־ל הַמּשֵׁל עָלַיִךְ יִמְשֹׁל [16:44](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:44) (but this is awkward; point הִנֵּה כֹּל הַמָּשָׁל); משְׁלִים [Nu 21:27](https://ref.ly/Num21:27) (JE)., **Pi.** *Pt.* הֲל֔אֹ מְמַשֵּׁל מְשָׁלִים הוּא Ez [21:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:5) *is he not a maker of parables?*

> \[Str4915] † I. \[מָ֫שֶׁל **n.\[m.]** likeness, one like; only sf. מָשְׁלוֺ [Jb 41:25](https://ref.ly/Job41:25) *his likeness,* i.e. one *like him.* p. 605

> \[Str4914] † מְשֹׁל **n.\[m.]** by\-word, only cstr. (strictly Inf.) לִמְשֹׁל עַמִּים [Jb 17:6](https://ref.ly/Job17:6) (they) *have made me a by\-word of the peoples.*

[Is 14:5](https://ref.ly/Isa14:5), [49:7](https://ref.ly/Isa49:7), [Je 51:46](https://ref.ly/Jer51:46), [51:46](https://ref.ly/Jer51:46), Ez [19:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:11), [Pr 6:7](https://ref.ly/Prov6:7), [23:1](https://ref.ly/Prov23:1), [29:12](https://ref.ly/Prov29:12), [29:26](https://ref.ly/Prov29:26), [Ec 10:4](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:4); משְׁלֵי הָעָם הַוֶּה [Is 28:14](https://ref.ly/Isa28:14). **2\.** of heavenly bodies, c. בְּ [Gn 1:18](https://ref.ly/Gen1:18). **3\.** of God, c. בְּ Ju [8:23](https://ref.ly/Judg8:23), [Is 63:19](https://ref.ly/Isa63:19), [Psalm 22:29](https://ref.ly/Ps22:29), [59:14](https://ref.ly/Ps59:14), [89:10](https://ref.ly/Ps89:10), [103:19](https://ref.ly/Ps103:19), [1 Ch 29:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:12), [2 Ch 20:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:6); abs. [Psalm 66:7](https://ref.ly/Ps66:7); לוֺ ´וּזְרֹעוֺ מ [Is 40:10](https://ref.ly/Isa40:10) *his arm ruling for him*. **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. sf. consec. וְהִמְשִׁילָם [Dn 11:39](https://ref.ly/Dan11:39); *Impf.* 2 ms. sf. תַּמְשִׁילֵהוּ [Psalm 8:7](https://ref.ly/Ps8:7); *Inf. abs.* הַמְשֵׁל [Jb 25:2](https://ref.ly/Job25:2); —*cause to rule,*, sq. acc. Pers. \+ בְּ [Psalm 8:7](https://ref.ly/Ps8:7) (subj. ´י), [Dn 11:39](https://ref.ly/Dan11:39); *exercise dominion,* only inf. abs. as n. abstr. *the exercise of dominion* [Jb 25:2](https://ref.ly/Job25:2) (attrib. of God).

## מָשַׁשׁ (id: BDB05787)

\[Str4959] † \[מָשַׁשׁ] **vb.** feel, grope (NH *id.* Pi.; Ar. مَسَّ *feel;* Eth. መርሰሰ፡ DIl66; Aram. ܡܰܫ, מְשַׁשׁ Pa., Palp.—v. also ימשׁ, II. מושׁ);—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 ms. sf. יְמֻשֵּׁנִי [Gn 27:12](https://ref.ly/Gen27:12) perhaps my father *will feel me;* וַיְמֻשֵּׁהוּ v:[22](https://ref.ly/Gen27:22) *and he felt of him* (both J). **Pi.** *Pf.* 2 ms. מִשַּׁשְׁתָּ; *Impf.* 3 ms. יְמַשֵּׁשׁ [Dt 28:29](https://ref.ly/Deut28:29), וַיְמַשֵּׁשׁ [Gn 31:34](https://ref.ly/Gen31:34); 3 mpl. יִמַשְׁשׁוּ [Jb 5:4](https://ref.ly/Job5:4), [12:25](https://ref.ly/Job12:25); *Pt.* מְמַשֵּׁשׁ [Dt 28:29](https://ref.ly/Deut28:29); —*feel over* or *through, grope:*—**1\.** לָבָּן אֶת־כָּל־הָאֹהֶל ´וַיְמ [Gn 31:24](https://ref.ly/Gen31:24) *and Laban felt through the whole tent* (i.e. searched it); so (obj. כֵּלַי) v:[37](https://ref.ly/Gen31:37) *thou hast felt through* all my possessions (or baggage; both E). **2\.** *grope,* of blind [Dt 28:29](https://ref.ly/Deut28:29) b (in simile), periphr. conj., וְהָיִיתָ מְמַשֵּׁשׁv:[29](https://ref.ly/Deut28:29) a *and thou shalt become one that gropes* at noonday (fig. of judicial blindness); cf. [Jb 5:14](https://ref.ly/Job5:14), [12:25](https://ref.ly/Job12:25). **Hiph.** *Impf.* וְיָמֵשׁ חשֶׁח [Ex 10:21](https://ref.ly/Exod10:21) *that one may feel* (the) *darkness;* rd. also *Imv.* sf. הֲמִשֵׁנִי (for והימשׁני, v. ימשׁ, p. 413 supr.) Ju [16:26](https://ref.ly/Judg16:26) *and let me touch* the pillars (2 acc.)

> \[Str4191,4960] מִשְׁתֶּה v. שׁתה. מֵת v. מות. p 559,1059

## מַת (id: BDB05789)

\[Str4962,4974] † \[מַת] **n.m.** male, man (מְת־וּ in n.pr.; Zinj. מת coll. *males, male offspring;* As. *mutu, husband;* Eth. ምት፡ *id.;* cf. Eg.*m\-t, phallus, male,* Steindorff in BaNB § 2b, δ);—only pl. מְתִים [Jb 11:3](https://ref.ly/Job11:3) \+ 3 t., מְתִם [Dt 2:34](https://ref.ly/Deut2:34), [3:6](https://ref.ly/Deut3:6) \+ Ju [20:48](https://ref.ly/Judg20:48) (probably), for MT מְתֹם, so GFM ; cstr. מְתֵי [Gn 34:30](https://ref.ly/Gen34:30) \+ 13 t.; sf. 2 fs. מְתַיִח; [Is 3:25](https://ref.ly/Isa3:25); 3 ms. מְתָיו [Dt 33:6](https://ref.ly/Deut33:6); —**1\.** *males, men,* [Is 3:25](https://ref.ly/Isa3:25) (‖גְּבוּרָתֵךְ); more distinctly, וְהַטָּף הַנָּשִׁים(וְ) מְתִם עִיר [Dt 2:34](https://ref.ly/Deut2:34), [3:6](https://ref.ly/Deut3:6) every *city of males* (male population, cf. Dr), *and the women,* etc.; Ju [20:48](https://ref.ly/Judg20:48) (v. supr.; opp. בְּהֵמָה). **2\.** usu. less emphasis on sex, in prose only phr.: **a.** מְתֵי מִסְפָּר *men of number* (numerable, i.e. few) [Gn 34:30](https://ref.ly/Gen34:30) (J; appos. of אֲנִי \= Jacob, representing his family), [Dt 4:27](https://ref.ly/Deut4:27), [Je 44:28](https://ref.ly/Jer44:28), also [Psalm 105:12](https://ref.ly/Ps105:12) \= [1 Ch 16:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:19); cf. וִיהִי מְתָיו מִסְפָּר [Dt 33:6](https://ref.ly/Deut33:6) *and (but) let his men be few.* **b.** בִּמְתֵי מְעָט \= *consisting of a few men* [Dt 26:5](https://ref.ly/Deut26:5), [28:62](https://ref.ly/Deut28:62) **3\.** elsewhere only poet., sex usu. not emph.: **a.** [Jb 11:3](https://ref.ly/Job11:3), [24:12](https://ref.ly/Job24:12), [Psalm 17:14](https://ref.ly/Ps17:14), [17:14](https://ref.ly/Ps17:14) (but text prob. corrupt, of. Bae We; at least om. ממתים ידך Ol BI Che, cf. Hup\-Now); מְתֵי ישראל [Is 41:14](https://ref.ly/Isa41:14). **b.** poet. phr.: מְתֵי שָׁוְא *men of false\-hood* [Jb 11:11](https://ref.ly/Job11:11), [Psalm 26:4](https://ref.ly/Ps26:4); אָוֶן ´מ [22:15](https://ref.ly/Ps22:15) *men of wickedness;* סוֺדִי ´מ [19:19](https://ref.ly/Job19:19) *men of my circle;* אָהֳלִי ´מ [Jb 31:31](https://ref.ly/Job31:31) *men of my tent* (household); רָעָב ´מ [Is 5:13](https://ref.ly/Isa5:13) *men of hunger,* but read probably ´מְזֵי ר *sucked out, empty* from hunger (as [Dt 32:24](https://ref.ly/Deut32:24)) Ew De Che Di Du (cf. מָזֶה).

(under fig. of beast), וּמִתְגִּי בִּשְפָתֶיְ [2 K 19:28](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:28) and I will put (וְשַמְתִּי) my hook in thy nose *and my bridle in thy lips* \= [Is 37:29](https://ref.ly/Isa37:29) (v. ## מתן (id: BDB05802)

מתן (√ of foll.; Ar. مَتَنَ *be stout, firm, enduring* (Frey Wahrm), II. *make stout, firm* (Lane)).

> \[Str4975] † מָתְנַ֫יִם **n. m. du** loins (Ar. مَتْنٌ *back*, مَتْنَتَانِ *the two sides of the back*, etc.; Syr. ܡܰܬ̈ܢܳܬܳܐ;—abs. ´מ [Dt 33:11](https://ref.ly/Deut33:11) \+ 7 t., מָתְָ\+ניִם Ez [29:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:7), [47:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:4); cstr. מָתְנֵי [Je 13:11](https://ref.ly/Jer13:11) \+ 3t.; sf. מָתְנַי [Is 21:3](https://ref.ly/Isa21:3), מָתְנָי [Je 13:2](https://ref.ly/Jer13:2); מָתְנֵיהֶם 1 K [20:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:32) \+ 4 t., etc.;—*loins:* **1\. a.** place of wearing girdle [1 K 2:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:5), [2 K 1:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs1:8), [Je 13:1](https://ref.ly/Jer13:1), [13:2](https://ref.ly/Jer13:2), [13:4](https://ref.ly/Jer13:4), [13:11](https://ref.ly/Jer13:11), Ez [23:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:15); fig. [Is 11:5](https://ref.ly/Isa11:5) *righteousness shall be the girdle of his loins*. **b.** hence place of things attached to girdle:—sword girded on (חגר על, אסר [2 S 20:8](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:8), [Ne 4:12](https://ref.ly/Neh4:12); opp., וּמָתְנֵי מְלָכִים אֲפַתֵּחַ [Is 45:1](https://ref.ly/Isa45:1) *and the loins of kings I will loosen*, i e. will disarm them (´י before Cyrus; cf. Che); ink\-horn Ez [9:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek9:2), [9:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek9:3), [9:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek9:11). **c.** girding up *loins* \= make ready for action, וַיְשַׁנֵּס מָתְנָיו [1 K 18:46](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:46); more oft. ´חָגַר מ [2 K 4:29](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:29), [9:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:1), [Ex 12:11](https://ref.ly/Exod12:11) (P), [Dn 10:5](https://ref.ly/Dan10:5); ´אָזַר מ [Je 1:17](https://ref.ly/Jer1:17). **d.** ´זַרְזִיר מ [Pr 30:31](https://ref.ly/Prov30:31) that which is *girt in the loins*, i.e. prob. either a *greyhound* Ew Bö De (contracted, as if by a belt), or a war\-horse, charger, Bo Ges Hi Str Wild (with a saddle); cf. also זרזיר, p. 267 supr.; text perh. corrupt (Wild). **e.** girded with sackcloth (in mourning) [Gn 37:34](https://ref.ly/Gen37:34) (J), [Am 8:10](https://ref.ly/Amos8:10), [Is 20:2](https://ref.ly/Isa20:2), [1 K 20:31](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:31), [20:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:32), [Je 48:37](https://ref.ly/Jer48:37). **f.** linen breeches of priests were to extend מִמָּתְנַיִם וְעַד יְרֵכַיִם [Ex 28:42](https://ref.ly/Exod28:42) (P); to be ´עַל־מָת Ez [44:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:18); loins of slaves are girt with waistcloth, cf. [Jb 12:18](https://ref.ly/Job12:18) (v. also אֵזוֺר). **g.** in gen. of the middle of the body, ´מֵי מָת Ez [47:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:4) (i.e. water reaching to the loins); so of the appearance of ´י in Ezek.'s visions Ez [1:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:27), [1:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:27), [8:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:2), [8:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:2). **2\. a.** *loins* as seat of strength, [Dt 33:11](https://ref.ly/Deut33:11), [1 K 12:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:10) \= [2 Ch 10:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr10:10); חַזֵּק מָתְנַיִם [Na 2:2](https://ref.ly/Nah2:2) (‖אַמֵּץ כֹּחַ); כֹּחוֺ בְּמָתְנָיו [Jb 40:16](https://ref.ly/Job40:16) (of hippopotamus); in combin. with **1 a** (fig.) חָֽגְרָה ´בְּעוֺז מ [Pr 31:17](https://ref.ly/Prov31:17) *she girdeth her loins with strength*, she puts on energy with her girdle; cf. also ´בְּשִׁבְרוֺן מ Ez [21:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:11) sigh *with breaking of loins*, i.e. in entire collapse of strength; so תָּמִיד ´הַמְעַָד וּמ [Psalm 69:24](https://ref.ly/Ps69:24) *and make their loins continually to shake*, make them totter; cf. Ez [29:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:7) (where rd. וְהִמְעַדְתָּ for וְהַעֲמַדְתָּ, v. מָעַד). **b.** as seat of keenest pain, due to grief or dread [Na 2:11](https://ref.ly/Nah2:11), [Is 21:3](https://ref.ly/Isa21:3) (as in travail); so prob. also [Psalm 66:11](https://ref.ly/Ps66:11) (cf. Bae; and v. מוּעָקָה sub עוק).

## מָתֹק (id: BDB05806)

\[Str4985,4988] † מָתֹק **vb.** become or be sweet, pleasant (NH מתק, esp. Pi.; As. \[*matâḳu*] *be sweet*, in deriv.; Eth. ምጡቅ፡ *sweet;* Aram. ܡܬܰܩ *suck* (with pleasure), מְתַק *id.*, *be sweet;* Ar. مطق (ط before ق) v. *smack* the lips (with pleasure), مَطْقَةٌ *sweetness*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. sf. מְתָקוֺ [Jb 24:20](https://ref.ly/Job24:20); 3 pl. מָֽתְקוּ [21:33](https://ref.ly/Job21:33); *Impf.* 3 mpl. יִמְתָּקוּ [Pr 9:17](https://ref.ly/Prov9:17), וַיִמְתְּקוּ [Ex 15:25](https://ref.ly/Exod15:25); —**1\.** lit. *become sweet*, of water (opp. מָרִים) [15:25](https://ref.ly/Exod15:25) (J);  *are* (i.e. taste) *sweet*, ´מַיִם גְּנוּבִים יִמ [Pr 9:17](https://ref.ly/Prov9:17) (fig. of delights of illicit pleasure). **2\.** \= *be pleasant*, מָֽתְקוּ לוֺ [Jb 21:33](https://ref.ly/Job21:33) *sweet* (pleasant) *to him* are the clods of the valley (said of one resting in the grave). **3\.** *suck* (Aram. sense, cf. Syr. supr.) מְתָקוֺ רִמָּה [Jb 24:20](https://ref.ly/Job24:20) the worm *doth suck him*, feast on him (on vb. masc. cf. Ges§ 145\. 7 KöSynt. § 345 a DaSynt. § 113 (b)), Di De Buhl (cf. Kau Da; also SS who render ‘ angenehm finden ’), but this sense in Heb. dub.; txt. perh. crpt. (cf. conject. by Bu Du). **Hiph.** *Impf.* 3 fs. אִם תַּמְתִּיק בְּפִיו רָעָה [Jb 20:12](https://ref.ly/Job20:12) *if evil gives a sweet taste in his mouth* (declarative Hiph.); 1 pl. אֲשֶׁר יַחְדָּו נַמְתִּיק סוֺד [Psalm 55:15](https://ref.ly/Ps55:15) *we who used to make sweet* (our) *intimacy*.

> \[Str4986] † מֶ֫תֶק **n. m.**:[Pr 16:21](https://ref.ly/Prov16:21) sweetness; cstr. שְפָתַיִם ´מ [Pr 16:21](https://ref.ly/Prov16:21) i.e. agreeable, attractive speech, so prob. רֵעֵהוּ ´מ [27:9](https://ref.ly/Prov27:9) *the sweetness of one's friend*, i.e. of his speech.

> \[Str4987] † \[מֹ֫תֶק] **n.**\[**m.**] sweetness;—only sf. מָתְקִי Ju [9:11](https://ref.ly/Judg9:11) *my sweetness* (fig\-tree loquitur), i.e. sweetness of the fruit.

> \[Str4966] † מָתוֺק **adj.** sweet, and **n.**\[**m.**] sweetness (on form v. Lag: BN 30, 60, cf. BaNB 13, 36\);—abs. ´מ Ju [14:14](https://ref.ly/Judg14:14) \+ 9 t.; f. מְתוּקָה [Ec 5:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:11); pl. מְתוּקִים [Psalm 19:11](https://ref.ly/Ps19:11); —**1\.** *sweet*, of honey Ju [14:14](https://ref.ly/Judg14:14) (where ´מ \= subst. *a sweet thing*), v:[18](https://ref.ly/Judg14:18) (both Samson's riddle), [Pr 24:13](https://ref.ly/Prov24:13); so \= *sweetness*, Ez [3:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:3); more gen., ´כָּל־מַר מ [Pr 27:7](https://ref.ly/Prov27:7) *every bitter thing is sweet* to one hungry; fig. [Psalm 19:11](https://ref.ly/Ps19:11) (God's commandments, sweeter than honey), cf. [Pr 16:24](https://ref.ly/Prov16:24) (sim. of pleasant words) לְחִכִּי ´וּפִרְיוֺ מ Ct [2:3](https://ref.ly/Song2:3) (i.e his caresses); לְמָר ´וּמ ´שָמִים מַר לְמ [Is 5:20](https://ref.ly/Isa5:20) *making bitter into sweet and sweet into bitter* (obscuring moral distinctions; ‖הָאֹמְרִים לָרַע טוֺב וְלַטּוֺב רָע). **2\.** \= *pleasant*, sleep of the toiler [Ec 5:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:11); light [11:7](https://ref.ly/Eccl11:7) (‖טוֺב לַעֵינַיִם לִרְאוֺת אֶת־הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ).

> \[Str4990] † מִתְרְדָת **n. pr. m.** name of two Persians; **1\.** Cyrus ’ time [Ezra 1:8](https://ref.ly/Ezra1:8). **2\.** Artaxerxes ’ time [Ezra 4:7](https://ref.ly/Ezra4:7).—LXX Μιθρα\[ι]δατης in both.

> \[Str4991–93] מַתַּת, מַתַּתָּה, (וּ)מַתִּתְיָה v. נתן–83\.
